# PUMP.FUN FUNDED-DEV BOT — CODE REVIEW PACK (read-only) — exported 2026-11-06 06:54 UTC, repo bsc-volume-alert/Top-coins-Bot branch tracker, commit 2a7a202 2026-11-06 05:08:14 +0000
# Deployed: launcher 0.9.12, tracker 0.19.2, scope 0.12.8. No credentials in this pack (.env files excluded; config shows env variable NAMES and defaults only).

## How a launch flows through the system
1. TRACKER (src/, separate pm2 process): subscribes to pump.fun program logs (logsSubscribe) — every launch and every trade. Writes `tokens` (one row per launch: creator, uri, bundle at finalize, socials) and, since 0.19, every trade (wallet, slot, sig, SOL, tokens, price, reserves, recv_ms) for launches that are bundle tier-1 OR reach VOL_TRACK_SOL (15 SOL of buys), for TRACK_MIN=125 min. `feed_gaps` logs socket status changes.
2. LAUNCHER fdScan (launcher/src/fundeddev.mjs): every LAUNCH_FD_SCAN_S (240 s) reads `tokens` created in the last 240 s not yet in `fd_watch`; for each: traces the creator's funding (operators.mjs creatorFunder → Helius RPC getSignaturesForAddress + getTransaction of the wallet's FIRST tx: the largest SOL sender in that tx = "funder"), counts the funder's other launches (creator_trace), applies the pre-filter PRE (private funder, ≤ MAX_FUNDER_LAUNCHES, funded ≥ 2 SOL within 2 h, bundle 10–60%, factory checks) → fd_watch.pre; pre_nb = a 1-in-NB_SAMPLE sample of launches that fail only the funding part (control). Launches failing both are recorded in fd_watch with `why` and get NO checks.
3. LAUNCHER fdCheck: for fd_watch rows with pre=1 or pre_nb=1, at 60 / 90 / 120 s after creation: study.mjs earlyTrades (Helius: all transactions of the mint up to that second) → metrics() (net SOL, buyers, sellers, big buys, mcSol from the curve, holders from GMGN token/info, top-N shares, launch-group selling); then every RULE in RULES (the books) is evaluated → vol_paper row per book (paper entry at the check price) → chainfill.chainEntry (simulated executable fill from the curve/pool reserves 1 s later; U books: guard on the pre-submission state) → live.mjs liveBuy for the live book (OFF). Decisions per rule per check → fd_decisions. Farm scores / funder history / wallet state / meta reuse recorded (farm.mjs).
4. CHAINFILL exit: onAccount subscription to the curve (or PumpSwap pool) reserves; on every change the position is valued (sell quote of its tokens); trail 40% off the high (curve) / 25% (graduated); trigger → fill 0.7 s later on the then state; 2-h cap; s_r = 0.2-stake result, s_r02 = the real 0.02 position's result (own tokens), s_x60 = value at +60 s, s_half_x, milestones.
5. SCOPE (website, not included): reads the launcher DB read-only and renders /fd2.

## Known faults and open questions (from 5–6 Nov)
- The funding test gates OBSERVATION: launches with "no funder" / tiny funding / high-launch-count funder get no checks (except the 1-in-7 pre_nb sample). Nine of the owner's eleven overnight runners were in that class. Proposed: observe every launch reaching 15 SOL net buying; funding as a label.
- Tracing every launch's creator costs ~10 credits each; 11,000 launches overnight ≈ 108k of the 120k daily FD budget. Proposed: trace only after 15 SOL.
- 'bracket' (EFWotn2U…): A1 entered at 04:26 with s_in_gap 26.8 (the fill price 27.8× the recorded check price); the 60-s check had mcUsd null. The paper check price for that row needs auditing (metrics mcSol → chain_px).
- GMGN holder_count vs chain buyers: 2,310 holders on 137 buyers (GTA6) — holders is not retention; dusting unproven without transfer data.
- Pool market cap on 79%-bundle tool launches reads ~100× (YouTube: $1.6M at 60 s) — decoder fault noted 4 Nov; sim fills use reserves, not that number.
- Launcher rss grows ~55 MB/h (statement caching added 0.9.12 helped partly); pm2 restart at 600 MB.
- Website (scope) process hung at ~05:00 UK with /api/fd never answering; recovered by reload; cause unknown (all handlers synchronous).
- Live book OFF since 19:06 UK 5 Nov; live caps in live.mjs (2 open, −0.15 SOL / 20 fills, −0.2 total).

## Files (in order): launcher/src/fundeddev.mjs, chainfill.mjs, farm.mjs, operators.mjs, study.mjs, live.mjs, volpaper.mjs, quote.mjs, rpc.mjs, index.mjs; tracker src/launch-monitor.js, src/db.js (schema), src/config.js

// What the 214 census winners ($100K+, migrated) and 10,128 traced launches said (LAUNCHER.md 4 Nov 09:50 / 10:45 UK):
//   a winner is launched by a first-time creator wallet funded with a few SOL shortly before launch by a wallet that rarely launches (exchange or
//   private), the bundle commits 10–60% (not the self-bought 60%+ factory), and in its FIRST MINUTE it has big buyers (net ≥ 27 SOL, 2-SOL+ buys)
//   rather than a bot swarm; it graduates in ~3 min, peaks at ~minute 16 and is worthless by minute 30.

//   2. at 60 / 90 / 120 s: the coin's trades from the chain (earlyTrades); the confirmation rules below; first pass → paper entry at GMGN's price,
//      into vol_paper (scored 2 h later by volScore with every exit side by side: half at 2x + trail, 2x/−50, 1.5x/−30, 40% trail, hold 60/120,
//      and 0.8.0's hard outs at 10 / 15 / 20 min with a −35% stop).
//   Rules: FD  = pre-filter + net ≥ 27 SOL + mc ≥ 100 SOL + ≥ 3 buys of 2+ SOL;  FDL = pre-filter + net ≥ 15 + mc ≥ 60;  FDB = FD's confirmation
//   with NO bundle condition (what the bundle filter is worth);  A1 = confirmation only, no pre-filter at all (book A of October, the control).

const uncrowded = (m) => m.holders != null && m.holders < 200 && m.mcUsd != null && m.mcUsd < 15000;
const GATE_SOL = n("LAUNCH_U_GATE_SOL", 2);
/** the 90-s confirmation: the coin passed the bar and U at its 60-s check, and net buying has grown by more than GATE_SOL since */
const gate90 = (m, prev, bar) => { const c60 = (prev || []).find((c) => c.at === 60); return !!c60 && bar(c60) && uncrowded(c60) && (m.net ?? 0) - (c60.net ?? 0) > GATE_SOL; };
const U_GUARD = n("LAUNCH_U_GUARD", 0.3);
const BASE_BAR = (m) => m.net >= 15 && (m.mcSol ?? 0) >= 60; // the loosest bar any rule accepts (FDL's): GMGN's token info is fetched once per check only past it
// 0.8.3 (13:05 UK): GMGN's price for a 60–120 s old coin can lag badly ("I voted": GMGN $2K while the chain said 115 SOL ≈ $10.6K), which would
// flatter every paper entry. The entry price is the CHAIN price at the check (market cap in SOL × SOL/USD ÷ supply); GMGN's is kept in the row for comparison.
let solUsd = null, solAt = 0;
async function solPrice() { if (Date.now() - solAt < 120000 && solUsd) return solUsd; try { const w = await gget("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol", address: "So11111111111111111111111111111111111111112" }); const v = N(w?.price?.price ?? w?.price); if (v > 0) { solUsd = v; solAt = Date.now(); } } catch {} return solUsd; }

export function initFD(db) {
  // 0.8.6: entries booked after graduation with the decoder's pool market cap (100× too high) → back to GMGN's price, re-scored
  try { for (const t of db.prepare("SELECT id, row, entry_price FROM vol_paper WHERE strat IN ('FD','FDL','FDB','A1') AND row LIKE '%\"chain_px\"%' AND (row LIKE '%graduated%' OR row LIKE '%\"venue\":\"other\"%')").all()) { let r = {}; try { r = JSON.parse(t.row || "{}"); } catch {} const g = r.gmgn_px; if (!(g > 0) || r.fixed_grad) continue;
      if (t.entry_price > g * 3) { r.fixed_grad = true; r.bad_px = t.entry_price; db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET entry_price=?, entry_mc=?, row=?, done=0, err=NULL WHERE id=?").run(g, g * 1e9, JSON.stringify(r), t.id); } } } catch {}
  // 0.8.4: the FD-family entries booked before 0.8.3 (4 Nov 10:45–13:05 UK) carry GMGN's lagging price; re-price them from the chain market cap stored in the row (SOL/USD ≈ the first chain-priced entry's rate, else 92)
  try { const ref = db.prepare("SELECT row FROM vol_paper WHERE strat IN ('FD','FDL','FDB','A1') AND row LIKE '%\"sol_usd\"%' ORDER BY entry_ts LIMIT 1").get(); let su = 92; try { su = JSON.parse(ref?.row || "{}").sol_usd || su; } catch {}
    for (const t of db.prepare("SELECT id, row, entry_price, entry_mc FROM vol_paper WHERE strat IN ('FD','FDL','FDB','A1') AND done=0 AND row NOT LIKE '%\"chain_px\"%'").all()) { let r = {}; try { r = JSON.parse(t.row || "{}"); } catch {} if (!(r.mcSol > 0)) continue;
      const px = r.mcSol * su / 1e9; r.gmgn_px = t.entry_price; r.chain_px = px; r.sol_usd = su; r.repriced = true; db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET entry_price=?, entry_mc=?, row=? WHERE id=?").run(px, px * 1e9, JSON.stringify(r), t.id); } } catch {}
  db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS fd_watch (mint TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT, symbol TEXT, creator TEXT, created_ts INTEGER, bundle_pct REAL, bundle_sol REAL, funder TEXT, funder_sol REAL, funded_ts INTEGER, funder_launches INTEGER,
    pre INTEGER, pre_nb INTEGER, why TEXT, checks TEXT DEFAULT '[]', entered TEXT DEFAULT '', done INTEGER DEFAULT 0, scanned_ts INTEGER);
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS fdw_done ON fd_watch(done, created_ts);`);
  for (const c of ["x_out10 REAL", "x_out15 REAL", "x_out20 REAL", "r_out10 REAL", "r_out15 REAL", "r_out20 REAL", "x_trail25 REAL", "x_trail60 REAL", "x_tw REAL", "r_trail25 REAL", "r_trail60 REAL", "r_tw REAL", "m_trail40 REAL"]) try { db.exec(`ALTER TABLE vol_paper ADD COLUMN ${c}`); } catch {} // 0.8.0 hard outs; 0.8.7 trails 25/60 and tight-then-wide
  try { db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET done=0 WHERE strat IN ('FDX','FD','FD35','FD120','FDL','FDB','A1') AND done=1 AND err IS NULL AND r_trail60 IS NULL").run(); } catch {} // 0.8.7: re-score today's trades for the new exits (one-off; ~2 GMGN calls a trade)
  // 0.8.9: when each rule was loaded (owner: "add the time of their shipment"). Rules that already have entries get their first entry's time; new ones get now.
  db.exec("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS fd_rules (k TEXT PRIMARY KEY, since_ts INTEGER, ver TEXT)");
  let ver = null; try { ver = JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL("../package.json", import.meta.url), "utf8")).version; } catch {}
  const hasVP = !!db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='vol_paper'").get(); // the smoke boot runs on an empty database (vol_paper is created later, by initVol)
  for (const r of RULES) { try { if (hasVP) db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fd_rules (k, since_ts, ver) VALUES (?, COALESCE((SELECT MIN(entry_ts) FROM vol_paper WHERE strat=?), ?), ?)").run(r.k, r.k, now(), ver); else db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fd_rules (k, since_ts, ver) VALUES (?,?,?)").run(r.k, now(), ver); } catch (e) { fdStat.lastErr = "fd_rules: " + e.message.slice(0, 120); } }
  try { db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fd_rules (k, since_ts, ver) VALUES ('VENUE', ?, ?)").run(now(), ver); } catch {}
  // 0.8.22: the curve flag from our own check, and the venue exit re-scored where it was wrong (curve coins had been scored with the 25% trail)
  try { const fixed = db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET curve=1 WHERE curve=0 AND json_valid(row) AND json_extract(row,'$.venue')='pump.fun curve'").run().changes;
    const resc = db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET x_venue=x_trail40, r_venue=r_trail40, m_venue=m_trail40 WHERE curve=1 AND done=1 AND err IS NULL AND x_trail40 IS NOT NULL AND (x_venue IS NULL OR x_venue<>x_trail40)").run().changes;
    if (fixed || resc) fdStat.lastErr = `0.8.22 curve flag fixed on ${fixed} rows, venue re-scored on ${resc}`;
    db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fd_rules (k, since_ts, ver) VALUES ('FDH_REV', ?, ?)").run(now(), ver); } catch (e) { fdStat.lastErr = "0.8.22 fix: " + e.message.slice(0, 120); } // 0.8.16: when the venue exit (25% trail graduated / 40% curve) was loaded — the page judges it on the trades after this
  // 0.8.11: recording columns (see the header) and the first-buyer tables
  for (const c of ["dev_buy_sol REAL", "name_1h INTEGER", "name_24h INTEGER", "socials TEXT", "image INTEGER", "replies INTEGER", "koth_ts INTEGER", "meta_ts INTEGER", "meta_err TEXT"]) try { db.exec(`ALTER TABLE fd_watch ADD COLUMN ${c}`); } catch {}
  for (const c of ["q_ts INTEGER", "q_px REAL", "q_gap REAL", "q_tokens REAL", "q_impact REAL", "q_ms INTEGER", "q_err TEXT", "q_exit_ts INTEGER", "q_exit_sol REAL", "q_exit_x REAL", "q_exit_gap REAL", "q_exit_delay INTEGER", "q_exit_impact REAL", "q_exit_err TEXT"]) try { db.exec(`ALTER TABLE vol_paper ADD COLUMN ${c}`); } catch {} // 0.8.15 quote shadowing
  db.exec("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS fd_hgrowth (mint TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT, h60 INTEGER, h120 INTEGER, ratio REAL, mc60 REAL, mc120 REAL, ts INTEGER)"); // 0.9.6
  db.exec("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS fd_quotes (mint TEXT, ts INTEGER, at INTEGER, entered TEXT, chain_px REAL, q_px REAL, q_gap REAL, q_impact REAL, q_ms INTEGER, venue TEXT, mcSol REAL, net REAL, err TEXT)");
  db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS fd_early (mint TEXT, wallet TEXT, slot0 INTEGER, sec REAL, sol REAL, tok REAL, exited INTEGER DEFAULT 0, created_ts INTEGER, PRIMARY KEY (mint, wallet));
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS fde_wallet ON fd_early(wallet); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS fde_ts ON fd_early(created_ts);
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS fd_sniper_stats (wallet TEXT PRIMARY KEY, launches INTEGER, slot0 INTEGER, graduated INTEGER, peak50k INTEGER, peak100k INTEGER, sol_avg REAL, exit_pct REAL, first_ts INTEGER, last_ts INTEGER, updated INTEGER)`);
}

/** every 15 s: every tracker launch of the last SCAN_S seconds not yet seen → funder lookup → pre-filter. */
let scanning = false;
export async function fdScan(db, tdb) {
  if (scanning || !tdb) return; scanning = true;
  try {
    if (fdStat.creditsDay !== today()) { fdStat.creditsDay = today(); fdStat.credits = 0; }
    const rows = tdb.prepare("SELECT mint, name, symbol, creator, create_ts, bundle_pct, bundle_sol, dev_buy_sol, peak_mc_usd, graduated FROM tokens WHERE create_ts>=? AND creator IS NOT NULL AND creator<>'' ORDER BY create_ts DESC LIMIT 400").all(now() - SCAN_S);
    const ins = db.prepare(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fd_watch (mint, name, symbol, creator, created_ts, bundle_pct, bundle_sol, funder, funder_sol, funded_ts, funder_launches, pre, pre_nb, why, scanned_ts, dev_buy_sol, name_1h, name_24h) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`);
    const sameName = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) c FROM fd_watch WHERE name=? COLLATE NOCASE AND mint<>? AND created_ts>=? AND created_ts<?"); // 0.8.11: the trending-name count (this launch's name in the hour / day before it)
    const insCT = db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO creator_trace VALUES (@mint,@creator,@create_ts,@peak_mc_usd,@graduated,@bundle_pct,@bundle_sol,@funder,@funder_sol,@funded_ts,@creator_txs,@err,@checked_ts)");
    for (const t of rows) {
      if (db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM fd_watch WHERE mint=?").get(t.mint)) continue;
      if (fdStat.credits >= DAY_CREDITS) { fdStat.lastErr = `daily Helius budget ${DAY_CREDITS} reached`; break; }
      fdStat.scanned++;
      let r = db.prepare("SELECT funder, funder_sol, funded_ts, creator_txs FROM creator_trace WHERE mint=?").get(t.mint) || db.prepare("SELECT funder, funder_sol, funded_ts, creator_txs FROM creator_trace WHERE creator=? AND funder IS NOT NULL LIMIT 1").get(t.creator);
      const known = !!r;
      if (!r) { const c0 = studyStat.credits; try { r = await creatorFunder(t.creator); fdStat.traced++; ctStat.traced++; } catch (e) { r = { err: e.message.slice(0, 100) }; fdStat.errors++; fdStat.lastErr = `${t.creator.slice(0, 6)} ${r.err}`; }
        fdStat.credits += studyStat.credits - c0; }
      { // 0.8.5: ALWAYS record this launch in creator_trace — a factory re-using one creator wallet (3GzDw7…, 2.133 SOL, 5 launches in 50 min) was counted as "0 launches" because the known-creator path skipped the insert
        insCT.run({ mint: t.mint, creator: t.creator, create_ts: t.create_ts, peak_mc_usd: t.peak_mc_usd ?? null, graduated: t.graduated ? 1 : 0, bundle_pct: t.bundle_pct ?? null, bundle_sol: t.bundle_sol ?? null, funder: r.funder || null, funder_sol: r.funder_sol ?? null, funded_ts: r.funded_ts ?? null, creator_txs: r.txs ?? null, err: r.err || null, checked_ts: now() }); }
      const fl = r.funder ? db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT mint) c FROM creator_trace WHERE funder=? AND mint<>?").get(r.funder, t.mint).c : null;
      const cl = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT mint) c FROM creator_trace WHERE creator=? AND mint<>?").get(t.creator, t.mint).c; // 0.8.5: the creator wallet's own other launches
      const amt = N(r.funder_sol) || 0, delay = r.funded_ts ? t.create_ts - r.funded_ts : null, bp = N(t.bundle_pct);
      const why = [];
      if (!r.funder) why.push("no funder"); else { if (amt < FUND_SOL) why.push(`funded ${amt.toFixed(2)} SOL`); if (delay == null || delay > FUND_S || delay < 0) why.push(`funded ${delay == null ? "?" : Math.round(delay / 60)} min before`); if (fl > FUNDER_MAX) why.push(`funder has ${fl} launches`); if (cl >= 1) why.push(`creator wallet has ${cl} other launch${cl > 1 ? "es" : ""}`); }
      let preNb = r.funder && amt >= FUND_SOL && delay != null && delay >= 0 && delay <= FUND_S && fl <= FUNDER_MAX && cl < 1 ? 1 : 0; // 0.8.5: first coin of this creator wallet (winners: median 1 prior launch on GMGN = none)
      if (preNb) { // 0.8.2: the two factory tests, only on the ~1% that get this far
        const same = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT mint) c FROM creator_trace WHERE mint<>? AND create_ts>=? AND ABS(funder_sol-?)<0.00005").get(t.mint, t.create_ts - AMOUNT_DAYS * 86400, amt).c; // 0.8.5: any other launch, same creator or not
        if (same >= 1) { preNb = 0; why.push(`same amount on ${same} other launch${same > 1 ? "es" : ""}`); }
        else {
          let g = db.prepare("SELECT funder FROM wallet_trace WHERE wallet=?").get(r.funder)?.funder;
          if (g === undefined) { const c0 = studyStat.credits; try { const gf = await creatorFunder(r.funder); g = gf.funder || null; db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO wallet_trace VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(r.funder, g, null, null, gf.funder_sol ?? null, gf.funded_ts ?? null, null, 1, gf.err || null, now()); } catch (e) { g = null; } fdStat.credits += studyStat.credits - c0; }
          const gPublic = g && db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM wallet_trace WHERE funder=? AND funder_type IS NOT NULL LIMIT 1").get(g); // an exchange / bridge behind the funder is normal (that is how people fund a dev wallet), not a factory
          if (g && !gPublic) { const gl = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT c.mint) c FROM creator_trace c JOIN wallet_trace w ON w.wallet=c.funder WHERE w.funder=? AND c.mint<>?").get(g, t.mint).c; if (gl > GRAND_MAX) { preNb = 0; why.push(`grand-funder has ${gl} launches`); } }
        }
      }
      const bundleOk = bp != null && bp >= B_LO && bp < B_HI; if (!bundleOk) why.push(bp == null ? "bundle unknown" : `bundle ${bp.toFixed(0)}%`);
      const pre = preNb && bundleOk ? 1 : 0; if (pre) fdStat.pre++;
      const nm = String(t.name || "").slice(0, 60).trim(); let n1 = null, n24 = null; if (nm) try { n1 = sameName.get(nm, t.mint, t.create_ts - 3600, t.create_ts).c; n24 = sameName.get(nm, t.mint, t.create_ts - 86400, t.create_ts).c; } catch {}
      ins.run(t.mint, nm, String(t.symbol || "").slice(0, 20), t.creator, t.create_ts, bp, N(t.bundle_sol), r.funder || null, amt || null, r.funded_ts ?? null, fl, pre, preNb, why.join("; "), now(), N(t.dev_buy_sol), n1, n24);
    }
    fdStat.lastScan = new Date().toISOString();
  } catch (e) { fdStat.errors++; fdStat.lastErr = "scan: " + e.message.slice(0, 160); }
  finally { scanning = false; }
}

export function metrics(r, upto, creator = null, bundle = null) {
  const xs = r.trades.filter((x) => x.t <= upto), buys = xs.filter((x) => x.isBuy), sells = xs.filter((x) => !x.isBuy);
  const bs = buys.reduce((s, x) => s + x.sol, 0) / 1e9, ss = sells.reduce((s, x) => s + x.sol, 0) / 1e9, last = xs[xs.length - 1];
  // 0.8.9: who is still holding — per wallet, tokens bought vs sold; "exited" = sold 95%+ of what it bought (sniper in-and-out). Chain-side twin of GMGN's holder count.
  const per = new Map(); for (const x of xs) { const p = per.get(x.user) || { b: 0, s: 0 }; if (x.isBuy) p.b += x.tok || 0; else p.s += x.tok || 0; per.set(x.user, p); }
  const exited = [...per.values()].filter((p) => p.b > 0 && p.s >= 0.95 * p.b).length, buyers = new Set(buys.map((x) => x.user)).size;
  const m = { net: +(bs - ss).toFixed(2), buySol: +bs.toFixed(2), sellSol: +ss.toFixed(2), buyers, buys: buys.length, sells: sells.length,
    big: buys.filter((x) => x.sol >= 2e9).length, mcSol: last?.mc ? +last.mc.toFixed(1) : null, exited, holding: buyers - exited, sellers: new Set(sells.map((x) => x.user)).size };
  // 0.8.11: the columns the failure study lacked. Supply is 1e9 tokens × 1e6 raw units; the share held is net tokens bought in the window ÷ supply.
  const held = [...per.values()].map((p) => Math.max(0, p.b - p.s)).sort((a, b) => b - a), SUPPLY = 1e15;
  m.top1_pct = +(100 * (held[0] || 0) / SUPPLY).toFixed(2); m.top5_pct = +(100 * held.slice(0, 5).reduce((a, c) => a + c, 0) / SUPPLY).toFixed(2); m.top10_pct = +(100 * held.slice(0, 10).reduce((a, c) => a + c, 0) / SUPPLY).toFixed(2);
  if (creator) { const d = per.get(creator); m.dev_buy = +(buys.filter((x) => x.user === creator).reduce((s, x) => s + x.sol, 0) / 1e9).toFixed(3); m.dev_sell = +(sells.filter((x) => x.user === creator).reduce((s, x) => s + x.sol, 0) / 1e9).toFixed(3); m.dev_sold_pct = d && d.b > 0 ? +(100 * Math.min(1, d.s / d.b)).toFixed(0) : null; }
  if (bundle && bundle.length) { let b = 0, s = 0, so = 0; for (const w of bundle) { const p = per.get(w); if (p) { b += p.b; s += p.s; } } for (const x of sells) if (bundle.includes(x.user)) so += x.sol; m.bundle_sold_pct = b > 0 ? +(100 * Math.min(1, s / b)).toFixed(0) : null; m.bundle_sell_sol = +(so / 1e9).toFixed(2); m.bundle_n = bundle.length; }
  const t0 = r.t0 ?? xs[0]?.t, s0 = xs[0]?.slot; m.early = buys.filter((x) => (s0 != null && x.slot === s0) || (t0 != null && x.t <= t0 + 3)).map((x) => ({ w: x.user, b0: s0 != null && x.slot === s0 ? 1 : 0, sec: t0 != null ? x.t - t0 : null, sol: +(x.sol / 1e9).toFixed(3), tok: x.tok || 0 }));
  m.early_n = new Set(m.early.map((e) => e.w)).size; m.early_sol = +m.early.reduce((a, e) => a + e.sol, 0).toFixed(2);
  return m;
}

/** every 15 s: checkpoints at 60 / 90 / 120 s for launches that passed any pre-filter (and a sample of the rest for the A1 control). */
let checking = false;
export async function fdCheck(db, tdb = null) {
  if (checking) return; checking = true;
  try {
    const t = now();
    // pre-filtered launches first; then the A1 control: every 7th other launch (so the control costs ~15% of the credits of "all launches")
    const list = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM fd_watch WHERE done=0 AND created_ts>=? AND (pre_nb=1 OR (rowid % 7)=0) ORDER BY pre DESC, created_ts").all(t - 300);
    fdStat.open = list.length;
    for (const w of list) {
      const age = t - w.created_ts, checks = JSON.parse(w.checks || "[]"), doneAt = new Set(checks.map((c) => c.at));
      if (age > CHECKS[CHECKS.length - 1] + 60) { db.prepare("UPDATE fd_watch SET done=1 WHERE mint=?").run(w.mint); continue; }
      const due = CHECKS.filter((c) => c <= age && !doneAt.has(c)); if (!due.length) continue;
      if (fdStat.credits >= DAY_CREDITS) { fdStat.lastErr = `daily Helius budget ${DAY_CREDITS} reached`; break; }
      const at = due[due.length - 1];
      try {
        const c0 = studyStat.credits, r = await earlyTrades(w.mint, w.created_ts + at); fdStat.credits += studyStat.credits - c0; fdStat.checks++; fdStat.lastCheck = new Date().toISOString();
        let bundle = null; if (tdb && REC_BUNDLE) try { bundle = tdb.prepare("SELECT wallet FROM cluster_wallets WHERE mint=? AND role IN ('bundle','dev')").all(w.mint).map((x) => x.wallet); } catch {} // 0.8.12: behind LAUNCH_FD_REC_BUNDLE (off) — the launcher restarted every 2–6 min after 0.8.11 with nothing in the error log (pm2 memory limit?); the tracker-table lookup and the pump.fun page fetch are the two suspects, switched off until proven
        const mm = metrics(r, (r.t0 || w.created_ts) + at, w.creator, bundle), early = mm.early; delete mm.early; // the wallets go to fd_early, not into the check row
        const m = { at, ...mm, txs: r.txs, venue: r.pumpCurve ? (r.complete ? "pump.fun graduated" : "pump.fun curve") : "other" };
        // 0.9.9: recording only — farm scores (previous-dump participants among this check's buyers, as known NOW), funder history, per-wallet state
        try { const upto = (r.t0 || w.created_ts) + at, tl = r.trades.filter((x) => x.t <= upto); Object.assign(m, farmScores(tl, t)); if (BASE_BAR(m)) { m.funder_hist = funderHistory(w.funder, w.created_ts); recordWalletState(w.mint, at, tl, r.t0 || w.created_ts, new Set((early || []).map((e) => e.w)), t); if (at === 60) m.meta_reuse = metaReuse(w.mint, w.created_ts); } } catch (e) { fdStat.lastErr = ("farm: " + e.message).slice(0, 160); }
        try { recordEarly(db, w, early, r, (r.t0 || w.created_ts) + at); } catch (e) { fdStat.lastErr = ("early: " + e.message).slice(0, 160); }
        if (REC_META && !w.meta_ts && !checks.length) { db.prepare("UPDATE fd_watch SET meta_ts=? WHERE mint=?").run(now(), w.mint); fetchMeta(db, w.mint).catch(() => {}); } // 0.8.11: once per launch, not awaited; 0.8.12: behind LAUNCH_FD_REC_META (off: pump.fun answers 403 to the droplet anyway)
        checks.push(m); const entered = new Set(String(w.entered || "").split(",").filter(Boolean));
        const prev = checks.slice(0, -1).filter((c) => c.at != null && !c.err);
        // 0.8.9: GMGN's token info ONCE per check (it was fetched once per rule that passed), only past the loosest bar; its holder count goes into the check row and the FDH rules
        let info = null; if (BASE_BAR(m) && entered.size < RULES.length) { try { info = await gget("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol", address: w.mint }); } catch {} m.holders = N(info?.holder_count) || null;
          const su0 = await solPrice(); m.mcUsd = m.mcSol > 0 && su0 > 0 ? +(m.mcSol * su0).toFixed(0) : null; } // 0.9.6: market cap in USD at the check, for the U books
        // 0.9.7: a second mint launched under a name a book already entered (Gemini, 5 Nov 19:18 UK: 6 of FDL+U's 10 remaining rugs, none of its winners; the generic same-name-in-the-hour count does NOT separate) — logged on every check, not a rule
        try { const sn = db.prepare("SELECT mint, entry_ts, strat FROM vol_paper WHERE lower(name)=lower(?) AND mint<>? AND entry_ts>=? AND entry_ts<? ORDER BY entry_ts LIMIT 1").get(w.name, w.mint, t - 7200, t); if (sn) { m.same_name_mint = sn.mint; m.same_name_min = +((t - sn.entry_ts) / 60).toFixed(1); } } catch {}
        // 0.9.6: holder growth 60 → 120 s on every mint the U books took at 60 s (logged, not a rule)
        if (at === 120 && m.holders != null && [...entered].some((k) => /U/.test(k))) { const c60 = checks.find((c) => c.at === 60 && c.holders != null); if (c60) db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fd_hgrowth (mint, name, h60, h120, ratio, mc60, mc120, ts) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(w.mint, w.name, c60.holders, m.holders, +(m.holders / c60.holders).toFixed(2), c60.mcUsd ?? null, m.mcUsd ?? null, t); }
        for (const rule of RULES) { // 0.9.9: every rule's decision is recorded at every check (passed / failed / already entered / no price), so rejected candidates are rows, not silence
          if (entered.has(rule.k)) { recordDecision(w.mint, at, t, VER, rule.k, 0, "already entered"); continue; }
          let ok = false; try { ok = !!rule.ok(w, m, prev); } catch {}
          if (!ok) { recordDecision(w.mint, at, t, VER, rule.k, 0, BASE_BAR(m) ? "failed" : "below base bar"); continue; }
          const gpx = N(info?.price?.price ?? info?.price), supply = N(info?.total_supply) || N(info?.circulating_supply) || 1e9, su = await solPrice();
          const graduated = /graduated|other/.test(m.venue || ""), px = !graduated && m.mcSol > 0 && su > 0 ? m.mcSol * su / supply : gpx; // 0.8.3: chain price on the curve; 0.8.6: GMGN's after graduation (the pool market cap from the trade decoder came out 100× on 4 Nov — "$13,737K" entries)
          if (!(px > 0)) { m["skip_" + rule.k] = "no price"; recordDecision(w.mint, at, t, VER, rule.k, 0, "no price"); continue; }
          recordDecision(w.mint, at, t, VER, rule.k, 1, "entered");
          m.gmgn_px = gpx, m.chain_px = px, m.sol_usd = su;
          const curve = m.venue === "pump.fun curve" ? 1 : m.venue ? 0 : (r.pumpCurve && !r.complete && N(info?.launchpad_progress) != null && N(info.launchpad_progress) < 1 ? 1 : 0); // 0.8.22: our own check decides (Codex, 5 Nov 09:08 UK: GMGN's progress field was missing on 29 coins → flagged graduated → the 25% trail scored them; all five FDH trades among them)
          db.prepare(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO vol_paper (strat, mint, name, symbol, entry_ts, entry_price, entry_mc, vol5, created_ts, age_min, holders, exchange, launchpad, curve, row) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
            .run(rule.k, w.mint, w.name, w.symbol, t, px, px * supply, null, w.created_ts, +(age / 60).toFixed(1), N(info?.holder_count), m.venue, info?.launchpad ?? null, curve,
              JSON.stringify({ fd: { funder: w.funder, funder_sol: w.funder_sol, funded_min_before: w.funded_ts ? +((w.created_ts - w.funded_ts) / 60).toFixed(1) : null, funder_launches: w.funder_launches, bundle_pct: w.bundle_pct, bundle_sol: w.bundle_sol }, ...m }));
          entered.add(rule.k); fdStat.entries++;
          if (isLiveBook(rule.k)) { const vid = db.prepare("SELECT id FROM vol_paper WHERE strat=? AND mint=?").get(rule.k, w.mint)?.id; if (vid) liveBuy({ id: vid, strat: rule.k, mint: w.mint, name: w.name, curve, entry_price: px, su, t }).catch(() => {}); } // 0.8.17: real buy, not awaited
        }
        // 0.8.15: quote shadowing — one buy quote per coin per check for the rows just booked (all rules share it), and a 1-in-N sample of checked launches that entered nothing
        if (m.chain_px > 0 && m.sol_usd > 0) { const rowsNow = db.prepare("SELECT id, strat FROM vol_paper WHERE mint=? AND entry_ts=? AND q_ts IS NULL").all(w.mint, t); const ids = rowsNow.map((x) => x.id); if (ids.length) {
          traceBuyers(w.mint, r.trades.filter((x) => x.t <= (r.t0 || w.created_ts) + 60), w.created_ts, w.creator).catch(() => {}); // 0.9.11: where the first-minute money came from, for every coin a book entered (not awaited)
          // 0.9.6: the U books fill through the guard (and the *120 variants 60 s later); every other book exactly as before, so the comparison stays like for like
          const opt = (x) => RULES.find((r) => r.k === x.strat) || {}, plain = rowsNow.filter((x) => !opt(x).u).map((x) => x.id), u = rowsNow.filter((x) => opt(x).u && !opt(x).delayMs).map((x) => x.id), u120 = rowsNow.filter((x) => opt(x).u && opt(x).delayMs).map((x) => x.id);
          if (plain.length) chainEntry(w.mint, plain, m.chain_px, m.sol_usd, [...entered].join(",")).catch(() => {});
          if (u.length) chainEntry(w.mint, u, m.chain_px, m.sol_usd, "U", { guard: U_GUARD }).catch(() => {});
          if (u120.length) chainEntry(w.mint, u120, m.chain_px, m.sol_usd, "U120", { guard: U_GUARD, delayMs: 60000 }).catch(() => {});
          shadowBuy(db, w.mint, m.chain_px, m.sol_usd, ids, m).catch(() => {}); } } // 0.9.0: the simulated executable fill from the chain's reserves (chainfill.mjs) first — it is the entry price that matters
        else if (at === 60 && entered.size === 0 && BASE_BAR(m) && SHADOW_SAMPLE > 0 && (fdStat.checks % SHADOW_SAMPLE) === 0) { const su = await solPrice(); if (su > 0 && m.mcSol > 0) shadowSample(db, w, m, m.mcSol * su / 1e9, su).catch(() => {}); }
        const dead = at >= 90 && m.net < 5 && m.buyers < 20 && w.pre !== 1; // nothing happening: stop reading it (credits); pre-filtered coins always get their 120 s check (FD120)
        db.prepare("UPDATE fd_watch SET checks=?, entered=?, done=? WHERE mint=?").run(JSON.stringify(checks), [...entered].join(","), dead || entered.size === RULES.length ? 1 : 0, w.mint);
      } catch (e) { fdStat.errors++; fdStat.lastErr = `${w.mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 160); checks.push({ at, err: e.message.slice(0, 80) }); db.prepare("UPDATE fd_watch SET checks=? WHERE mint=?").run(JSON.stringify(checks), w.mint); }
    }
  } finally { checking = false; }
}

/** 0.8.11: the first buyers of a launch — the create-block wallets (b0) and anyone in the first 3 s — kept per wallet so hit rates can be counted later.
 *  Inserted at the first check that sees them (at most 40 wallets, biggest buys first); "exited" (sold 95%+ of what it bought) is refreshed at every check. */
export function recordEarly(db, w, early, r, upto) {
  if (!early || !early.length) return;
  const have = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) c FROM fd_early WHERE mint=?").get(w.mint).c;
  if (!have) { const ins = db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fd_early (mint, wallet, slot0, sec, sol, tok, exited, created_ts) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,0,?)"); const seen = new Set();
    for (const e of [...early].sort((a, b) => b.sol - a.sol)) { if (seen.has(e.w)) continue; seen.add(e.w); if (seen.size > 40) break; ins.run(w.mint, e.w, e.b0, e.sec, e.sol, e.tok, w.created_ts); } }
  const per = new Map(); for (const x of r.trades) { if (x.t > upto) continue; const p = per.get(x.user) || { b: 0, s: 0 }; if (x.isBuy) p.b += x.tok || 0; else p.s += x.tok || 0; per.set(x.user, p); }
  const upd = db.prepare("UPDATE fd_early SET exited=? WHERE mint=? AND wallet=?");
  for (const row of db.prepare("SELECT wallet FROM fd_early WHERE mint=?").all(w.mint)) { const p = per.get(row.wallet); upd.run(p && p.b > 0 && p.s >= 0.95 * p.b ? 1 : 0, w.mint, row.wallet); }
}

/** 0.8.11: socials / image / reply count from pump.fun's own coin page (the tracker's socials.js uses the same endpoint). Not awaited; one call a launch. */
async function fetchMeta(db, mint) {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`https://frontend-api-v3.pump.fun/coins/${mint}`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000), headers: { accept: "application/json", "user-agent": "Mozilla/5.0" } });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error("pump " + res.status);
    const j = await res.json(); if (!j || !j.mint) throw new Error("pump empty");
    const soc = []; if (j.twitter) soc.push("tw"); if (j.telegram) soc.push("tg"); if (j.website) soc.push("web");
    db.prepare("UPDATE fd_watch SET socials=?, image=?, replies=?, koth_ts=?, meta_err=NULL WHERE mint=?").run(soc.join(",") || "none", j.image_uri ? 1 : 0, N(j.reply_count) ?? null, j.king_of_the_hill_timestamp ? Math.floor(Number(j.king_of_the_hill_timestamp) / 1000) : null, mint);
    fdStat.meta = (fdStat.meta || 0) + 1;
  } catch (e) { try { db.prepare("UPDATE fd_watch SET meta_err=? WHERE mint=?").run(e.message.slice(0, 60), mint); } catch {} fdStat.metaErr = (fdStat.metaErr || 0) + 1; }
}

/** 0.8.11, hourly: every early-buyer wallet's record over the last 7 days against the tracker's outcome for each launch (graduated; peak ≥ $50K / $100K on the curve
 *  and as far as the tracker followed it). The "follow the sniper" question — a wallet in many winners AND few duds — is answered from fd_sniper_stats.csv. */
export const snStat = { runs: 0, wallets: 0, lastRun: null, lastErr: "" };
export function fdSniperStats(db, tdb) {
  try {
    const since = now() - 7 * 86400;
    const rows = db.prepare("SELECT mint, wallet, slot0, sol, exited, created_ts FROM fd_early WHERE created_ts>=?").all(since);
    if (!rows.length) return;
    const out = new Map(); const mints = [...new Set(rows.map((r) => r.mint))];
    for (let i = 0; i < mints.length; i += 500) { const chunk = mints.slice(i, i + 500); let q = []; try { q = tdb ? tdb.prepare(`SELECT mint, graduated, peak_mc_usd FROM tokens WHERE mint IN (${chunk.map(() => "?").join(",")})`).all(...chunk) : []; } catch {} for (const t of q) out.set(t.mint, t); }
    const per = new Map();
    for (const r of rows) { const t = out.get(r.mint) || {}; const p = per.get(r.wallet) || { launches: 0, slot0: 0, graduated: 0, peak50k: 0, peak100k: 0, sol: 0, exited: 0, first: r.created_ts, last: r.created_ts };
      p.launches++; p.slot0 += r.slot0 ? 1 : 0; p.graduated += t.graduated ? 1 : 0; p.peak50k += (t.peak_mc_usd || 0) >= 50000 ? 1 : 0; p.peak100k += (t.peak_mc_usd || 0) >= 100000 ? 1 : 0; p.sol += r.sol || 0; p.exited += r.exited ? 1 : 0; p.first = Math.min(p.first, r.created_ts); p.last = Math.max(p.last, r.created_ts); per.set(r.wallet, p); }
    const ins = db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO fd_sniper_stats (wallet, launches, slot0, graduated, peak50k, peak100k, sol_avg, exit_pct, first_ts, last_ts, updated) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)");
    const tx = db.transaction(() => { db.prepare("DELETE FROM fd_sniper_stats").run(); for (const [w, p] of per) ins.run(w, p.launches, p.slot0, p.graduated, p.peak50k, p.peak100k, +(p.sol / p.launches).toFixed(3), +(100 * p.exited / p.launches).toFixed(0), p.first, p.last, now()); }); tx();
    snStat.runs++; snStat.wallets = per.size; snStat.lastRun = new Date().toISOString();
  } catch (e) { snStat.lastErr = e.message.slice(0, 160); }
}

/** 0.8.15: the real buy quote for SHADOW_SOL at the moment of a paper entry, kept on every row booked for that coin at that check. q_gap = quote price / chain price − 1 (positive = we would have paid more than the paper book). */
async function shadowBuy(db, mint, chainPx, su, ids, m) {
  const lamports = Math.round(SHADOW_SOL * 1e9);
  try { const q = await buyQuote(mint, lamports); const px = q.pxSol * su, gap = px / chainPx - 1; qStat.buys++;
    db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET q_ts=?, q_px=?, q_gap=?, q_tokens=?, q_impact=?, q_ms=? WHERE id IN (" + ids.map(() => "?").join(",") + ")").run(now(), px, +gap.toFixed(4), q.tokens, q.impact, q.ms, ...ids);
    db.prepare("INSERT INTO fd_quotes (mint, ts, at, entered, chain_px, q_px, q_gap, q_impact, q_ms, venue, mcSol, net, err) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,NULL)").run(mint, now(), m.at, "entry", chainPx, px, +gap.toFixed(4), q.impact, q.ms, m.venue, m.mcSol, m.net);
  } catch (e) { qStat.errors++; qStat.lastErr = `buy ${mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 120); db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET q_ts=?, q_err=? WHERE id IN (" + ids.map(() => "?").join(",") + ")").run(now(), e.message.slice(0, 80), ...ids); }
}
/** 0.8.15: the same quote on a sample of launches that passed a check but entered no book — the fill cost of what we avoid */
async function shadowSample(db, w, m, chainPx, su) {
  const lamports = Math.round(SHADOW_SOL * 1e9);
  try { const q = await buyQuote(w.mint, lamports); const px = q.pxSol * su; qStat.samples++;
    db.prepare("INSERT INTO fd_quotes (mint, ts, at, entered, chain_px, q_px, q_gap, q_impact, q_ms, venue, mcSol, net, err) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,NULL)").run(w.mint, now(), m.at, "sample", chainPx, px, +(px / chainPx - 1).toFixed(4), q.impact, q.ms, m.venue, m.mcSol, m.net);
  } catch (e) { qStat.errors++; qStat.lastErr = `sample ${w.mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 120); db.prepare("INSERT INTO fd_quotes (mint, ts, at, entered, chain_px, venue, mcSol, net, err) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(w.mint, now(), m.at, "sample", chainPx, m.venue, m.mcSol, m.net, e.message.slice(0, 80)); }
}

// launcher 0.9.0 (owner, 5 Nov 2026 12:14 UK: "correct both buy and sell pricing across every book … buy: use current reserves to calculate tokens
// received for 0.2 SOL, including price impact and venue fees; sell: track that token quantity and calculate its executable sell proceeds from
// updated reserves; run the trail on those proceeds, including the price after a dump; preserve the original paper results; label the corrected
// results as simulated executable fills"). The first real trade (The 10 Year Old Trencher, 09:43 UK) filled 44% above the paper entry price with
// sub-second execution: the paper book books the last trade our decoder saw, which is stale on a coin being bought hard, and its exits run on
// 1-minute candles that cannot see a one-transaction dump.
// SIMULATED EXECUTABLE FILL ("s_*"), one per paper trade, every book:
//   entry: at the second a rule passes, the bonding curve's reserves (or the PumpSwap pool's, after graduation) are read and the tokens 0.2 SOL
//          would receive are computed with the venue's own maths (pump.mjs quoteBuy / the PumpSwap constant product), fees and impact included;
//   exit:  the position's curve (or pool reserve accounts) is subscribed to; every change re-prices the tokens as the SOL they would fetch
//          (quoteSell) — so after a dump the proceeds are the post-dump proceeds; the venue trail (40% curve / 25% graduated, off the running high
//          of those proceeds, from 1 = what went in) or the 2-hour horizon sells; a graduation mid-trade moves the subscription to the pool;
//   backstop: every 30 s every open position's accounts are re-read (gap detection for dropped subscriptions; flagged on the trade).
// What this cannot do (stays on the page): guarantee a fill — latency, ordering, a failed transaction, network fees. The live book measures those.
import { PublicKey } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { conn, rpcStats, getAccounts, onAccount, offAccount } from "./rpc.mjs";
import { GLOBAL, PUMP, WSOL, pda, decodeGlobal, decodeCurve, quoteBuy, quoteSell, curveOf } from "./pump.mjs";
import { trailPct } from "./volpaper.mjs";

const n = (k, d) => (process.env[k] != null && process.env[k] !== "" && Number.isFinite(Number(process.env[k])) ? Number(process.env[k]) : d);
const STAKE = n("LAUNCH_SHADOW_SOL", 0.2), LAMPORTS = BigInt(Math.round(STAKE * 1e9)), STAKE02 = n("LAUNCH_SIM_SMALL_SOL", 0.02), LAMPORTS02 = BigInt(Math.round(STAKE02 * 1e9)), HORIZON = 120 * 60, NET_FEE = n("LAUNCH_SIM_NET_FEE", 0.0015); // network + priority fee, both legs, in SOL
const now = () => Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
const BUY_LAND_MS = n("LAUNCH_SIM_BUY_LAND_MS", 1000), SELL_LAND_MS = n("LAUNCH_SIM_SELL_LAND_MS", 700); // 0.9.2: a transaction executes against the chain state this long after we decide (measured on the first live FD trades: buys landed 0.6–1.0 s after the check, sells 0.5 s after the trail)
// 0.9.12: prepared statements are cached (the tick loop prepared each statement afresh on every price update — a million a day — and the native memory behind them is only freed by the garbage collector; rss climbed ~55 MB/h)
const STMT = new Map(); const q = (sql) => { let st = STMT.get(sql); if (!st) { st = db.prepare(sql); STMT.set(sql, st); } return st; };
export const cfStat = { entries: 0, skipped: 0, entryErrs: 0, updates: 0, exits: 0, subs: 0, resubs: 0, drops: 0, backstops: 0, errors: 0, lastErr: "" };

// ---- PumpSwap (pump.fun's AMM) — pool lookup, decoding and the constant-product quotes, ported from executor/src/amm.mjs ----
export const PUMP_AMM = new PublicKey("pAMMBay6oceH9fJKBRHGP5D4bD4sWpmSwMn52FMfXEA");
const u16 = (v) => { const b = Buffer.alloc(2); b.writeUInt16LE(v); return b; };
const rPk = (d, o) => new PublicKey(d.subarray(o, o + 32)), rU64 = (d, o) => d.readBigUInt64LE(o);
const POOL_DISC = Buffer.from([241, 154, 109, 4, 17, 177, 109, 188]);
const GLOBAL_CONFIG = pda(["global_config"], PUMP_AMM);
const poolAuthorityOf = (mint) => pda(["pool-authority", mint], PUMP);
export const migratedPoolOf = (mint) => pda(["pool", u16(0), poolAuthorityOf(mint), mint, WSOL], PUMP_AMM);
const pk = (m) => (m instanceof PublicKey ? m : new PublicKey(m)); // 0.9.1: mints arrive as strings; a seed must be the 32-byte key ("Max seed length exceeded" on the first 0.9.0 entries)
function decodePool(d) {
  if (!d || d.length < 243 || !d.subarray(0, 8).equals(POOL_DISC)) return null;
  const p = { baseMint: rPk(d, 43), quoteMint: rPk(d, 75), baseAcc: rPk(d, 139), quoteAcc: rPk(d, 171), virtualQuote: 0n, creatorFeeBps: null };
  if (d.length >= 261) { try { p.virtualQuote = (d.readBigInt64LE(253) << 64n) | d.readBigUInt64LE(245); } catch { p.virtualQuote = 0n; } }
  if (d.length >= 269) p.creatorFeeBps = Number(rU64(d, 261));
  return p;
}
function decodeCfg(d) { if (!d || d.length < 321) return null; return { lpFeeBps: Number(rU64(d, 40)), protocolFeeBps: Number(rU64(d, 48)), coinCreatorFeeBps: Number(rU64(d, 313)) }; }
const ceilDiv = (a, b) => (a + b - 1n) / b;
const poolBps = (pool, cfg) => BigInt((cfg?.lpFeeBps ?? 20) + (cfg?.protocolFeeBps ?? 5) + (pool.creatorFeeBps ?? cfg?.coinCreatorFeeBps ?? 95));
function poolBuy(pool, cfg, baseRes, quoteRes, quoteIn) { const fee = poolBps(pool, cfg), q = BigInt(quoteIn), net = q - ceilDiv(q * fee, 10000n), qr = BigInt(quoteRes) + (pool.virtualQuote > 0n ? pool.virtualQuote : 0n); return net > 0n ? (BigInt(baseRes) * net) / (qr + net) : 0n; }
function poolSell(pool, cfg, baseRes, quoteRes, baseIn) { const fee = poolBps(pool, cfg), b = BigInt(baseIn), qr = BigInt(quoteRes) + (pool.virtualQuote > 0n ? pool.virtualQuote : 0n); const gross = (qr * b) / (BigInt(baseRes) + b); return gross - ceilDiv(gross * fee, 10000n); }
const tokenAmount = (d) => (d && d.length >= 72 ? d.readBigUInt64LE(64) : null); // SPL token account: amount at bytes 64–71

let db = null, G = null, gAt = 0, CFG = null, cfgAt = 0;
async function global() { if (G && Date.now() - gAt < 600000) return G; const [a] = await getAccounts([GLOBAL], "confirmed"); const g = decodeGlobal(a?.data); if (g) { G = g; gAt = Date.now(); } return G; }
async function ammCfg() { if (CFG && Date.now() - cfgAt < 600000) return CFG; const [a] = await getAccounts([GLOBAL_CONFIG], "confirmed"); const c = decodeCfg(a?.data); if (c) { CFG = c; cfgAt = Date.now(); } return CFG; }

export function initChainFill(d) { STMT.clear();
  db = d;
  db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chain_fill (vol_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, mint TEXT, strat TEXT, entry_ts INTEGER, venue TEXT, curve INTEGER, s_sol_in REAL, s_tokens TEXT, s_px REAL, s_in_gap REAL, s_x0 REAL,
    s_hi REAL, s_last_x REAL, s_last_ts INTEGER, s_n INTEGER DEFAULT 0, s_exit_x REAL, s_exit_ts INTEGER, s_exit_reason TEXT, s_r REAL, s_drops INTEGER DEFAULT 0, s_backstops INTEGER DEFAULT 0, s_err TEXT, done INTEGER DEFAULT 0)`);
  db.exec("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS chain_fill_open ON chain_fill (done)");
  for (const c of ["s_exit_src TEXT", "s_max_gap_s INTEGER", "s_trig_ts REAL", "s_trig_x REAL", "s_fill_ms INTEGER", "s_entry_state INTEGER", "s_r02 REAL", "s_half_x REAL", "s_r_half REAL", "s_delay_ms INTEGER", "s_tokens02 TEXT", "s_x02 REAL", "s_hi02 REAL", "s_exit_x02 REAL", "s_x60 REAL", "s_x60_02 REAL", "s_gap_dec REAL", "s_t2x INTEGER", "s_t3x INTEGER", "s_t5x INTEGER"]) try { db.exec(`ALTER TABLE chain_fill ADD COLUMN ${c}`); } catch {} // 0.9.1: which source produced the exit (push / backstop) and the longest silence between price updates before it
  try { q("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fd_rules (k, since_ts, ver) VALUES ('SIM', ?, ?)").run(now(), "0.9.0"); } catch {} // when the simulated fills started — the page counts every book from here on this measure
}

/** Read what a position is worth now from the chain: curve → quoteSell; pool → constant product on the two reserve token accounts. Returns { lamports, venue, complete, pool? } or null. */
async function readValue(mint, tokens, st) {
  const g = await global();
  if (!st.pool) {
    const [a] = await getAccounts([curveOf(pk(mint))], "processed"); const c = decodeCurve(a?.data);
    if (!c) return null;
    if (!c.complete) return { lamports: quoteSell(c, g, tokens), venue: "curve", complete: false };
    st.complete = true;
  }
  // graduated: the pool (may not exist for a moment after the curve completes)
  if (!st.pool) { const pkp = migratedPoolOf(pk(mint)); const [p] = await getAccounts([pkp], "processed"); const pool = decodePool(p?.data); if (!pool) return { lamports: null, venue: "migrating", complete: true }; st.pool = pool; st.poolPk = pkp; } // 0.9.5: the local was shadowing the pk() helper ("Cannot access pk before initialization" in every backstop read since 0.9.1)
  const cfg = await ammCfg(); const [b, q] = await getAccounts([st.pool.baseAcc, st.pool.quoteAcc], "processed"); const br = tokenAmount(b?.data), qr = tokenAmount(q?.data);
  if (br == null || qr == null) return { lamports: null, venue: "pool", complete: true };
  st.baseRes = br; st.quoteRes = qr;
  return { lamports: poolSell(st.pool, cfg, br, qr, tokens), venue: "pool", complete: true };
}

/** one read of the reserves: tokens for STAKE on this state, plus a sell quote on the same state (for the round-trip figure). Sets st.pool when graduated. */
async function fillState(mint, st) {
  const g = await global(); if (!g) throw new Error("no Global");
  if (!st.pool) { const [a] = await getAccounts([curveOf(pk(mint))], "processed"); const c = decodeCurve(a?.data);
    if (c && !c.complete) { st.sellOf = (tok) => quoteSell(c, g, tok); return { tokens: quoteBuy(c, g, LAMPORTS).tokens, tokens02: quoteBuy(c, g, LAMPORTS02).tokens, venue: "curve", curve: 1 }; }
    if (!c) return null; st.complete = true; }
  if (!st.pool) { const pkp = migratedPoolOf(pk(mint)); const [p] = await getAccounts([pkp], "processed"); const pool = decodePool(p?.data); if (!pool) throw new Error("migrating: no pool yet"); st.pool = pool; st.poolPk = pkp; }
  const cfg = await ammCfg(); const [b, q] = await getAccounts([st.pool.baseAcc, st.pool.quoteAcc], "processed"); const br = tokenAmount(b?.data), qr = tokenAmount(q?.data);
  if (br == null || qr == null) throw new Error("pool reserves unreadable"); st.baseRes = br; st.quoteRes = qr;
  const pool = st.pool; st.sellOf = (tok) => poolSell(pool, cfg, br, qr, tok);
  return { tokens: poolBuy(pool, cfg, br, qr, LAMPORTS), tokens02: poolBuy(pool, cfg, br, qr, LAMPORTS02), venue: "pool", curve: 0 };
}
/** ENTRY: called once per coin per check when any rule books a paper entry (ids = the vol_paper rows just booked for that coin). */
export async function chainEntry(mint, ids, chainPx, su, strat, opts = {}) {
  if (!db || !ids.length) return;
  const delayMs = opts.delayMs || 0, guard = opts.guard || 0; // 0.9.6: delayMs = decide now, fill later (the 120-s variants of the U books); guard = skip the fill if the price has already dropped this far from the check price (0.3 = 30%)
  if (delayMs > 0) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, delayMs));
  const t = now(); const st = { pool: null };
  try {
    const g = await global(); if (!g) throw new Error("no Global");
    const [a] = await getAccounts([curveOf(pk(mint))], "processed"); const c = decodeCurve(a?.data);
    let tokens = 0n, tokens02 = 0n, venue = "curve", curve = 1;
    if (c && !c.complete) { tokens = quoteBuy(c, g, LAMPORTS).tokens; tokens02 = quoteBuy(c, g, LAMPORTS02).tokens; st.sellOf = (tok) => quoteSell(c, g, tok); }
    else { // graduated (or not a pump.fun curve): the pool
      const pkp = migratedPoolOf(pk(mint)); const [p] = await getAccounts([pkp], "processed"); const pool = decodePool(p?.data);
      if (!pool) throw new Error(c ? "migrating: no pool yet" : "no curve, no pool");
      const cfg = await ammCfg(); const [b, q] = await getAccounts([pool.baseAcc, pool.quoteAcc], "processed"); const br = tokenAmount(b?.data), qr = tokenAmount(q?.data);
      if (br == null || qr == null) throw new Error("pool reserves unreadable");
      tokens = poolBuy(pool, cfg, br, qr, LAMPORTS); tokens02 = poolBuy(pool, cfg, br, qr, LAMPORTS02); venue = "pool"; curve = 0; st.pool = pool; st.poolPk = pkp; st.baseRes = br; st.quoteRes = qr; st.sellOf = (tok) => poolSell(pool, cfg, br, qr, tok);
    }
    if (tokens <= 0n) throw new Error("zero tokens");
    // 0.9.8 (review, 5 Nov 21:00 UK): the guard is decided on the state a live bot would read BEFORE submitting — this one — and the fill lands BUY_LAND_MS later on whatever state is there then
    const pxDec = STAKE / (Number(tokens) / 1e6) * (su || 0), gapDec = chainPx > 0 && pxDec > 0 ? pxDec / chainPx - 1 : null;
    if (guard > 0 && gapDec != null && gapDec <= -guard) {
      const skip = q("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO chain_fill (vol_id, mint, strat, entry_ts, venue, curve, s_sol_in, s_px, s_in_gap, s_gap_dec, s_exit_reason, s_r, s_r02, s_delay_ms, done) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,0,?,?,?,'skipped-dump',0,0,?,1)");
      for (const id of ids) skip.run(id, mint, strat, t, venue, curve, pxDec || null, +gapDec.toFixed(4), +gapDec.toFixed(4), delayMs); cfStat.skipped = (cfStat.skipped || 0) + ids.length; return; }
    // 0.9.2: the buy lands BUY_LAND_MS after the check — wait that long, re-read the reserves, and fill on that state (the check-time state is kept as the fallback if the re-read fails)
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, BUY_LAND_MS)); let entryState = 0;
    try { const r2 = await fillState(mint, st); if (r2) { tokens = r2.tokens; tokens02 = r2.tokens02; venue = r2.venue; curve = r2.curve; entryState = 1; } } catch (e) { cfStat.lastErr = `entry re-read ${mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 120); }
    const x0 = st.sellOf ? Number(st.sellOf(tokens)) / Number(LAMPORTS) : null; // the round trip on the SAME reserve state: what the buy costs in fees + impact
    const px = STAKE / (Number(tokens) / 1e6) * (su || 0), gap = chainPx > 0 && px > 0 ? px / chainPx - 1 : null; // USD per token, like the paper entry_price
    const x02 = st.sellOf && tokens02 > 0n ? Number(st.sellOf(tokens02)) / Number(LAMPORTS02) : null; // 0.9.8: the real 0.02 SOL position's round trip on the same state
    const ins = q("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO chain_fill (vol_id, mint, strat, entry_ts, venue, curve, s_sol_in, s_tokens, s_px, s_in_gap, s_gap_dec, s_x0, s_hi, s_last_x, s_last_ts, s_n, s_entry_state, s_delay_ms, s_tokens02, s_x02, s_hi02) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1,?,?,?,?,1)");
    for (const id of ids) ins.run(id, mint, strat, t, venue, curve, STAKE, tokens.toString(), px || null, gap != null ? +gap.toFixed(4) : null, gapDec != null ? +gapDec.toFixed(4) : null, x0 != null ? +x0.toFixed(4) : null, 1, x0 != null ? +x0.toFixed(4) : null, now(), entryState, delayMs, tokens02 > 0n ? tokens02.toString() : null, x02 != null ? +x02.toFixed(4) : null);
    cfStat.entries += ids.length;
    watch(mint, st);
  } catch (e) { cfStat.entryErrs++; cfStat.lastErr = `entry ${mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 140);
    const ins = q("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO chain_fill (vol_id, mint, strat, entry_ts, venue, s_err, done) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,1)"); for (const id of ids) ins.run(id, mint, strat, t, "none", e.message.slice(0, 100)); }
}

// ---- open positions: one watcher per mint (all books' rows of that coin share it) ----
const W = new Map(); // mint → { st, subs: [ids], lastPush }
const pending = new Set(); // 0.9.2: mints with a triggered sell waiting for the landing state
setInterval(() => { for (const mint of [...pending]) { pending.delete(mint); setTimeout(() => { const w = W.get(mint); if (!w) return; readValue(mint, 1n, w.st).then((v) => { if (!v || v.lamports == null) return; const st = w.st; if (v.venue === "curve") getAccounts([curveOf(pk(mint))], "processed").then(([a]) => { const c = decodeCurve(a?.data); if (c && G) apply(mint, (tok) => quoteSell(c, G, tok), "land"); }).catch(() => {}); else if (st.pool && CFG) apply(mint, (tok) => poolSell(st.pool, CFG, st.baseRes, st.quoteRes, tok), "land"); }).catch(() => {}); }, SELL_LAND_MS + 50); } }, 200);
function rows(mint) { return q("SELECT vol_id, strat, curve, s_tokens, s_hi, entry_ts, s_last_ts, s_max_gap_s, s_trig_ts, s_trig_x, s_half_x, s_tokens02, s_hi02, s_x60, s_t2x, s_t3x, s_t5x FROM chain_fill WHERE mint=? AND done=0 AND s_tokens IS NOT NULL").all(mint); }
function apply(mint, lamportsOf, src) {
  const t = now(); let open = 0;
  for (const r of rows(mint)) {
    const lam = lamportsOf(BigInt(r.s_tokens)); if (lam == null) { open++; continue; }
    const x = Number(lam) / Number(LAMPORTS), hi = Math.max(r.s_hi || 1, x), keep = 1 - trailPct(r.curve);
    const lam02 = r.s_tokens02 ? lamportsOf(BigInt(r.s_tokens02)) : null, x02 = lam02 != null ? Number(lam02) / Number(LAMPORTS02) : null, hi02 = x02 != null ? Math.max(r.s_hi02 || 1, x02) : null; // 0.9.8: the real 0.02 position, valued on the same state
    if (r.s_x60 == null && t - r.entry_ts >= 60) q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_x60=?, s_x60_02=? WHERE vol_id=? AND s_x60 IS NULL").run(+x.toFixed(4), x02 != null ? +x02.toFixed(4) : null, r.vol_id); // 0.9.8: executable value of the still-open position 60 s after entry (the time-stop column)
    if (x02 != null) q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_x02=?, s_hi02=? WHERE vol_id=?").run(+x02.toFixed(4), +hi02.toFixed(4), r.vol_id);
    const gap = Math.max(r.s_max_gap_s || 0, r.s_last_ts ? t - r.s_last_ts : 0); // the longest silence between price updates so far
    const tms = Date.now();
    if (r.s_trig_ts != null) { // 0.9.2: the sell was decided at s_trig_ts; it executes on the first state SELL_LAND_MS or more after that
      if (tms - r.s_trig_ts * 1000 >= SELL_LAND_MS) { const reason = t - r.entry_ts >= HORIZON && !(r.s_trig_x <= (r.s_hi || 1) * keep) ? "2h" : "trail";
        const rHalf = r.s_half_x != null ? 0.5 * r.s_half_x + 0.5 * x - 1 - NET_FEE / STAKE : x - 1 - NET_FEE / STAKE; // 0.9.6: half sold at the first 2x print, the rest on this exit
        const r02 = x02 != null ? x02 - 1 - NET_FEE / STAKE02 : x - 1 - NET_FEE / STAKE02; // 0.9.8: true 0.02 result from the real 0.02 position (falls back to the rescaled figure if the small quote was unavailable)
        q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_last_x=?, s_last_ts=?, s_hi=?, s_n=s_n+1, s_exit_x=?, s_exit_ts=?, s_exit_reason=?, s_r=?, s_r02=?, s_exit_x02=?, s_r_half=?, s_exit_src=?, s_max_gap_s=?, s_fill_ms=?, done=1 WHERE vol_id=?").run(+x.toFixed(4), t, +hi.toFixed(4), +x.toFixed(4), t, reason, +(x - 1 - NET_FEE / STAKE).toFixed(4), +r02.toFixed(4), x02 != null ? +x02.toFixed(4) : null, +rHalf.toFixed(4), src, gap, Math.round(tms - r.s_trig_ts * 1000), r.vol_id); cfStat.exits++; }
      else { q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_last_x=?, s_last_ts=?, s_hi=?, s_n=s_n+1, s_max_gap_s=? WHERE vol_id=?").run(+x.toFixed(4), t, +hi.toFixed(4), gap, r.vol_id); open++; }
      cfStat.updates++; continue; }
    const trigger = x <= (r.s_hi || 1) * keep || t - r.entry_ts >= HORIZON;
    if (trigger) { q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_last_x=?, s_last_ts=?, s_hi=?, s_n=s_n+1, s_max_gap_s=?, s_trig_ts=?, s_trig_x=? WHERE vol_id=?").run(+x.toFixed(4), t, +hi.toFixed(4), gap, tms / 1000, +x.toFixed(4), r.vol_id); open++; pending.add(mint); }
    else { q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_last_x=?, s_last_ts=?, s_hi=?, s_n=s_n+1, s_max_gap_s=? WHERE vol_id=?").run(+x.toFixed(4), t, +hi.toFixed(4), gap, r.vol_id); open++; }
    if (r.s_half_x == null && x >= 2) q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_half_x=? WHERE vol_id=? AND s_half_x IS NULL").run(+x.toFixed(4), r.vol_id); // 0.9.6: the half-at-2x column — the first print at or above 2x is where half would have gone
    if (r.s_t2x == null && x >= 2) q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_t2x=? WHERE vol_id=? AND s_t2x IS NULL").run(t, r.vol_id); // 0.9.9: executable milestones, kept apart from the final result
    if (r.s_t3x == null && x >= 3) q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_t3x=? WHERE vol_id=? AND s_t3x IS NULL").run(t, r.vol_id);
    if (r.s_t5x == null && x >= 5) q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_t5x=? WHERE vol_id=? AND s_t5x IS NULL").run(t, r.vol_id);
    cfStat.updates++;
  }
  if (src === "backstop") q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_backstops=s_backstops+1 WHERE mint=? AND done=0").run(mint);
  if (!open) unwatch(mint);
}
async function watch(mint, st) {
  if (W.has(mint)) return; const w = { st, subs: [], lastPush: Date.now() }; W.set(mint, w);
  try {
    if (!st.pool) {
      const id = await onAccount(curveOf(pk(mint)), (data) => { try { const c = decodeCurve(data); if (!c) return; w.lastPush = Date.now();
        if (c.complete) { if (!w.st.complete) { w.st.complete = true; resub(mint).catch(() => {}); } return; }
        const g = G; if (!g) return; apply(mint, (tok) => quoteSell(c, g, tok), "sub"); } catch (e) { cfStat.errors++; cfStat.lastErr = "sub: " + e.message.slice(0, 100); } });
      w.subs.push(id); cfStat.subs++;
    } else await subPool(mint, w);
  } catch (e) { cfStat.errors++; cfStat.lastErr = "watch: " + e.message.slice(0, 100); }
}
async function subPool(mint, w) {
  const st = w.st; if (!st.pool) return;
  const onRes = (which) => (data) => { try { const amt = tokenAmount(data); if (amt == null) return; w.lastPush = Date.now(); if (which === "base") st.baseRes = amt; else st.quoteRes = amt;
    if (st.baseRes == null || st.quoteRes == null || !CFG) return; apply(mint, (tok) => poolSell(st.pool, CFG, st.baseRes, st.quoteRes, tok), "sub"); } catch (e) { cfStat.errors++; cfStat.lastErr = "psub: " + e.message.slice(0, 100); } };
  await ammCfg();
  w.subs.push(await onAccount(st.pool.baseAcc, onRes("base")), await onAccount(st.pool.quoteAcc, onRes("quote"))); cfStat.subs += 2;
}
/** the curve completed mid-trade: drop the curve subscription, find the pool (retry until it exists), subscribe to its reserves */
async function resub(mint) {
  const w = W.get(mint); if (!w) return; cfStat.resubs++;
  for (const id of w.subs.splice(0)) await offAccount(id);
  for (let i = 0; i < 20 && W.has(mint); i++) { try { const v = await readValue(mint, 1n, w.st); if (w.st.pool) { await subPool(mint, w); q("UPDATE chain_fill SET venue='pool' WHERE mint=? AND done=0").run(mint); return; } } catch {} await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 3000)); }
}
async function unwatch(mint) { const w = W.get(mint); if (!w) return; W.delete(mint); for (const id of w.subs) await offAccount(id); }

/** BACKSTOP, every 30 s: re-read every open position from the chain (catches dropped subscriptions and anything missed), re-arm watchers after a restart, apply the 2 h horizon. */
let busy = false;
export async function chainBackstop() {
  if (!db || busy) return; busy = true;
  try {
    const open = q("SELECT DISTINCT mint FROM chain_fill WHERE done=0 AND s_tokens IS NOT NULL").all().map((r) => r.mint);
    for (const mint of open) {
      let w = W.get(mint);
      if (!w) { w = { st: { pool: null }, subs: [], lastPush: 0 }; await watch(mint, w.st); w = W.get(mint) || w; cfStat.drops++; q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_drops=s_drops+1 WHERE mint=? AND done=0").run(mint); } // no watcher (restart or lost): re-armed
      try { const v = await readValue(mint, 1n, w.st); cfStat.backstops++;
        if (v && v.lamports != null) { const st = w.st; if (v.venue === "curve") { const [a] = await getAccounts([curveOf(pk(mint))], "processed"); const c = decodeCurve(a?.data); if (c && G) apply(mint, (tok) => quoteSell(c, G, tok), "backstop"); }
          else if (st.pool && CFG && st.baseRes != null && st.quoteRes != null) { if (!w.subs.length) await subPool(mint, w); apply(mint, (tok) => poolSell(st.pool, CFG, st.baseRes, st.quoteRes, tok), "backstop"); } }
        else if (v && v.venue === "migrating") { const t = now(); for (const r of rows(mint)) if (t - r.entry_ts >= HORIZON + 600) q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_exit_reason='migrating-stuck', s_err='no pool 10 min past horizon', done=1 WHERE vol_id=?").run(r.vol_id); }
        else { const t = now(); for (const r of rows(mint)) if (t - r.entry_ts >= HORIZON) { q("UPDATE chain_fill SET s_exit_x=s_last_x, s_exit_ts=?, s_exit_reason='2h-unreadable', s_r=?, s_r02=?, s_r_half=?, s_exit_src='stale', done=1 WHERE vol_id=?").run(t, r.s_last_x != null ? +(r.s_last_x - 1 - NET_FEE / STAKE).toFixed(4) : null, r.s_last_x != null ? +(r.s_last_x - 1 - NET_FEE / 0.02).toFixed(4) : null, r.s_last_x != null ? +(r.s_last_x - 1 - NET_FEE / STAKE).toFixed(4) : null, r.vol_id); } }
      } catch (e) { cfStat.errors++; cfStat.lastErr = `backstop ${mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 120); }
    }
    for (const mint of [...W.keys()]) if (!open.includes(mint)) await unwatch(mint); // nothing open any more
  } catch (e) { cfStat.errors++; cfStat.lastErr = "backstop: " + e.message.slice(0, 120); }
  finally { busy = false; }
}

// 0.9.9 (owner go 5 Nov 2026 ~20:40 UK, after the Codex + Gemini reviews — recording only, no rule reads any of this):
//   farm_wallets  — every wallet that sold inside a collapse (price ≥ 60% down within ≤ 4 slots) of a coin the books had entered, with the dump time. Rebuilt every 30 min
//                   from the tracker's trades table (tracker ≥ 0.19 stores every trade of any launch reaching 15 SOL of buys). "Previous dump participant" only — not ownership.
//   farm scores   — at every check, from the check's own trade list: share of buyers already in the list at that moment, share of buying SOL they put in, share of supply they
//                   still hold; the same three counting only dumps in the last 4 h (4 h = since the qualifying dump). Evidence frozen at decision time: only dumps BEFORE the check count.
//   funder history — launches this funder was seen behind (fd_watch), how many the books entered, how many resolved, severe losses (< 0.35x), runners (≥ 1.2x), unresolved.
//   fd_wallet_state — per check, per first-minute buyer: bought, sold, held, share of supply, first buy, SOL in/out, launch-group and farm flags.
//   fd_decisions   — per check, per rule: passed / failed / already entered / no price, with the rule version.
import { creatorFunder } from "./operators.mjs";
let db = null, tdb = null;
const now = () => Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), today = () => new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const n = (k, d) => (process.env[k] != null && process.env[k] !== "" && Number.isFinite(Number(process.env[k])) ? Number(process.env[k]) : d);
const BF_TOP = n("LAUNCH_BF_TOP", 10), BF_DAY = n("LAUNCH_BF_DAY", 1500); // 0.9.11: funding trace of the top-10 first-minute buyers of every entered coin, ≤ 1,500 wallets a day
export const farmStat = { builds: 0, wallets: 0, dumps: 0, lastBuild: null, lastErr: "", bfWallets: 0, bfDay: "", bfErr: "", bfQueue: 0 };

export function initFarm(_db, _tdb) {
  db = _db; tdb = _tdb;
  db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS farm_wallets (wallet TEXT, mint TEXT, dump_ts INTEGER, sold_sol REAL, drop_pct REAL, PRIMARY KEY (wallet, mint));
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS fw_wallet ON farm_wallets(wallet, dump_ts);
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS farm_dumps (mint TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT, entry_ts INTEGER, dump_ts INTEGER, slot0 INTEGER, slot1 INTEGER, drop_pct REAL, sellers INTEGER, sold_sol REAL, built_ts INTEGER);
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS fd_wallet_state (mint TEXT, at INTEGER, wallet TEXT, bought_tok REAL, sold_tok REAL, held_tok REAL, held_pct REAL, first_ts INTEGER, sol_in REAL, sol_out REAL, early INTEGER, farm INTEGER, farm_4h INTEGER, PRIMARY KEY (mint, at, wallet));
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS fws_mint ON fd_wallet_state(mint, at);
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS fd_decisions (mint TEXT, at INTEGER, ts INTEGER, ver TEXT, rule TEXT, passed INTEGER, reason TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (mint, at, rule));
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS buyer_funding (mint TEXT, wallet TEXT, sol_in REAL, first_ts INTEGER, funder TEXT, funder_sol REAL, funded_ts INTEGER, age_s INTEGER, txs INTEGER, err TEXT, ts INTEGER, PRIMARY KEY (mint, wallet));
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS buyer_funding_sum (mint TEXT PRIMARY KEY, n INTEGER, traced INTEGER, funders INTEGER, same_funder_max INTEGER, same_funder_wallet TEXT, fresh_n INTEGER, fresh_10m_n INTEGER, ts INTEGER);
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS bf_funder ON buyer_funding(funder);`);
}

/** 0.9.11 (owner, 5 Nov 23:34 UK: "add 2 and 4 on"): where the first-minute money came from — the top BF_TOP buyers by SOL of a coin a book entered: who funded each wallet, how much, when
 *  (age_s = seconds between that funding and the launch; "fresh" = funded within 1 h of launch, "fresh_10m" within 10 min). Not awaited by the check loop. A measurement; shared funders are a suspected link, never ownership. */
const bfBusy = new Set();
export async function traceBuyers(mint, trades, createdTs, creator) {
  if (!db || bfBusy.has(mint)) return; if (farmStat.bfDay !== today()) { farmStat.bfDay = today(); farmStat.bfWallets = 0; }
  if (farmStat.bfWallets >= BF_DAY) { farmStat.bfErr = `daily buyer-trace cap ${BF_DAY}`; return; }
  if (db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM buyer_funding_sum WHERE mint=?").get(mint)) return;
  bfBusy.add(mint); farmStat.bfQueue = bfBusy.size;
  try {
    const per = new Map();
    for (const x of trades) if (x.isBuy && x.user !== creator) { const p = per.get(x.user) || { sol: 0, first: x.t }; p.sol += (x.sol || 0) / 1e9; per.set(x.user, p); }
    const top = [...per.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1].sol - a[1].sol).slice(0, BF_TOP);
    const ins = db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO buyer_funding (mint, wallet, sol_in, first_ts, funder, funder_sol, funded_ts, age_s, txs, err, ts) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)");
    for (const [w, p] of top) {
      const known = db.prepare("SELECT funder, funder_sol, funded_ts, txs FROM buyer_funding WHERE wallet=? AND funder IS NOT NULL LIMIT 1").get(w); // a wallet traced on an earlier coin is not traced again
      let f = known; if (!f) { try { f = await creatorFunder(w); farmStat.bfWallets++; } catch (e) { f = { err: e.message.slice(0, 80) }; } }
      ins.run(mint, w, +p.sol.toFixed(4), p.first, f?.funder || null, f?.funder_sol ?? null, f?.funded_ts ?? null, f?.funded_ts ? createdTs - f.funded_ts : null, f?.txs ?? null, f?.err || null, now());
    }
    const rows = db.prepare("SELECT funder, funded_ts FROM buyer_funding WHERE mint=?").all(mint), byF = new Map();
    for (const r of rows) if (r.funder) byF.set(r.funder, (byF.get(r.funder) || 0) + 1);
    const best = [...byF.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0];
    db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO buyer_funding_sum (mint, n, traced, funders, same_funder_max, same_funder_wallet, fresh_n, fresh_10m_n, ts) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)")
      .run(mint, top.length, rows.filter((r) => r.funder).length, byF.size, best ? best[1] : 0, best ? best[0] : null, rows.filter((r) => r.funded_ts && createdTs - r.funded_ts <= 3600 && createdTs - r.funded_ts >= 0).length, rows.filter((r) => r.funded_ts && createdTs - r.funded_ts <= 600 && createdTs - r.funded_ts >= 0).length, now());
  } catch (e) { farmStat.bfErr = e.message.slice(0, 120); } finally { bfBusy.delete(mint); farmStat.bfQueue = bfBusy.size; }
}

/** 0.9.11: metadata reuse at creation time — other launches in the last 24 h with the same Twitter / website / Telegram handle or the same metadata URI (the tracker records socials at launch). Counts, as known at the check. */
export function metaReuse(mint, createdTs) {
  if (!tdb) return null;
  try {
    const t = tdb.prepare("SELECT uri, socials FROM tokens WHERE mint=?").get(mint); if (!t) return null;
    let soc = {}; try { soc = JSON.parse(t.socials || "{}"); } catch {}
    const out = { uri_dup_24h: 0, tw_24h: 0, web_24h: 0, tg_24h: 0 }, since = createdTs - 86400;
    if (t.uri) out.uri_dup_24h = tdb.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) c FROM tokens WHERE create_ts>=? AND create_ts<? AND uri=? AND mint<>?").get(since, createdTs + 1, t.uri, mint).c;
    const cnt = (k) => { const v = soc[k]; if (!v || typeof v !== "string" || v.length < 4) return 0; return tdb.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) c FROM tokens WHERE create_ts>=? AND create_ts<? AND mint<>? AND socials LIKE ?").get(since, createdTs + 1, mint, `%${JSON.stringify(v).slice(1, -1)}%`).c; };
    out.tw_24h = cnt("t"); out.web_24h = cnt("w"); out.tg_24h = cnt("g");
    return out;
  } catch { return null; }
}

/** Rebuild the farm list from the tracker's trades for coins the books entered in the last `hours`. A dump = the worst price fall within ≤ 4 consecutive slots after the entry, if ≥ 60%. */
export function buildFarm(hours = 6) {
  if (!db || !tdb) return;
  const t = now(); let dumps = 0, wallets = 0;
  try {
    const mints = db.prepare("SELECT mint, MIN(entry_ts) e FROM chain_fill WHERE entry_ts>=? AND s_tokens IS NOT NULL GROUP BY mint").all(t - hours * 3600);
    const q = tdb.prepare("SELECT slot, ts, wallet, is_buy, sol, price_sol FROM trades WHERE mint=? AND ts>? ORDER BY slot, rowid LIMIT 8000");
    const insD = db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO farm_dumps (mint, name, entry_ts, dump_ts, slot0, slot1, drop_pct, sellers, sold_sol, built_ts) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)");
    const insW = db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO farm_wallets (wallet, mint, dump_ts, sold_sol, drop_pct) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)");
    const tx = db.transaction(() => {
      for (const m of mints) {
        const rows = q.all(m.mint, m.e); if (rows.length < 10) continue;
        const p0 = rows[0].price_sol; if (!(p0 > 0)) continue;
        const bySlot = new Map(); for (const r of rows) { const a = bySlot.get(r.slot) || []; a.push(r); bySlot.set(r.slot, a); }
        const slots = [...bySlot.keys()].sort((a, b) => a - b); let best = { drop: 0, s0: null, s1: null };
        for (let i = 0; i < slots.length; i++) {
          const before = i > 0 ? Math.max(...bySlot.get(slots[i - 1]).map((r) => r.price_sol)) : Math.max(...bySlot.get(slots[i]).map((r) => r.price_sol));
          let j = i; while (j + 1 < slots.length && slots[j + 1] - slots[i] <= 3) j++;
          let after = Infinity; for (let k = i; k <= j; k++) for (const r of bySlot.get(slots[k])) if (r.price_sol < after) after = r.price_sol;
          const drop = before > 0 ? 1 - after / before : 0; if (drop > best.drop) best = { drop, s0: slots[i], s1: slots[j] };
        }
        if (best.drop < 0.6) continue;
        const sells = rows.filter((r) => !r.is_buy && r.slot >= best.s0 && r.slot <= best.s1); if (!sells.length) continue;
        const dumpTs = sells[0].ts, byW = new Map(); for (const r of sells) byW.set(r.wallet, (byW.get(r.wallet) || 0) + r.sol);
        const name = db.prepare("SELECT name FROM vol_paper WHERE mint=? LIMIT 1").get(m.mint)?.name || null;
        insD.run(m.mint, name, m.e, dumpTs, best.s0, best.s1, +best.drop.toFixed(4), byW.size, +[...byW.values()].reduce((a, c) => a + c, 0).toFixed(3), t);
        for (const [w, sol] of byW) insW.run(w, m.mint, dumpTs, +sol.toFixed(4), +best.drop.toFixed(4));
        dumps++; wallets += byW.size;
      }
    }); tx();
    farmStat.builds++; farmStat.dumps = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) c FROM farm_dumps").get().c; farmStat.wallets = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT wallet) c FROM farm_wallets").get().c; farmStat.lastBuild = new Date().toISOString();
  } catch (e) { farmStat.lastErr = e.message.slice(0, 160); }
}

/** Farm scores for one check from its trade list (trades = [{user, isBuy, sol, tok, t}] up to the check time). Only dumps recorded BEFORE `asOf` count. */
export function farmScores(trades, asOf) {
  if (!db) return {};
  const buyers = new Map(), held = new Map(); let solAll = 0;
  for (const x of trades) { if (x.isBuy) { buyers.set(x.user, (buyers.get(x.user) || 0) + (x.sol || 0) / 1e9); solAll += (x.sol || 0) / 1e9; } held.set(x.user, (held.get(x.user) || 0) + (x.isBuy ? (x.tok || 0) : -(x.tok || 0))); }
  if (!buyers.size) return {};
  const q = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n, MAX(dump_ts) last FROM farm_wallets WHERE wallet=? AND dump_ts<?");
  let fb = 0, fb4 = 0, fsol = 0, fsol4 = 0, ftok = 0, ftok4 = 0, totHeld = 0;
  for (const [w, sol] of buyers) {
    const h = Math.max(0, held.get(w) || 0); totHeld += h;
    const r = q.get(w, asOf); if (!r || !r.n) continue;
    fb++; fsol += sol; ftok += h; if (r.last >= asOf - 4 * 3600) { fb4++; fsol4 += sol; ftok4 += h; }
  }
  const SUPPLY = 1e15;
  return { farm_buyers_pct: +(100 * fb / buyers.size).toFixed(1), farm_sol_pct: solAll ? +(100 * fsol / solAll).toFixed(1) : null, farm_held_pct: +(100 * ftok / SUPPLY).toFixed(2),
    farm4h_buyers_pct: +(100 * fb4 / buyers.size).toFixed(1), farm4h_sol_pct: solAll ? +(100 * fsol4 / solAll).toFixed(1) : null, farm4h_held_pct: +(100 * ftok4 / SUPPLY).toFixed(2), farm_known: farmStat.wallets };
}

/** Funder history as known now: launches this funder was seen behind, entered by the books, resolved, severe (< 0.35x), runners (≥ 1.2x), unresolved. */
export function funderHistory(funder, beforeTs) {
  if (!db || !funder) return null;
  try {
    const seen = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) c FROM fd_watch WHERE funder=? AND created_ts<?").get(funder, beforeTs).c;
    const ent = db.prepare("SELECT mint, MIN(entry_ts) e FROM vol_paper WHERE mint IN (SELECT mint FROM fd_watch WHERE funder=? AND created_ts<?) GROUP BY mint").all(funder, beforeTs);
    let resolved = 0, severe = 0, runners = 0;
    for (const m of ent) { const c = db.prepare("SELECT s_exit_x FROM chain_fill WHERE mint=? AND done=1 AND s_exit_x IS NOT NULL ORDER BY entry_ts LIMIT 1").get(m.mint); if (!c) continue; resolved++; if (c.s_exit_x < 0.35) severe++; else if (c.s_exit_x >= 1.2) runners++; }
    return { seen, entered: ent.length, resolved, severe, runners, unresolved: ent.length - resolved };
  } catch { return null; }
}

/** Per-check wallet state for the first-minute buyers (and anyone who bought ≥ 0.05 SOL), capped at 400 rows. */
export function recordWalletState(mint, at, trades, t0, earlySet, asOf) {
  if (!db) return;
  try {
    const per = new Map();
    for (const x of trades) { const p = per.get(x.user) || { b: 0, s: 0, si: 0, so: 0, first: null }; if (x.isBuy) { p.b += x.tok || 0; p.si += (x.sol || 0) / 1e9; if (p.first == null) p.first = x.t; } else { p.s += x.tok || 0; p.so += (x.sol || 0) / 1e9; } per.set(x.user, p); }
    const q = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n, MAX(dump_ts) last FROM farm_wallets WHERE wallet=? AND dump_ts<?");
    const ins = db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fd_wallet_state (mint, at, wallet, bought_tok, sold_tok, held_tok, held_pct, first_ts, sol_in, sol_out, early, farm, farm_4h) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)");
    const rows = [...per.entries()].filter(([w, p]) => p.si >= 0.05 || earlySet.has(w)).sort((a, b) => b[1].si - a[1].si).slice(0, 400);
    db.transaction(() => { for (const [w, p] of rows) { const held = Math.max(0, p.b - p.s), f = q.get(w, asOf); ins.run(mint, at, w, p.b, p.s, held, +(100 * held / 1e15).toFixed(3), p.first, +p.si.toFixed(4), +p.so.toFixed(4), earlySet.has(w) ? 1 : 0, f && f.n ? f.n : 0, f && f.n && f.last >= asOf - 4 * 3600 ? 1 : 0); } })();
  } catch (e) { farmStat.lastErr = ("wallet_state: " + e.message).slice(0, 160); }
}

export function recordDecision(mint, at, ts, ver, rule, passed, reason) {
  if (!db) return; try { db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO fd_decisions (mint, at, ts, ver, rule, passed, reason) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(mint, at, ts, ver, rule, passed ? 1 : 0, reason || null); } catch {}
}
