// launcher 0.7.0 (owner, 3 Nov 2026 10:16–10:22 UK: "find the early wallet buyers linked to the actual coin dev … trace it back to its source … the funder wallet …
// build up an excellent database of funder wallets … use Helius as much as you need"). THE OPERATOR DATABASE.
//
// What it does. For every studied coin (study_buyers: creator + first-100 buyers, with block-0 / sniper flags) it traces the wallets that matter —
// the creator, the creation-block (bundle) wallets and the first-3-second snipers — back through Helius's Wallet API:
//   funded-by  : the wallet's FIRST incoming SOL transfer (who, how much, when) with Helius's entity label when the sender is known (Centralized Exchange…)
//   transfers  : where a wallet's SOL went OUT after the coin (the profit trail, one hop)
// Each hop is cached for good (a wallet's first funding never changes). Private funders are followed one more hop (funder of the funder).
// Then it clusters: two coins share an operator when they share a funder (private, not a CEX / program / public wallet), a bundle wallet, or a
// payout destination. Operators are scored: launches, runners (peak ≥ $30K and ≥ $25K at 5 or 15 min), hit rate, median peak, CEX habit.
// Read-only. Nothing is bought. Tables: wallet_trace, wallet_out, operators, operator_coins. Exported as operators.csv, operator_coins.csv, wallet_trace.csv.
import { CFG } from "./config.mjs";
import { rpc, keysOf } from "./study.mjs"; // 0.7.6: cheap RPC reads for throwaway creator wallets

const n = (k, d) => (process.env[k] != null && process.env[k] !== "" && Number.isFinite(Number(process.env[k])) ? Number(process.env[k]) : d);
const HOPS = n("LAUNCH_OP_HOPS", 2), PER_RUN = n("LAUNCH_OP_PER_RUN", 400), OUT_HOP = n("LAUNCH_OP_OUT", 1);
export const opStat = { runs: 0, traced: 0, outs: 0, errors: 0, lastErr: "", lastRun: null, running: false, calls: 0, cex: 0, unknown404: 0, operators: 0, sample: null };
const nap = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
const API = process.env.LAUNCH_HELIUS_WALLET_API || "https://api.helius.xyz";
const key = () => CFG.HELIUS_KEY;

// Helius Wallet API (paid plan). 429 → back off; 404 → never funded by a SOL transfer (airdrop / program-created); 403 → plan.
async function wget(path, params = {}) {
  const q = new URLSearchParams({ ...params, "api-key": key() });
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    opStat.calls++;
    const r = await fetch(`${API}${path}?${q}`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(20000) });
    if (r.status === 429) { await nap(1500 * (i + 1)); continue; }
    if (r.status === 404) return null;
    const j = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!r.ok) { const e = new Error(`${path.split("/").pop()}: HTTP ${r.status} ${(j.error || j.message || "").toString().slice(0, 100)}`); e.http = r.status; throw e; }
    return j;
  }
  throw new Error("rate limited");
}

export function initOperators(db) {
  db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS wallet_trace (wallet TEXT PRIMARY KEY, funder TEXT, funder_name TEXT, funder_type TEXT, amount_sol REAL, funded_ts INTEGER, sig TEXT, hop INTEGER, err TEXT, checked_ts INTEGER);
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS wallet_out (wallet TEXT, mint TEXT, dest TEXT, dest_name TEXT, dest_type TEXT, amount_sol REAL, ts INTEGER, sig TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (wallet, sig));
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS operators (op_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, anchor TEXT UNIQUE, kind TEXT, coins INTEGER, runners INTEGER, hit_rate REAL, med_peak REAL, best_peak REAL, bundle_sol REAL, wallets INTEGER, cex_funded INTEGER, first_ts INTEGER, last_ts INTEGER, updated INTEGER);
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS operator_coins (anchor TEXT, mint TEXT, role TEXT, link TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (anchor, mint, link));
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS creator_trace (mint TEXT PRIMARY KEY, creator TEXT, create_ts INTEGER, peak_mc_usd REAL, graduated INTEGER, bundle_pct REAL, bundle_sol REAL, funder TEXT, funder_sol REAL, funded_ts INTEGER, creator_txs INTEGER, err TEXT, checked_ts INTEGER);
  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ct_funder ON creator_trace(funder); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ct_creator ON creator_trace(creator);
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS snipers (wallet TEXT PRIMARY KEY, coins INTEGER, census INTEGER, runners INTEGER, hit_rate REAL, buy_sol REAL, sell_sol REAL, block0 INTEGER, creator_linked INTEGER, funder TEXT, funder_name TEXT, txs INTEGER, first_ts INTEGER, last_ts INTEGER, updated INTEGER);
  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS wt_funder ON wallet_trace(funder);`);
  for (const c of ["launches INTEGER", "graduated_all INTEGER", "hits_all INTEGER", "hit_rate_all REAL"]) try { db.exec(`ALTER TABLE operators ADD COLUMN ${c}`); } catch {} // 0.7.6
}

/** funded-by for one wallet, cached forever. Returns the row. */
export async function trace(db, wallet, hop = 0) {
  const have = db ? db.prepare("SELECT * FROM wallet_trace WHERE wallet=?").get(wallet) : null;
  if (have && (!have.err || have.err === "404")) return have;
  let row = { wallet, funder: null, funder_name: null, funder_type: null, amount_sol: null, funded_ts: null, sig: null, hop, err: null, checked_ts: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) };
  try {
    const j = await wget(`/v1/wallet/${wallet}/funded-by`);
    if (!j) { row.err = "404"; opStat.unknown404++; }
    else { row.funder = j.funder || null; row.funder_name = j.funderName || null; row.funder_type = j.funderType || null; row.amount_sol = j.amount != null ? Number(j.amount) : null; row.funded_ts = j.timestamp || null; row.sig = j.signature || null;
      if (!opStat.sample) opStat.sample = JSON.stringify(j).slice(0, 400); if (row.funder_type) opStat.cex += /exchange/i.test(row.funder_type) ? 1 : 0; }
    opStat.traced++;
  } catch (e) { row.err = e.message.slice(0, 120); opStat.errors++; opStat.lastErr = `${wallet.slice(0, 6)} ${row.err}`; if (e.http === 403 || e.http === 401) throw e; }
  if (db) db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO wallet_trace VALUES (@wallet,@funder,@funder_name,@funder_type,@amount_sol,@funded_ts,@sig,@hop,@err,@checked_ts)").run(row);
  return row;
}
const isPublic = (r) => !!(r?.funder_type) || !r?.funder; // labelled entity (CEX, DeFi, market maker…) or none: not a private link

/** Outgoing SOL from a wallet after `sinceTs` (profit trail), one hop; cached per signature. */
export async function outflows(db, wallet, mint, sinceTs) {
  if (db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM wallet_out WHERE wallet=? AND mint=? LIMIT 1").get(wallet, mint)) return;
  try {
    const j = await wget(`/v1/wallet/${wallet}/transfers`, { limit: "100", type: "native" });
    const list = j?.transfers || j?.data || j?.items || (Array.isArray(j) ? j : []);
    const ins = db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO wallet_out VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)"); let k = 0;
    for (const t of list) {
      const ts = Number(t.timestamp || t.blockTime || 0), dir = String(t.direction || "").toLowerCase(), amt = Number(t.amount ?? t.uiAmount ?? 0);
      const dest = t.counterparty || t.to || t.destination || null;
      if (!dest || ts < sinceTs || !(dir === "out" || dir === "sent" || dir === "outgoing") || !(amt >= 0.05)) continue;
      ins.run(wallet, mint, dest, t.counterpartyName || null, t.counterpartyType || null, amt, ts, t.signature || `${wallet}:${ts}:${dest}`); k++;
    }
    if (!k) ins.run(wallet, mint, "", null, null, 0, sinceTs, `${wallet}:${mint}:none`); // mark as checked
    opStat.outs++;
  } catch (e) { opStat.errors++; opStat.lastErr = `out ${wallet.slice(0, 6)} ${e.message.slice(0, 100)}`; if (e.http === 403 || e.http === 401) throw e; }
}

/** 0.7.5: wallets that were in the creation block or the first 3 s of REPEAT_SNIPER or more studied coins are sniper bots, not an operator's identity
 *  (3 Nov 20:32 UK: 74 of 461 early wallets sat on 2+ of 43 census coins, the top ones on 14–15, buying 0.05–0.2 SOL each; they stitched 91 coins into one "operator"). */
const REPEAT_SNIPER = n("LAUNCH_OP_REPEAT_SNIPER", 3);
let repeatSet = new Set(), repeatAt = 0;
function repeatSnipers(db) {
  if (Date.now() - repeatAt < 60000) return repeatSet;
  const cnt = new Map();
  for (const r of db.prepare("SELECT wallet, data FROM study_buyers").all()) { let b; try { b = JSON.parse(r.data); } catch { continue; } if ((b.block0 || b.sniper) && !b.creatorLinked) cnt.set(r.wallet, (cnt.get(r.wallet) || 0) + 1); }
  repeatSet = new Set([...cnt].filter(([, c]) => c >= REPEAT_SNIPER).map(([w]) => w)); repeatAt = Date.now(); return repeatSet;
}

/** The wallets that matter for a coin: creator, block-0 buyers (bundle), first-3-s snipers. 0.7.5: a block-0 wallet that is a repeat sniper and not creator-linked is "sniper", not "bundle". */
function keyWallets(db, mint) {
  const c = db.prepare("SELECT data FROM study_coins WHERE mint=? AND err IS NULL").get(mint); if (!c) return null;
  let d; try { d = JSON.parse(c.data); } catch { return null; }
  const rep = repeatSnipers(db);
  const out = new Map(); if (d.creator) out.set(d.creator, "creator");
  for (const r of db.prepare("SELECT wallet, data FROM study_buyers WHERE mint=? ORDER BY rank").all(mint)) { let b; try { b = JSON.parse(r.data); } catch { continue; }
    if (b.block0 && (b.creatorLinked || !rep.has(r.wallet))) out.set(r.wallet, out.get(r.wallet) || "bundle"); else if (b.block0 || b.sniper) out.set(r.wallet, out.get(r.wallet) || "sniper"); }
  return { d, wallets: out };
}

/** 0.7.5: the sniper table — every wallet in the creation block / first 3 s of 2+ studied coins: coins, how many ran, SOL bought, SOL sold back inside the study window. (Studied coins are mostly winners, so this hit rate is an upper bound until the wallet's own history is read.) */
export function rebuildSnipers(db) {
  db.exec("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS snipers (wallet TEXT PRIMARY KEY, coins INTEGER, census INTEGER, runners INTEGER, hit_rate REAL, buy_sol REAL, sell_sol REAL, block0 INTEGER, creator_linked INTEGER, funder TEXT, funder_name TEXT, txs INTEGER, first_ts INTEGER, last_ts INTEGER, updated INTEGER)");
  const peak = new Map(db.prepare("SELECT mint, peak_mc FROM copy_peaks").all().map((r) => [r.mint, r.peak_mc || 0]));
  const role = new Map(db.prepare("SELECT mint, role, studied_ts FROM study_coins WHERE err IS NULL").all().map((r) => [r.mint, r]));
  const traceOf = new Map(db.prepare("SELECT wallet, funder, funder_name FROM wallet_trace").all().map((r) => [r.wallet, r]));
  const acc = new Map();
  for (const r of db.prepare("SELECT mint, wallet, data FROM study_buyers").all()) { let b; try { b = JSON.parse(r.data); } catch { continue; } if (!(b.block0 || b.sniper) || !role.has(r.mint)) continue;
    const a = acc.get(r.wallet) || { coins: 0, census: 0, runners: 0, buy: 0, sell: 0, block0: 0, linked: 0, txs: b.txs, ts: [] }; a.coins++; if (role.get(r.mint).role === "census") a.census++; if ((peak.get(r.mint) || 0) >= 100000) a.runners++;
    a.buy += b.buy_sol || 0; a.sell += b.sell_sol || 0; a.block0 += b.block0 ? 1 : 0; a.linked += b.creatorLinked ? 1 : 0; a.ts.push(role.get(r.mint).studied_ts || 0); acc.set(r.wallet, a); }
  db.exec("DELETE FROM snipers");
  const ins = db.prepare("INSERT INTO snipers VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)"); const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  for (const [w, a] of acc) if (a.coins >= 2) { const t = traceOf.get(w); ins.run(w, a.coins, a.census, a.runners, +(a.runners / a.coins).toFixed(2), +a.buy.toFixed(2), +a.sell.toFixed(2), a.block0, a.linked, t?.funder || null, t?.funder_name || null, a.txs, Math.min(...a.ts), Math.max(...a.ts), now); }
}

// ---------- 0.7.6: the denominator — every launch's creator, traced to its funder (owner, 3 Nov 21:03 UK: "how many coins they launch and how many are successful") ----------
// Creator wallets are throwaway (median age 0 s), so two 1-credit RPC calls find the funder: the wallet's first transaction and who paid SOL into it.
// The 100-credit wallet-API lookup is spent only on funders that stand behind 2+ creators (the second hop), cached forever in wallet_trace.
const CT_DAYS = n("LAUNCH_CT_DAYS", 14), CT_PER_RUN = n("LAUNCH_CT_PER_RUN", 3000) /* 0.7.7: 800 an hour meant a day for the 14-day pass; the RPC route is cheap */, CT_PARALLEL = n("LAUNCH_CT_PARALLEL", 4), CT_FUNDER_PER_RUN = n("LAUNCH_CT_FUNDERS", 60);
export const ctStat = { runs: 0, creators: 0, traced: 0, errors: 0, lastErr: "", funders: 0, queued: 0, running: false, lastRun: null };
export async function creatorFunder(w) {
  const recent = await rpc("getSignaturesForAddress", [w, { limit: 1000, commitment: "confirmed" }]); const txs = recent?.length ?? 0;
  if (!txs) return { txs, err: "no transactions" };
  if (txs >= 1000) { const fb = await trace(null, w); return { txs, funder: fb?.funder || null, funder_sol: fb?.amount_sol ?? null, funded_ts: fb?.funded_ts ?? null, err: fb?.err || null }; } // busy creator (rare): wallet API
  const first = recent[recent.length - 1];
  const t = await rpc("getTransaction", [first.signature, { maxSupportedTransactionVersion: 1, encoding: "json", commitment: "confirmed" }]);
  if (!t?.meta) return { txs, err: "first tx unreadable" };
  const keys = keysOf(t), pre = t.meta.preBalances, post = t.meta.postBalances, me = keys.indexOf(w); let best = -1, drop = 0;
  keys.forEach((k, i) => { if (i !== me && i < pre.length && pre[i] - post[i] > drop) { drop = pre[i] - post[i]; best = i; } });
  return { txs, funder: best >= 0 ? keys[best] : null, funder_sol: me >= 0 && me < pre.length ? (post[me] - pre[me]) / 1e9 : null, funded_ts: first.blockTime || null, err: null };
}
export async function creatorCensus(db, tdb, done) {
  if (ctStat.running || !tdb) return; ctStat.running = true; ctStat.runs++;
  try {
    const since = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - CT_DAYS * 86400;
    const todo = tdb.prepare("SELECT mint, creator, create_ts, peak_mc_usd, graduated, bundle_pct, bundle_sol FROM tokens WHERE create_ts>=? AND creator IS NOT NULL AND creator<>'' ORDER BY create_ts DESC LIMIT 20000").all(since)
      .filter((t) => !db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM creator_trace WHERE mint=?").get(t.mint));
    ctStat.queued = todo.length; const queue = todo.slice(0, CT_PER_RUN);
    const ins = db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO creator_trace VALUES (@mint,@creator,@create_ts,@peak_mc_usd,@graduated,@bundle_pct,@bundle_sol,@funder,@funder_sol,@funded_ts,@creator_txs,@err,@checked_ts)");
    const worker = async () => { for (let t; (t = queue.shift()); ) {
      const known = db.prepare("SELECT funder, funder_sol, funded_ts, creator_txs FROM creator_trace WHERE creator=? AND funder IS NOT NULL LIMIT 1").get(t.creator); // the same creator wallet used twice: no new calls
      let r; try { r = known ? { txs: known.creator_txs, funder: known.funder, funder_sol: known.funder_sol, funded_ts: known.funded_ts, err: null } : await creatorFunder(t.creator); ctStat.traced++; } catch (e) { r = { err: e.message.slice(0, 100) }; ctStat.errors++; ctStat.lastErr = `${t.creator.slice(0, 6)} ${r.err}`; }
      ins.run({ mint: t.mint, creator: t.creator, create_ts: t.create_ts, peak_mc_usd: t.peak_mc_usd ?? null, graduated: t.graduated ? 1 : 0, bundle_pct: t.bundle_pct ?? null, bundle_sol: t.bundle_sol ?? null, funder: r.funder || null, funder_sol: r.funder_sol ?? null, funded_ts: r.funded_ts ?? null, creator_txs: r.txs ?? null, err: r.err || null, checked_ts: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) });
      ctStat.creators++;
    } };
    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: CT_PARALLEL }, worker));
    // second hop, wallet API, only for funders behind 2+ creators and not yet traced
    const multi = db.prepare("SELECT funder, COUNT(*) c FROM creator_trace WHERE funder IS NOT NULL GROUP BY funder HAVING c>=2 ORDER BY c DESC").all().filter((f) => !db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM wallet_trace WHERE wallet=? AND (err IS NULL OR err='404')").get(f.funder)).slice(0, CT_FUNDER_PER_RUN);
    for (const f of multi) { if (!key()) break; await trace(db, f.funder, 1); ctStat.funders++; }
    ctStat.lastRun = new Date().toISOString();
  } catch (e) { ctStat.errors++; ctStat.lastErr = e.message.slice(0, 160); }
  finally { ctStat.running = false; try { done?.(); } catch {} }
}
/** Launch counts behind an anchor wallet, from creator_trace: creators it funded directly, or through one private hop. */
export function launchesBehind(db, anchor) {
  const direct = db.prepare("SELECT mint, peak_mc_usd, graduated FROM creator_trace WHERE funder=?").all(anchor);
  const hop = db.prepare("SELECT c.mint, c.peak_mc_usd, c.graduated FROM creator_trace c JOIN wallet_trace w ON w.wallet=c.funder WHERE w.funder=? AND w.funder_type IS NULL").all(anchor);
  const seen = new Map(); for (const r of [...direct, ...hop]) seen.set(r.mint, r);
  const all = [...seen.values()]; return { launches: all.length, graduated: all.filter((r) => r.graduated).length, hits: all.filter((r) => (r.peak_mc_usd || 0) >= 100000).length };
}

/** One run: trace every key wallet of every studied coin not yet traced (HOPS deep), outflows for bundle wallets of runners, then recluster. */
export async function runOperators(db, done) {
  if (opStat.running || !key()) return; opStat.running = true; opStat.runs++;
  try {
    const coins = db.prepare("SELECT s.mint, p.created_ts FROM study_coins s LEFT JOIN copy_peaks p ON p.mint=s.mint WHERE s.err IS NULL ORDER BY s.studied_ts DESC").all();
    let budget = PER_RUN;
    for (const c of coins) {
      if (budget <= 0) break;
      const kw = keyWallets(db, c.mint); if (!kw) continue;
      for (const [w, role] of kw.wallets) {
        if (budget <= 0) break;
        let cur = w;
        for (let h = 0; h < HOPS && cur; h++) {
          const had = db.prepare("SELECT funder, funder_type, err FROM wallet_trace WHERE wallet=?").get(cur);
          const r = had && (!had.err || had.err === "404") ? had : (budget--, await trace(db, cur, h));
          if (!r || isPublic(r)) break; cur = r.funder;
        }
        if (OUT_HOP && role === "bundle" && kw.d.peak_mc_usd >= 30000 && budget > 0) { budget--; await outflows(db, w, c.mint, (kw.d.t0 || c.created_ts || 0)); }
      }
    }
    recluster(db); rebuildSnipers(db);
    opStat.lastRun = new Date().toISOString();
  } catch (e) { opStat.errors++; opStat.lastErr = e.message.slice(0, 160); }
  finally { opStat.running = false; try { done?.(); } catch {} }
}

/** Cluster coins into operators by shared private funder (any hop), shared bundle wallet, or shared payout destination. Union-find over coins. */
export function recluster(db) {
  const coins = db.prepare("SELECT mint, data FROM study_coins WHERE err IS NULL").all().map((r) => { let d = {}; try { d = JSON.parse(r.data); } catch {} return { mint: r.mint, d }; }).filter((c) => c.d.venue === "pump.fun");
  const parent = new Map(); const find = (x) => { while (parent.get(x) !== x) { parent.set(x, parent.get(parent.get(x))); x = parent.get(x); } return x; }; const union = (a, b) => { a = find(a); b = find(b); if (a !== b) parent.set(a, b); };
  for (const c of coins) parent.set(c.mint, c.mint);
  const links = new Map(); // key -> {kind, mints:Set}
  const add = (keyS, kind, mint) => { if (!keyS) return; const k = kind + ":" + keyS; if (!links.has(k)) links.set(k, { kind, key: keyS, mints: new Set() }); links.get(k).mints.add(mint); };
  const traceOf = new Map(db.prepare("SELECT wallet, funder, funder_type, err FROM wallet_trace").all().map((r) => [r.wallet, r]));
  const outs = db.prepare("SELECT wallet, mint, dest, dest_type FROM wallet_out WHERE dest<>''").all();
  for (const c of coins) {
    const kw = keyWallets(db, c.mint); if (!kw) continue;
    for (const [w, role] of kw.wallets) {
      if (role === "sniper") continue; // 0.7.5: snipers are everyone's; only the creator and the bundle wallets say who made the coin
      if (role === "bundle" || role === "creator") add(w, role === "creator" ? "creator" : "bundle_wallet", c.mint); // the same bundle wallet in two coins = one operator
      let cur = w; for (let h = 0; h < HOPS; h++) { const t = traceOf.get(cur); if (!t || !t.funder || t.funder_type) break; add(t.funder, "funder", c.mint); cur = t.funder; }
    }
  }
  for (const o of outs) if (!o.dest_type) add(o.dest, "payout", o.mint);
  // 0.7.1: the launch fingerprint — the same bundle % and SOL to two decimals on two coins is the same software and money (GR.md, 23 Oct: 79.31% / 85.0 SOL)
  for (const c of coins) { const t = c.d.tracker; if (t && t.bundle_pct >= 20 && t.bundle_sol >= 5) add(`${(+t.bundle_pct).toFixed(2)}/${(+t.bundle_sol).toFixed(1)}`, "fingerprint", c.mint); }
  // a key shared by too many coins is a public hub (a bot service, a popular distributor), not one operator
  const MAX_SHARE = n("LAUNCH_OP_MAX_SHARE", 60), MAX_PAYOUT_SHARE = n("LAUNCH_OP_MAX_PAYOUT_SHARE", 4);
  // 0.7.5: a payout destination that receives from the bundle wallets of 5+ coins is shared plumbing (tip accounts, fee wallets, unlabelled exchanges), not a person
  const good = [...links.values()].filter((l) => l.mints.size >= 2 && l.mints.size <= (l.kind === "payout" ? MAX_PAYOUT_SHARE : MAX_SHARE) && l.kind !== "creator");
  const pairKinds = new Map(); // "a|b" -> set of distinct link KINDS joining a and b (0.7.5: two shared payouts count once; funder + bundle wallet count twice)
  for (const l of good) { const ms = [...l.mints]; for (let i = 0; i < ms.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < ms.length; j++) { const k = ms[i] < ms[j] ? ms[i] + "|" + ms[j] : ms[j] + "|" + ms[i]; if (!pairKinds.has(k)) pairKinds.set(k, new Set()); pairKinds.get(k).add(l.kind); } }
  const pairLinks = new Map([...pairKinds].map(([k, v]) => [k, v.size]));
  const size = () => { const sz = new Map(); for (const c of coins) { const r = find(c.mint); sz.set(r, (sz.get(r) || 0) + 1); } return sz; };
  // pass 1: pairs with 2+ independent links always merge; pass 2: single links merge only while both groups are small (< 8 coins) — one shared wallet must not glue two big operators together
  for (const [k, cnt] of pairLinks) if (cnt >= 2) { const [a, b] = k.split("|"); union(a, b); }
  const sz = size();
  for (const [k, cnt] of pairLinks) if (cnt === 1) { const [a, b] = k.split("|"); const ra = find(a), rb = find(b); if (ra !== rb && (sz.get(ra) || 1) < 8 && (sz.get(rb) || 1) < 8) { union(a, b); const m = (sz.get(ra) || 1) + (sz.get(rb) || 1); sz.set(find(a), m); } }
  // build operators
  const groups = new Map(); for (const c of coins) { const r = find(c.mint); if (!groups.has(r)) groups.set(r, []); groups.get(r).push(c); }
  db.exec("DELETE FROM operators; DELETE FROM operator_coins");
  const insO = db.prepare("INSERT INTO operators (anchor, kind, coins, runners, hit_rate, med_peak, best_peak, bundle_sol, wallets, cex_funded, first_ts, last_ts, updated) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)");
  const insC = db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO operator_coins VALUES (?,?,?,?)");
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000); let nOps = 0;
  const peakRow = (m) => db.prepare("SELECT peak_mc, peak_close_mc, mc5, mc15, created_ts FROM copy_peaks WHERE mint=?").get(m);
  for (const [root, cs] of groups) {
    if (cs.length < 2) continue; // a lone coin is not (yet) an operator
    const myLinks = good.filter((l) => [...l.mints].some((m) => find(m) === root));
    const funders = myLinks.filter((l) => l.kind === "funder").sort((a, b) => b.mints.size - a.mints.size);
    const anchor = (funders[0] || myLinks.sort((a, b) => b.mints.size - a.mints.size)[0])?.key || root, kind = funders[0] ? "funder" : (myLinks[0]?.kind || "coin");
    const peaks = [], run = []; let bsol = [], cex = 0, wallets = new Set(), ts = [];
    for (const c of cs) {
      const p = peakRow(c.mint); const pk = Math.min(p?.peak_mc ?? c.d.peak_mc_usd ?? 0, (p?.peak_close_mc || Infinity) * 1.5) || 0; peaks.push(pk); const isRun = pk >= 30000 && Math.max(p?.mc5 || 0, p?.mc15 || 0, c.d.tracker ? pk : 0) >= 25000; run.push(isRun ? 1 : 0);
      if (c.d.block0_sol) bsol.push(c.d.block0_sol); ts.push(p?.created_ts || 0);
      const kw = keyWallets(db, c.mint); if (kw) for (const [w, role] of kw.wallets) { if (role !== "sniper") wallets.add(w); const t = traceOf.get(w); if (t?.funder_type && /exchange/i.test(t.funder_type)) cex++; }
      for (const l of myLinks) if (l.mints.has(c.mint)) insC.run(anchor, c.mint, c.d.role || "", `${l.kind}:${l.key}`);
    }
    const sorted = [...peaks].sort((a, b) => a - b), med = sorted[Math.floor(sorted.length / 2)] || 0;
    insO.run(anchor, kind, cs.length, run.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), +(run.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / cs.length).toFixed(2), Math.round(med), Math.round(Math.max(...peaks)), bsol.length ? +(bsol.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / bsol.length).toFixed(1) : null, wallets.size, cex, Math.min(...ts.filter(Boolean)) || null, Math.max(...ts) || null, now);
    if (kind === "funder") { const lb = launchesBehind(db, anchor); if (lb.launches) db.prepare("UPDATE operators SET launches=?, graduated_all=?, hits_all=?, hit_rate_all=? WHERE anchor=?").run(lb.launches, lb.graduated, lb.hits, +(lb.hits / lb.launches).toFixed(2), anchor); } // 0.7.6: the true denominator, duds included
    nOps++;
  }
  opStat.operators = nOps;
}

// launcher 0.3.0 (owner, 31 Oct 2026 18:21 UK: "study the copycats … what made them run up? bundled wallets, wash trading, sniping, fresh wallets, or genuine traders?").
// For every copycat that ran (peak ≥ STUDY_MIN_PEAK, from copy_peaks) and, as a comparison, the copies of the same post that did not run and our own coin,
// it reads the coin's whole first 40 minutes from the chain (Helius: every buy and sell on the pump.fun bonding curve, in order, with block and position)
// and profiles the first 100 buyers (wallet age, activity, who funded them). It measures, for each coin:
//   the creator (own first buy, when it sold), bundles (buyers in the creation block, Jito-tipped and multi-buy transactions), snipers (first 3 s),
//   fresh wallets, wallets funded by the same source (clusters) and by the creator, wash trading (wallets buying and selling back and forth),
//   whales, how concentrated the buying was, the 5-minute volume, and how much of the buying came from "organic" traders (none of the above).
// Read-only: no transaction is signed or sent. Helius credits are counted (getTransactionsForAddress 10 per 100 transactions; getTransaction 1).
import bs58 from "bs58";
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { PublicKey } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { CFG } from "./config.mjs";
import { gget, N } from "./gmgn.mjs";
import { curveOf, PUMP } from "./pump.mjs";
import { one as peakOne } from "./copypeaks.mjs";
import { trace as fundedBy } from "./operators.mjs"; // 0.7.4: the fast wallet-API funder for busy wallets

const n = (k, d) => (process.env[k] != null && process.env[k] !== "" && Number.isFinite(Number(process.env[k])) ? Number(process.env[k]) : d);
const MIN_PEAK = n("LAUNCH_STUDY_MIN_PEAK", 30000), WINDOW = n("LAUNCH_STUDY_WINDOW_MIN", 40) * 60, BUYERS = n("LAUNCH_STUDY_BUYERS", 100);
const PER_RUN = n("LAUNCH_STUDY_PER_RUN", 400) /* 0.7.7: was 30 an hour — with coins at ~80 s each the hourly cap was the bottleneck */, DAY_CREDITS = n("LAUNCH_STUDY_DAY_CREDITS", 250000), MAX_TX = n("LAUNCH_STUDY_MAX_TX", 60000), CENSUS_MAX_TX = n("LAUNCH_STUDY_CENSUS_MAX_TX", 5000), PARALLEL = n("LAUNCH_STUDY_PARALLEL", 3); // 0.6.2: busy runners do 15,000+ transactions in their first minutes; 0.7.3: census coins stop at 5,000
const STUDY_V = 7; // bump to redo every coin with a newer method
export const studyStat = { runs: 0, studied: 0, errors: 0, lastErr: "", lastRun: null, running: false, credits: 0, creditsDay: "", creditsToday: 0, calls: 0, gtfa: null, probe: null, current: "" };

const disc = (s) => createHash("sha256").update(s).digest().subarray(0, 8);
const D_TRADE = disc("event:TradeEvent"), D_CREATE = disc("event:CreateEvent"), D_COMPLETE = disc("event:CompleteEvent"), EVT_TAG = disc("anchor:event");
const PUMP_S = PUMP.toBase58();
const JITO = new Set(["96gYZGLnJYVFmbjzopPSU6QiEV5fGqZNyN9nmNhvrZU5", "HFqU5x63VTqvQss8hp11i4wVV8bD44PvwucfZ2bU7gRe", "Cw8CFyM9FkoMi7K7Crf6HNQqf4uEMzpKw6QNghXLvLkY", "ADaUMid9yfUytqMBgopwjb2DTLSokTSzL1zt6iGPaS49",

const nap = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
const today = () => new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

// ---------- Helius ----------
function spend(c) { if (studyStat.creditsDay !== today()) { studyStat.creditsDay = today(); studyStat.creditsToday = 0; } studyStat.credits += c; studyStat.creditsToday += c; }
export async function rpc(method, params, credits = 1) {
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    studyStat.calls++;
    try {
      const r = await fetch(CFG.RPC_URL, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: 1, method, params }), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30000) });
      if (r.status === 429) { await nap(1500 * (i + 1)); continue; }
      const j = await r.json();
      if (j.error) { if (/rate|429|too many/i.test(j.error.message || "")) { await nap(1500 * (i + 1)); continue; } const e = new Error(`${method}: ${j.error.code} ${(j.error.message || "").slice(0, 120)}`); e.rpc = j.error; throw e; }
      spend(typeof credits === "function" ? credits(j.result) : credits);
      return j.result;
    } catch (e) { if (e.rpc || i === 4) throw e; await nap(1000 * (i + 1)); }
  }
  throw new Error(`${method}: rate limited`);
}
const fullCost = (res) => Math.max(10, Math.ceil(((res?.data || []).length) / 100) * 10);

/** Every transaction touching `addr` from its first one, oldest first, until blockTime > tEnd (or MAX_TX). Helius getTransactionsForAddress, else signatures + getTransaction. */
// 0.6.1: each transaction is turned into a small record (its trades for `mint`, create/complete, signer, Jito) the moment its page arrives,
// so a busy coin no longer holds thousands of full transactions in memory (the launcher was running out of its 256 MB and restarting).
function compact(t, mint) {
  if (!studyStat.probeDone) { studyStat.probeDone = true; studyStat.probe = JSON.stringify({ keys: Object.keys(t), ix: t.transactionIndex, ev: events(t).map((e) => e.k).join("+"), inner: (t.meta?.innerInstructions || []).length, logs: (t.meta?.logMessages || []).length }).slice(0, 400); }
  const err = !!t.meta?.err, keys = keysOf(t), ev = err ? [] : events(t);
  let tr = [], generic = false;
  if (!err) { tr = fillZero(t, mint, ev.filter((e) => e.k === "trade" && e.mint === mint).map((e) => ({ ...e, mc: (e.vSol / e.vTok) * 1e6 }))); if (!tr.length) { tr = balTrades(t, mint); generic = tr.length > 0; } }
  return { slot: t.slot, transactionIndex: t.transactionIndex ?? null, blockTime: t.blockTime, err, sig: sigOf(t), keys0: keys[0], jito: keys.some((k) => JITO.has(k)),
    cr: ev.find((e) => e.k === "create" && e.mint === mint) || null, complete: ev.some((e) => e.k === "complete"), pump: tr.some((e) => e.vSol != null), tr, generic };
}
async function history(addr, tEnd, mint, maxTx = MAX_TX) {
  const out = [];
  if (studyStat.gtfa !== false) {
    try {
      let token = null;
      for (let page = 0; page < maxTx / 100; page++) {
        const res = await rpc("getTransactionsForAddress", [addr, { transactionDetails: "full", sortOrder: "asc", limit: 100, maxSupportedTransactionVersion: 1, encoding: "json", commitment: "confirmed", filters: { status: "succeeded", blockTime: { lte: tEnd } }, ...(token ? { paginationToken: token } : {}) }], fullCost); // 0.6.3: failed transactions (bot spam on hot coins) are not fetched
        studyStat.gtfa = true;
        const rows = res?.data || []; let past = false;
        for (const t of rows) { if (t.blockTime && t.blockTime > tEnd) { past = true; break; } out.push(compact(t, mint)); }
        token = res?.paginationToken; if (past || !token || !rows.length) break;
      }
      return out;
    } catch (e) { if (studyStat.gtfa === true || !e.rpc) throw e; studyStat.gtfa = false; studyStat.probe = "getTransactionsForAddress unavailable: " + e.message.slice(0, 140); out.length = 0; }
  }
  // fallback: newest → oldest signatures, keep those inside the window, then fetch each transaction
  let before = null, sigs = [];
  for (let page = 0; page < 40; page++) {
    const s = await rpc("getSignaturesForAddress", [addr, { limit: 1000, commitment: "confirmed", ...(before ? { before } : {}) }]);
    if (!s?.length) break; sigs.push(...s); before = s[s.length - 1].signature; if (s.length < 1000) break;
  }
  sigs = sigs.reverse(); const t0 = sigs[0]?.blockTime || 0; sigs = sigs.filter((s) => !s.blockTime || s.blockTime <= Math.max(tEnd, t0)).slice(0, maxTx);
  for (let i = 0; i < sigs.length; i += 8) {
    const got = await Promise.all(sigs.slice(i, i + 8).map((s) => rpc("getTransaction", [s.signature, { maxSupportedTransactionVersion: 1, encoding: "json", commitment: "confirmed" }]).catch(() => null)));
    got.forEach((t, k) => t && out.push(compact({ ...t, transactionIndex: null, _sig: sigs[i + k].signature }, mint)));
  }
  return out;
}

// ---------- decoding ----------
export const keysOf = (t) => [...(t.transaction?.message?.accountKeys || []).map((k) => (typeof k === "string" ? k : k.pubkey)), ...(t.meta?.loadedAddresses?.writable || []), ...(t.meta?.loadedAddresses?.readonly || [])];
const pk = (b, o) => bs58.encode(b.subarray(o, o + 32));
function str(b, o) { const l = b.readUInt32LE(o); return [b.subarray(o + 4, o + 4 + l).toString("utf8"), o + 4 + l]; }
export const dbg = { samples: [], programs: {} };
function events(t) {
  const keys = keysOf(t), out = [], raws = [];
  if (dbg.on) for (const ix of t.transaction?.message?.instructions || []) { const p = keys[ix.programIdIndex]; if (p) dbg.programs[p] = (dbg.programs[p] || 0) + 1; }
  for (const g of t.meta?.innerInstructions || []) for (const ix of g.instructions || []) {
    if (keys[ix.programIdIndex] !== PUMP_S || !ix.data) continue;
    let b; try { b = Buffer.from(bs58.decode(ix.data)); } catch { continue; }
    if (b.length > 16 && b.subarray(0, 8).equals(EVT_TAG)) raws.push(b.subarray(8));
  }
  for (const l of t.meta?.logMessages || []) if (l.startsWith("Program data: ")) { try { raws.push(Buffer.from(l.slice(14), "base64")); } catch {} }
  for (const b of raws) {
    if (dbg.on && dbg.samples.length < 4 && !dbg.seen.has(b.subarray(0, 8).toString("hex") + b.length)) { dbg.seen.add(b.subarray(0, 8).toString("hex") + b.length); dbg.samples.push({ len: b.length, hex: b.subarray(0, 260).toString("hex") }); }
    try {
      const d = b.subarray(0, 8), p = b.subarray(8);
      if (d.equals(D_TRADE) && p.length >= 121) out.push({ k: "trade", mint: pk(p, 0), sol: Number(p.readBigUInt64LE(32)), tok: Number(p.readBigUInt64LE(40)), isBuy: p[48] === 1, user: pk(p, 49), ts: Number(p.readBigInt64LE(81)), vSol: Number(p.readBigUInt64LE(89)), vTok: Number(p.readBigUInt64LE(97)), rTok: Number(p.readBigUInt64LE(113)) });
      else if (d.equals(D_CREATE)) { let o = 0, name, symbol, uri; [name, o] = str(p, o); [symbol, o] = str(p, o); [uri, o] = str(p, o); out.push({ k: "create", name, symbol, mint: pk(p, o), curve: pk(p, o + 32), user: pk(p, o + 64), creator: p.length >= o + 128 ? pk(p, o + 96) : null }); }
      else if (d.equals(D_COMPLETE)) out.push({ k: "complete" });
    } catch {}
  }
  return out;
}
const sigOf = (t) => t._sig || t.transaction?.signatures?.[0];
const WSOL_S = "So11111111111111111111111111111111111111112";
/** 0.4.1: trades read from balance changes — works on any launchpad or pool (Meteora DBC / DAMM, PumpSwap, Raydium…), used where no pump.fun event is found.
 *  A trade = a signer whose balance of the coin changed, with SOL (native + wrapped) moving the other way by more than 0.0005 SOL. Market cap assumes 1B supply. */
function balTrades(t, mint) {
  const keys = keysOf(t), nSig = t.transaction?.message?.header?.numRequiredSignatures || 1, signers = new Set(keys.slice(0, nSig)), m = t.meta || {};
  const tok = new Map(), ws = new Map(); let dec = 6;
  const add = (map, owner, v) => map.set(owner, (map.get(owner) || 0) + v);
  const pre = new Map((m.preTokenBalances || []).map((b) => [b.accountIndex, b])), post = new Map((m.postTokenBalances || []).map((b) => [b.accountIndex, b]));
  for (const i of new Set([...pre.keys(), ...post.keys()])) { const a = pre.get(i), b = post.get(i), x = b || a; if (!x?.owner) continue;
    const d = Number(b?.uiTokenAmount?.amount || 0) - Number(a?.uiTokenAmount?.amount || 0); if (!d) continue;
    if (x.mint === mint) { add(tok, x.owner, d); dec = x.uiTokenAmount?.decimals ?? dec; } else if (x.mint === WSOL_S) add(ws, x.owner, d); }
  const out = [];
  for (const [owner, d] of tok) { if (!signers.has(owner)) continue; const i = keys.indexOf(owner); if (i < 0 || i >= (m.preBalances || []).length) continue;
    let lam = m.postBalances[i] - m.preBalances[i] + (ws.get(owner) || 0); if (i === 0) lam += m.fee || 0;
    const isBuy = d > 0, sol = isBuy ? -lam : lam; if (!(sol > 500000)) continue;
    const ui = Math.abs(d) / 10 ** dec; out.push({ k: "trade", mint, sol, tok: Math.abs(d), isBuy, user: owner, mc: (sol / 1e9 / ui) * 1e9 }); }
  return out;
}

/** 0.5.0: a coin's trades from its first transaction up to tEnd (any launchpad), cheap form for the runner-follow test. */
export async function earlyTrades(mint, tEnd) {
  // 0.6.1: read the coin's own address, not the bonding curve — a coin that graduates in under 2 min keeps trading on PumpSwap, which the curve never sees
  const txs = await history(mint, tEnd, mint, n("LAUNCH_RF_MAX_TX", 8000));
  txs.sort((a, b) => a.slot - b.slot || (a.transactionIndex ?? 0) - (b.transactionIndex ?? 0));
  const trades = []; let t0 = null, complete = false, pump = false;
  for (const t of txs) {
    if (t.err) continue; if (t0 == null) t0 = t.blockTime;
    if (t.complete) complete = true; if (t.pump || t.cr) pump = true;
    t.tr.forEach((e) => trades.push({ t: t.blockTime, user: e.user, isBuy: e.isBuy, sol: e.sol, mc: e.mc, tok: e.tok, slot: t.slot })); // 0.8.9: token amount, for the "still holding" count; 0.8.11: slot, for the create-block buyers
  }
  return { t0, trades, txs: txs.length, pumpCurve: pump, complete };
}

/** 0.5.1: some pump.fun coins (serial launchers; curve quoted in another token) report sol_amount = 0 and empty SOL reserves in their TradeEvent.
 *  For those trades the SOL the trader actually paid or received is taken from the transaction's balance changes (same wallet); flagged q0. */
function fillZero(t, mint, tr) {
  if (!tr.some((e) => !e.sol)) return tr;
  const bt = balTrades(t, mint);
  return tr.map((e) => { if (e.sol) return e; const b = bt.find((x) => x.user === e.user && x.isBuy === e.isBuy); return b ? { ...e, sol: b.sol, mc: b.mc, q0: 1 } : { ...e, q0: 1 }; });
}

// ---------- wallets ----------
const walletCache = new Map();
async function walletProfile(db, w) {
  if (walletCache.has(w)) return walletCache.get(w);
  const row = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM study_wallets WHERE wallet=?").get(w);
  if (row && row.checked_ts > Date.now() / 1000 - 3 * 86400) { walletCache.set(w, row); return row; }
  let first = null, txs = null, funder = null, funded_sol = null, err = null;
  try {
    const recent = await rpc("getSignaturesForAddress", [w, { limit: 1000, commitment: "confirmed" }]); txs = recent?.length ?? 0;
    if (txs < 1000 && txs > 0) first = recent[recent.length - 1];
    else if (txs >= 1000) { // 0.7.4: a wallet with 1,000+ transactions (trading bot) — the "first transaction" scan on it was the slow call (timeouts + retries, ~20 min a coin); Helius's funded-by index answers at once with the first SOL transfer in
      const fb = await fundedBy(db, w); if (fb && !fb.err) { funder = fb.funder; funded_sol = fb.amount_sol; first = fb.funded_ts ? { blockTime: fb.funded_ts } : null; } else if (fb?.err === "404") first = null; }
    if (first && !funder) {
      const t = await rpc("getTransaction", [first.signature, { maxSupportedTransactionVersion: 1, encoding: "json", commitment: "confirmed" }]);
      if (t?.meta) { const keys = keysOf(t), pre = t.meta.preBalances, post = t.meta.postBalances, me = keys.indexOf(w);
        let best = -1, drop = 0; keys.forEach((k, i) => { if (i !== me && i < pre.length && pre[i] - post[i] > drop) { drop = pre[i] - post[i]; best = i; } });
        funder = best >= 0 ? keys[best] : null; funded_sol = me >= 0 && me < pre.length ? (post[me] - pre[me]) / 1e9 : null; }
    }
  } catch (e) { err = e.message.slice(0, 120); }
  const p = { wallet: w, first_ts: first?.blockTime ?? null, txs, funder, funded_sol, err, checked_ts: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) };
  db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO study_wallets VALUES (@wallet,@first_ts,@txs,@funder,@funded_sol,@err,@checked_ts)").run(p);
  walletCache.set(w, p); return p;
}

export function initStudy(db) {
  db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS study_wallets (wallet TEXT PRIMARY KEY, first_ts INTEGER, txs INTEGER, funder TEXT, funded_sol REAL, err TEXT, checked_ts INTEGER);
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS study_coins (mint TEXT PRIMARY KEY, launch_id INTEGER, role TEXT, studied_ts INTEGER, data TEXT, err TEXT);
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS study_buyers (mint TEXT, wallet TEXT, rank INTEGER, data TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (mint, wallet));`);
}

// ---------- one coin ----------
export async function studyCoin(db, c, solUsd) {
  const curve = curveOf(new PublicKey(c.mint)).toBase58();
  const tStart = Date.now(); // 0.7.4: phase timing (ms) → studyStat.timing and the row
  let gmgn = null; try { const i = await gget("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol", address: c.mint }), st = i?.stat || {}, w = i?.wallet_tags_stat || {}, dv = i?.dev || {}, P = (x) => (N(x) == null ? null : +(N(x) * 100).toFixed(1));
    gmgn = { launchpad: i?.launchpad ?? null, platform: i?.launchpad_platform ?? null, status: i?.launchpad_status ?? null, top10: P(st.top_10_holder_rate), bundler: P(st.top_bundler_trader_percentage), insider: P(st.top_rat_trader_percentage), fresh: P(st.fresh_wallet_rate),
      bots: P(st.bot_degen_rate), sniperHold: P(st.top70_sniper_hold_rate), smart: N(w.smart_wallets), kol: N(w.renowned_wallets), snipers: N(w.sniper_wallets), bundlers: N(w.bundler_wallets), freshW: N(w.fresh_wallets), ratW: N(w.rat_trader_wallets),
      devLaunches: N(dv.creator_open_count ?? st.creator_created_count), devStatus: dv.creator_token_status ?? null, twitter: i?.link?.twitter_username ?? i?.link?.twitter ?? null, website: i?.link?.website ?? null, telegram: i?.link?.telegram ?? null, dexPaid: dv.dexscr_ad ?? null }; } catch (e) { gmgn = { err: e.message.slice(0, 80) }; }
  dbg.on = true; dbg.samples = []; dbg.programs = {}; dbg.seen = new Set();
  // 0.7.3: census coins (the operator database) need the creation block, the snipers and the first 100 buyers, all inside the first minute even on the
  // busiest coin (~100 transactions a second). Reading their whole first 40 minutes cost 600 pages / 6,000 credits / 20-40 minutes a coin (2 coins an hour);
  // CENSUS_MAX_TX pages are enough for the wallets and cost 1/12 of that. The 40-minute figures (vol5, wash) are then partial: covered_s says how much was read.
  const cap = c.role === "census" ? CENSUS_MAX_TX : MAX_TX, tm = { gmgn: Date.now() - tStart, pages: 0, wallets: 0 }; let tP = Date.now();
  const txs = await history(c.mint, c.created_ts + WINDOW, c.mint, cap); tm.pages = Date.now() - tP; tP = Date.now(); // 0.6.1: the coin's own address — curve, PumpSwap and any other pool
  if (!txs.length) throw new Error("no transactions found");
  // trades in chain order
  const trades = []; let created = null, t0 = null, slot0 = null, complete = null, generic = 0, creatorGuess = null;
  txs.sort((a, b) => a.slot - b.slot || (a.transactionIndex ?? 0) - (b.transactionIndex ?? 0));
  for (const t of txs) {
    if (t.err) continue;
    if (t.cr && !created) { created = t.cr; t0 = t.blockTime; slot0 = t.slot; }
    if (t.complete && !complete) complete = t.blockTime;
    const tr = t.tr; if (t.generic) generic++;
    if (t0 == null && !created) { t0 = t.blockTime; slot0 = t.slot; creatorGuess = t.keys0; }
    tr.forEach((e) => trades.push({ ...e, t: t.blockTime, slot: t.slot, idx: t.transactionIndex, sig: t.sig, payer: t.keys0, jito: t.jito, inTx: tr.length }));
  }
  if (t0 == null) { t0 = txs[0].blockTime; slot0 = txs[0].slot; }
  const venue = created ? "pump.fun" : generic ? "other (balance changes)" : "none";
  const creator = created?.creator || created?.user || creatorGuess || trades[0]?.user || null;
  const rel = (x) => x.t - t0, W = (s) => trades.filter((x) => rel(x) <= s);
  const sum = (a, f) => a.reduce((s, x) => s + f(x), 0), uniq = (a) => new Set(a).size, sol = (l) => l / 1e9;
  const mcSol = (x) => x.mc; // market cap in SOL (pump.fun: from the curve; others: the trade's own price × 1B supply)
  // per-wallet tallies within the window
  const per = new Map();
  for (const x of trades) { const p = per.get(x.user) || { w: x.user, buys: 0, sells: 0, bsol: 0, ssol: 0, btok: 0, stok: 0, first: null, firstSell: null, slot: null, idx: null, jito: false, multi: false };
    if (x.isBuy) { p.buys++; p.bsol += x.sol; p.btok += x.tok; if (p.first == null) { p.first = rel(x); p.slot = x.slot - slot0; p.idx = x.idx; p.jito = x.jito; p.multi = x.inTx > 1; } } else { p.sells++; p.ssol += x.sol; p.stok += x.tok; if (p.firstSell == null) p.firstSell = rel(x); }
    per.set(x.user, p); }
  const dev = per.get(creator) || { buys: 0, sells: 0, bsol: 0, ssol: 0 };
  const buyers = [...per.values()].filter((p) => p.buys > 0 && p.w !== creator).sort((a, b) => a.first - b.first || (a.slot - b.slot) || ((a.idx ?? 0) - (b.idx ?? 0)));
  const block0 = buyers.filter((p) => p.slot === 0), snipe = buyers.filter((p) => p.slot > 0 && p.first <= 3);
  // early insiders' selling: tokens bought by creation-block buyers and snipers that were sold within 15 min
  const early = new Set([...block0, ...snipe].map((p) => p.w)), eBought = sum(trades.filter((x) => x.isBuy && early.has(x.user)), (x) => x.tok), eSold15 = sum(trades.filter((x) => !x.isBuy && early.has(x.user) && rel(x) <= 900), (x) => x.tok);
  const devBought = sum(trades.filter((x) => x.isBuy && x.user === creator), (x) => x.tok), devSold = sum(trades.filter((x) => !x.isBuy && x.user === creator), (x) => x.tok);
  // profiles of the first BUYERS buyers (+ the creator)
  const prof = new Map(); const look = [creator, ...buyers.slice(0, BUYERS).map((p) => p.w)].filter(Boolean);
  for (let i = 0; i < look.length; i += 6) { const got = await Promise.all(look.slice(i, i + 6).map((w) => walletProfile(db, w))); got.forEach((g) => prof.set(g.wallet, g)); }
  tm.wallets = Date.now() - tP; tm.total = Date.now() - tStart; tm.heavy = look.filter((w) => (prof.get(w)?.txs ?? 0) >= 1000).length; studyStat.timing = { coin: c.mint.slice(0, 6), ...tm };
  const cp = prof.get(creator) || {};
  const fGroups = new Map(); for (const p of buyers.slice(0, BUYERS)) { const f = prof.get(p.w)?.funder; if (f) fGroups.set(f, (fGroups.get(f) || 0) + 1); }
  const linked = (w) => { const f = prof.get(w)?.funder; return !!f && (f === creator || (cp.funder && f === cp.funder) || f === cp.funder); };
  const ageH = (p) => { const f = prof.get(p.w)?.first_ts; return f ? (t0 + p.first - f) / 3600 : null; };
  const tag = (p, rank) => {
    const pr = prof.get(p.w) || {}, a = ageH(p), grp = pr.funder ? fGroups.get(pr.funder) || 1 : 1;
    const f = { block0: p.slot === 0, sniper: p.slot > 0 && p.first <= 3, jito: p.jito, multi: p.multi, fresh1h: a != null && a < 1, fresh24h: a != null && a < 24, lowAct: pr.txs != null && pr.txs <= 20, cluster: grp >= 3, creatorLinked: linked(p.w), wash: p.buys >= 2 && p.sells >= 2 };
    f.organic = rank < BUYERS && !f.block0 && !f.sniper && !f.fresh24h && !f.cluster && !f.creatorLinked && !f.multi && !f.wash && pr.txs != null;
    return { ...f, ageH: a == null ? null : +a.toFixed(2), txs: pr.txs, funder: pr.funder, funderGroup: grp, fundedSol: pr.funded_sol };
  };
  const tagged = buyers.map((p, i) => ({ p, f: i < BUYERS ? tag(p, i) : null }));
  const first = tagged.filter((x) => x.f), share = (pred) => (first.length ? +(first.filter((x) => pred(x.f)).length / first.length * 100).toFixed(1) : null);
  const solShare = (pred) => { const tot = sum(first, (x) => x.p.bsol); return tot ? +(sum(first.filter((x) => pred(x.f)), (x) => x.p.bsol) / tot * 100).toFixed(1) : null; };
  // wash: wallets buying and selling back and forth (≥ 2 of each in 15 min), their share of the volume, and the round-trip share of all volume
  const w15 = new Map(); for (const x of W(900)) { const p = w15.get(x.user) || { b: 0, s: 0, bs: 0, ss: 0 }; if (x.isBuy) { p.b++; p.bs += x.sol; } else { p.s++; p.ss += x.sol; } w15.set(x.user, p); }
  const vol15 = sum(W(900), (x) => x.sol), washW = [...w15.values()].filter((p) => p.b >= 2 && p.s >= 2);
  const roundTrip = sum([...w15.values()], (p) => 2 * Math.min(p.bs, p.ss));
  // 5-minute buckets (SOL volume, buys, sells, new buyers) for the first 40 min
  const buckets = []; const seenB = new Set();
  for (let b = 0; b < WINDOW / 300; b++) { const xs = trades.filter((x) => rel(x) >= b * 300 && rel(x) < (b + 1) * 300); const nb = xs.filter((x) => x.isBuy && !seenB.has(x.user) && (seenB.add(x.user) || true)).length;
    buckets.push({ v: +sol(sum(xs, (x) => x.sol)).toFixed(2), b: xs.filter((x) => x.isBuy).length, s: xs.filter((x) => !x.isBuy).length, nb, mc: xs.length ? +mcSol(xs[xs.length - 1]).toFixed(1) : null }); }
  const mcAt = (s) => { const xs = W(s); return xs.length ? +mcSol(xs[xs.length - 1]).toFixed(1) : null; };
  const peakT = trades.reduce((m, x) => (!m || mcSol(x) > mcSol(m) ? x : m), null);
  const buys15 = W(900).filter((x) => x.isBuy && x.user !== creator), sizes = buys15.map((x) => sol(x.sol)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const byW = new Map(); buys15.forEach((x) => byW.set(x.user, (byW.get(x.user) || 0) + x.sol)); const top10 = [...byW.values()].sort((a, b) => b - a).slice(0, 10);
  const org = tagged.filter((x) => x.f?.organic);
  const multiTx15 = uniq(W(900).filter((x) => x.inTx > 1).map((x) => x.sig)), jitoTx30 = uniq(trades.filter((x) => rel(x) <= 30 && x.jito).map((x) => x.sig));
  const win = (s, f) => ({ buys: W(s).filter((x) => x.isBuy).length, sells: W(s).filter((x) => !x.isBuy).length, buyers: uniq(W(s).filter((x) => x.isBuy).map((x) => x.user)), bsol: +sol(sum(W(s).filter((x) => x.isBuy), (x) => x.sol)).toFixed(2), ssol: +sol(sum(W(s).filter((x) => !x.isBuy), (x) => x.sol)).toFixed(2) });
  const d = {
    mint: c.mint, launch_id: c.launch_id, role: c.role, post: c.post_name, name: created?.name || c.name, symbol: created?.symbol || c.symbol, post_delay_s: c.post_delay_s, peak_mc_usd: c.peak_mc, peak_min: c.peak_min, mc_now_usd: c.mc_now, holders_now: c.holders, graduated_now: c.progress >= 1,
    sol_usd: solUsd, txs: txs.length, trades: trades.length, window_min: WINDOW / 60, complete_s: complete ? complete - t0 : null,
    creator, creator_age_h: cp.first_ts ? +((t0 - cp.first_ts) / 3600).toFixed(1) : null, creator_txs: cp.txs ?? null, creator_funder: cp.funder ?? null,
    dev_buy_sol: +sol(dev.bsol).toFixed(3), dev_sell_sol: +sol(dev.ssol).toFixed(3), dev_first_sell_s: per.get(creator)?.firstSell ?? null, dev_sold_pct: devBought ? +(devSold / devBought * 100).toFixed(0) : null,
    block0_buyers: block0.length, block0_sol: +sol(sum(block0, (p) => p.bsol)).toFixed(2), sniper_buyers_3s: snipe.length, sniper_sol_3s: +sol(sum(snipe, (p) => p.bsol)).toFixed(2), early_sold_15m_pct: eBought ? +(eSold15 / eBought * 100).toFixed(0) : null,
    jito_txs_30s: jitoTx30, multi_buy_txs_15m: multiTx15,
    w1m: win(60), w5m: win(300), w15m: win(900), w40m: win(WINDOW),
    mc_sol: { m1: mcAt(60), m5: mcAt(300), m15: mcAt(900), m30: mcAt(1800), peak: peakT ? +mcSol(peakT).toFixed(1) : null, peak_s: peakT ? rel(peakT) : null },
    vol5: buckets,
    whale_buys_2sol_15m: sizes.filter((s) => s >= 2).length, median_buy_sol_15m: sizes.length ? +sizes[Math.floor(sizes.length / 2)].toFixed(3) : null, top10_buy_share_15m: sum(buys15, (x) => x.sol) ? +(sum(top10, (v) => v) / sum(buys15, (x) => x.sol) * 100).toFixed(1) : null,
    wash_wallets_15m: washW.length, wash_vol_share_15m: vol15 ? +(sum(washW, (p) => p.bs + p.ss) / vol15 * 100).toFixed(1) : null, roundtrip_share_15m: vol15 ? +(roundTrip / vol15 * 100).toFixed(1) : null,
    profiled: first.length, fresh1h_pct: share((f) => f.fresh1h), fresh24h_pct: share((f) => f.fresh24h), lowact_pct: share((f) => f.lowAct), cluster_pct: share((f) => f.cluster), creator_linked: first.filter((x) => x.f.creatorLinked).length,
    fresh24h_sol_pct: solShare((f) => f.fresh24h), cluster_sol_pct: solShare((f) => f.cluster), creator_linked_sol_pct: solShare((f) => f.creatorLinked), biggest_funder_group: Math.max(0, ...fGroups.values()),
    v: STUDY_V, venue, graduated_in_window: !!complete, tracker: c.tracker || null, covered_s: txs.length ? txs[txs.length - 1].blockTime - t0 : null, tx_cap_hit: txs.length >= cap, generic_txs: generic, gmgn, timing: tm,
    debug: { samples: dbg.samples, programs: Object.entries(dbg.programs).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 8), zeroSolBuys: trades.filter((x) => x.isBuy && !x.sol).length },
    organic_buyers: org.length, organic_pct: share((f) => f.organic), organic_sol_pct: solShare((f) => f.organic), first_organic_s: org[0]?.p.first ?? null,
  };
  db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO study_coins VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(c.mint, c.launch_id, c.role, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), JSON.stringify(d), null);
  const ins = db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO study_buyers VALUES (?,?,?,?)");
  tagged.slice(0, BUYERS).forEach((x, i) => ins.run(c.mint, x.p.w, i + 1, JSON.stringify({ first_s: x.p.first, slot: x.p.slot, idx: x.p.idx, buys: x.p.buys, sells: x.p.sells, buy_sol: +sol(x.p.bsol).toFixed(3), sell_sol: +sol(x.p.ssol).toFixed(3), first_sell_s: x.p.firstSell, ...x.f })));
  return d;
}

// ---------- which coins ----------
let CENSUS = [];
/** 0.7.1: refresh the census list from the tracker's database (graduated, peak ≥ CENSUS_MIN, last CENSUS_DAYS days). */
export function loadCensus(tdb) {
  if (!tdb) return 0;
  try { CENSUS = tdb.prepare("SELECT mint, name, symbol, creator, create_ts, bundle_pct, bundle_sol, bundle_wallets, dev_buy_sol, peak_mc_usd FROM tokens WHERE graduated=1 AND peak_mc_usd>=? AND create_ts>=? ORDER BY create_ts DESC LIMIT 3000").all(n("LAUNCH_CENSUS_MIN", 100000), Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - n("LAUNCH_CENSUS_DAYS", 60) * 86400); } catch (e) { studyStat.lastErr = "census: " + e.message.slice(0, 100); }
  return CENSUS.length;
}
function pickCoins(db) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), done = new Set(db.prepare("SELECT mint, data, err, studied_ts FROM study_coins").all().filter((r) => (!r.err && (() => { try { return JSON.parse(r.data).v === STUDY_V; } catch { return false; } })()) || (r.err && r.studied_ts > now - 600)).map((r) => r.mint)); // a failed coin is tried again after 50 min
  const peaks = db.prepare("SELECT p.*, l.t_post_ms FROM copy_peaks p LEFT JOIN launches l ON l.id=p.launch_id WHERE p.created_ts <= ? AND p.created_ts >= ?").all(now - WINDOW - 300, now - 3 * 86400);
  const runners = peaks.filter((p) => !p.ours && p.peak_mc >= MIN_PEAK).sort((a, b) => b.peak_mc - a.peak_mc);
  const out = [];
  const add = (p, role) => { if (!done.has(p.mint) && !out.some((o) => o.mint === p.mint)) out.push({ ...p, role, post_delay_s: p.t_post_ms ? p.created_ts - Math.floor(p.t_post_ms / 1000) : null }); };
  // 0.7.1 (owner, 3 Nov 10:41 UK: "every coin over 100K, also migrated … work all the way back"): the census seed — every pump.fun launch the tracker
  // recorded that graduated and peaked ≥ $100K (tracker.db tokens; read-only), newest first, 60 days back. Role "census". The tracker's bundle data rides along.
  try {
    if (CENSUS && CENSUS.length) for (const t of CENSUS) { if (!done.has(t.mint) && !out.some((o) => o.mint === t.mint)) out.push({ mint: t.mint, name: t.name, symbol: t.symbol, created_ts: t.create_ts, ours: 0, peak_mc: t.peak_mc_usd, role: "census", post_name: "census", launch_id: null, post_delay_s: null, tracker: { bundle_pct: t.bundle_pct, bundle_sol: t.bundle_sol, bundle_wallets: t.bundle_wallets, dev_buy_sol: t.dev_buy_sol, creator: t.creator } }); }
  } catch {}
  for (const r of runners) add(r, "runner");
  for (const lid of [...new Set(runners.map((r) => r.launch_id))]) { // the same post's copies that did not run, and ours
    const same = peaks.filter((p) => p.launch_id === lid && p.peak_mc < MIN_PEAK);
    same.filter((p) => p.ours).forEach((p) => add(p, "ours"));
    same.filter((p) => !p.ours).sort((a, b) => (b.holders || 0) - (a.holders || 0) || b.peak_mc - a.peak_mc).slice(0, 3).forEach((p) => add(p, "control"));
  }
  return out.slice(0, PER_RUN);
}

export async function runStudy(db, done) {
  if (studyStat.running) return; studyStat.running = true; studyStat.runs++;
  try {
    let solUsd = null; try { const w = await gget("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol", address: "So11111111111111111111111111111111111111112" }); solUsd = N(w?.price?.price ?? w?.price); } catch {}
    const queue = pickCoins(db), busy = new Set(); // 0.7.4: PARALLEL coins at once (each one is mostly waiting on Helius)
    const worker = async () => { for (let c; (c = queue.shift()); ) {
      if (studyStat.creditsDay === today() && studyStat.creditsToday >= DAY_CREDITS) { studyStat.lastErr = `daily Helius budget ${DAY_CREDITS} credits reached`; queue.length = 0; break; }
      busy.add(`${c.role} ${c.name} ${c.mint.slice(0, 6)}`); studyStat.current = [...busy].join(" | ");
      if (c.role === "census" && !db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM copy_peaks WHERE mint=?").get(c.mint)) { try { await peakOne(db, { mint: c.mint, launch_id: null, post_name: "census", name: c.name || "", symbol: c.symbol || "", created_ts: c.created_ts, ours: 0 }, { klines: false }); // 0.7.7: a 60-day coin's candle walk is ~175 GMGN calls; the tracker already holds its peak
        if (c.peak_mc) db.prepare("UPDATE copy_peaks SET peak_mc=COALESCE(peak_mc, ?), peak_close_mc=COALESCE(peak_close_mc, ?) WHERE mint=?").run(c.peak_mc, c.peak_mc, c.mint); const row = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM copy_peaks WHERE mint=?").get(c.mint); if (row) Object.assign(c, { peak_mc: row.peak_mc, peak_min: row.peak_min, mc_now: row.mc_now, holders: row.holders, progress: row.progress }); } catch {} }
      try { await studyCoin(db, c, solUsd); studyStat.studied++; }
      catch (e) { studyStat.errors++; studyStat.lastErr = `${c.mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 200); db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO study_coins VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(c.mint, c.launch_id, c.role, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), JSON.stringify({ name: c.name }), e.message.slice(0, 200)); }
      busy.delete(`${c.role} ${c.name} ${c.mint.slice(0, 6)}`); studyStat.current = [...busy].join(" | ");
      if (studyStat.studied <= 2) try { done?.(); } catch {} // the first coins go out at once, to check the decoding
    } };
    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: PARALLEL }, worker));
    studyStat.lastRun = new Date().toISOString();
  } catch (e) { studyStat.errors++; studyStat.lastErr = e.message.slice(0, 200); }
  finally { studyStat.running = false; studyStat.current = ""; try { done?.(); } catch {} }
}

// 0.6.0 (owner, 31 Oct 23:33 UK: "Can I just give you a CA of new coins and you can tell me how they pump?"): study any coin by address.
// Addresses come from launcher/study-now.txt (pushed by the chat) or a website request {action:"study", mint}. Prices first (GMGN), then the chain study.
export const manualStat = { queued: 0, done: 0, lastErr: "", last: null };
let mq = Promise.resolve();
export function studyAddress(db, mint, done) {
  if (!/^[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{32,44}$/.test(String(mint || ""))) return false;
  manualStat.queued++;
  mq = mq.then(async () => {
    try {
      const info = await gget("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol", address: mint });
      let created = N(info?.creation_timestamp ?? info?.open_timestamp); if (created > 1e12) created = Math.floor(created / 1000);
      if (!created) throw new Error("GMGN gives no creation time for this coin");
      await peakOne(db, { mint, launch_id: null, post_name: "manual", name: info?.name || "", symbol: info?.symbol || "", created_ts: created, ours: 0 });
      const row = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM copy_peaks WHERE mint=?").get(mint) || { mint, created_ts: created };
      let solUsd = null; try { const w = await gget("/v1/token/info", { chain: "sol", address: "So11111111111111111111111111111111111111112" }); solUsd = N(w?.price?.price ?? w?.price); } catch {}
      await studyCoin(db, { ...row, launch_id: null, role: "manual", post_name: "manual", name: row.name || info?.name, symbol: row.symbol || info?.symbol, post_delay_s: null }, solUsd);
      manualStat.done++; manualStat.last = mint;
    } catch (e) { manualStat.lastErr = `${String(mint).slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 200); db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO study_coins VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(mint, null, "manual", Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), "{}", e.message.slice(0, 200)); }
    try { done?.(); } catch {}
  });
  return true;
}

// launcher 0.8.17 (owner, 5 Nov 2026 06:15 UK: "just build it … each trade at the minimum … choose one of the books … trade really minimalistically,
// but in real time … what price are we actually buying into"): LIVE SHADOW TRADES — real money, tiny size, one book.
// Every paper entry of the live book (livebook.txt: "FD 0.02" = the FD rule, 0.02 SOL a trade) is bought for real through Jupiter's swap
// (pump.fun curve or PumpSwap, whichever the coin is on), the position is re-quoted for the sell every few seconds and sold for real the moment the
// venue trail (25% off the high graduated / 40% on the curve, same as the paper columns) fires on those quotes — or at 2 h, the paper horizon.
// What is kept per trade is the chain's own account of it: SOL that left the wallet for the buy (network fee, priority fee and token-account rent
// included), tokens received, SOL that came back from the sell and from closing the token account. x = SOL back ÷ SOL out; r = x − 1 is the real
// net return, set beside the paper venue exit (r_venue) and the 0.2 SOL quote on the same trade. Wallet = the launcher's (its key never leaves the box).
// Stops: launcher/livebook.txt missing or "off", a file /root/launcher/live_stop, the wallet under MIN_BALANCE_SOL + stake, or 6 open positions.
import { PublicKey, VersionedTransaction, TransactionInstruction } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { CFG, HOME } from "./config.mjs";
import { conn, rpcStats, getBalance, simulate, waitConfirm, readFill, buildTx, blockHeight } from "./rpc.mjs";
import { jqRaw, swapTxFor, SOL, qStat } from "./quote.mjs";
import { trailPct } from "./volpaper.mjs";
import { ixBudget } from "./pump.mjs";

const n = (k, d) => (process.env[k] != null && process.env[k] !== "" && Number.isFinite(Number(process.env[k])) ? Number(process.env[k]) : d);
const PRIO_MAX = n("LAUNCH_LIVE_PRIO_LAMPORTS", 1_000_000), MAX_OPEN = n("LAUNCH_LIVE_MAX_OPEN", 2), LOSS_ROLL = n("LAUNCH_LIVE_LOSS_ROLL_SOL", 0.15), LOSS_ROLL_N = n("LAUNCH_LIVE_LOSS_ROLL_N", 20), LOSS_TOTAL = n("LAUNCH_LIVE_LOSS_TOTAL_SOL", 0.2), SLIP_BUY = n("LAUNCH_LIVE_SLIP_BUY", 1500), SLIP_SELL = n("LAUNCH_LIVE_SLIP_SELL", 2500); // 0.9.8 (reviews, 5 Nov): 2 open at most; stop new buys when the last ≤ 20 fills have lost 0.15 SOL (from the first trade) or the experiment has lost 0.2 SOL in all; selling is never blocked
const HORIZON = 120 * 60, GIVE_UP = 180 * 60; // sell at 2 h like the paper score; a coin with no route for 3 h is written off
const now = () => Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
export const liveStat = { enabled: false, book: null, stake: 0, buys: 0, buyFails: 0, sells: 0, sellFails: 0, skipped: 0, open: 0, writtenOff: 0, balanceSol: null, lastErr: "", lastSig: "", polls: 0 };
liveStat.expStart = n("LAUNCH_LIVE_EXP_START", 0) || Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000); // 0.9.8: the cumulative loss limit counts fills from here (env to pin it across restarts)
let db = null, wallet = null, ME = null, cfgAt = 0, cfg = { book: null, stake: 0 };

/** livebook.txt in the launcher folder: "<BOOK> <SOL>" (e.g. "FD 0.02"); "off" or missing = no live trades. Env LAUNCH_LIVE_BOOK / LAUNCH_LIVE_SOL override. Re-read every 5 s. */
export function liveCfg() {
  if (Date.now() - cfgAt < 5000) return cfg; cfgAt = Date.now();
  let book = process.env.LAUNCH_LIVE_BOOK || null, stake = n("LAUNCH_LIVE_SOL", 0);
  if (!book) { try { const [b, s] = readFileSync(new URL("../livebook.txt", import.meta.url), "utf8").trim().split(/\s+/); if (b && b.toLowerCase() !== "off") { book = b.toUpperCase(); stake = Number(s) || 0.02; } } catch {} }
  if (existsSync(join(HOME, "live_stop"))) book = null;
  if (CFG.SMOKE || !wallet) book = null;
  cfg = { book, stake: book ? Math.max(0.005, stake) : 0 }; liveStat.enabled = !!book; liveStat.book = book; liveStat.stake = cfg.stake; return cfg;
}
export const isLiveBook = (k) => liveCfg().book === k;

export function initLive(d, kp) {
  db = d; wallet = kp; ME = kp.publicKey;
  db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS live_trades (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, vol_id INTEGER UNIQUE, strat TEXT, mint TEXT, name TEXT, curve INTEGER, entry_ts INTEGER, entry_price REAL, sol_usd REAL, stake_sol REAL,
    buy_ts INTEGER, buy_sig TEXT, buy_status TEXT, buy_ms INTEGER, buy_err TEXT, buy_impact REAL, sol_in REAL, tokens REAL, buy_px REAL, in_gap REAL,
    hi REAL, last_x REAL, last_ts INTEGER, n_quotes INTEGER DEFAULT 0, sell_ts INTEGER, sell_sig TEXT, sell_status TEXT, sell_reason TEXT, sell_tries INTEGER DEFAULT 0, sell_err TEXT, sell_impact REAL,
    sol_out REAL, x REAL, r REAL, closed INTEGER DEFAULT 0, done INTEGER DEFAULT 0)`);
  for (const c of ["buy_quote_ms INTEGER", "buy_sim_ms INTEGER", "buy_land_ms INTEGER", "buy_slot INTEGER", "buy_lag_s INTEGER", "sell_ms INTEGER", "sell_land_ms INTEGER", "sell_slot INTEGER"]) try { db.exec(`ALTER TABLE live_trades ADD COLUMN ${c}`); } catch {} // 0.8.19 (owner, 07:45 UK: "transaction speed … both ways … is the key")
  db.exec("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS live_open ON live_trades (done, buy_status)");
  liveCfg(); try { if (cfg.book) db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fd_rules (k, since_ts, ver) VALUES ('LIVE', ?, ?)").run(now(), (() => { try { return JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL("../package.json", import.meta.url), "utf8")).version; } catch { return null; } })()); } catch {} // when live trading was first switched on (the page's "Loaded" time)
}
const openCount = () => db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM live_trades WHERE done=0").get().n;

/** Send a signed transaction (plain RPC; Jupiter's transaction carries no Sender tip) and keep re-sending the same bytes until it lands, fails, or its blockhash expires. */
async function land(tx, lastValid, ms = 25000) {
  const raw = tx.serialize(); const t0 = Date.now(); rpcStats.sends++;
  const sig = await conn.sendRawTransaction(raw, { skipPreflight: true, maxRetries: 0 }); let resends = 0;
  while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) {
    const c = await waitConfirm(sig, 2500); if (c.status !== "timeout") return { ...c, sig, resends, ms: Date.now() - t0 };
    if (lastValid) { try { if ((await blockHeight()) > lastValid) return { status: "expired", sig, resends, ms: Date.now() - t0, err: null }; } catch {} }
    try { await conn.sendRawTransaction(raw, { skipPreflight: true, maxRetries: 0 }); resends++; } catch {}
  }
  const late = await waitConfirm(sig, 8000); return { ...late, sig, resends, ms: Date.now() - t0 };
}
async function fill(sig, mint) { for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { const f = await readFill(sig, ME, new PublicKey(mint), 1); if (f) return f; await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 700)); } return null; }

/** The real buy for a paper entry of the live book. Not awaited by the caller; everything it learns goes into live_trades. */
export async function liveBuy(row) {
  const c = liveCfg(); if (!c.book || row.strat !== c.book) return;
  const t = now(), lamports = Math.round(c.stake * 1e9);
  const ins = (status, err = null) => db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO live_trades (vol_id, strat, mint, name, curve, entry_ts, entry_price, sol_usd, stake_sol, buy_ts, buy_status, buy_err, done) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)")
    .run(row.id, row.strat, row.mint, row.name, row.curve, t, row.entry_price, row.su, c.stake, t, status, err, status === "skipped" ? 1 : 0).changes;
  const upd = (status, extra = {}) => { const ks = Object.keys(extra); db.prepare(`UPDATE live_trades SET buy_status=?${ks.map((k) => `, ${k}=?`).join("")} WHERE vol_id=?`).run(status, ...ks.map((k) => extra[k]), row.id); };
  if (openCount() >= MAX_OPEN) { if (ins("skipped", `${MAX_OPEN} open`)) liveStat.skipped++; return; }
  { const roll = db.prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(sol_out - sol_in), 0) v, COUNT(*) n FROM (SELECT sol_out, sol_in FROM live_trades WHERE done=1 AND buy_status='confirmed' AND sol_out IS NOT NULL ORDER BY sell_ts DESC LIMIT ?)").get(LOSS_ROLL_N);
    const total = db.prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(sol_out - sol_in), 0) v FROM live_trades WHERE done=1 AND buy_status='confirmed' AND sol_out IS NOT NULL AND buy_ts >= ?").get(liveStat.expStart || 0);
    if (roll.v <= -LOSS_ROLL) { if (ins("skipped", `loss limit: ${roll.v.toFixed(3)} SOL over the last ${roll.n} fills`)) liveStat.skipped++; liveStat.lastErr = `stopped: ${roll.v.toFixed(3)} SOL over the last ${roll.n} fills`; return; }
    if (total.v <= -LOSS_TOTAL) { if (ins("skipped", `experiment loss limit: ${total.v.toFixed(3)} SOL`)) liveStat.skipped++; liveStat.lastErr = `stopped: experiment at ${total.v.toFixed(3)} SOL`; return; } }
  let bal = null; try { bal = await getBalance(ME); liveStat.balanceSol = +(bal / 1e9).toFixed(4); } catch (e) { liveStat.lastErr = "balance: " + e.message.slice(0, 80); }
  if (bal == null || bal < (CFG.MIN_BALANCE_SOL + c.stake) * 1e9) { if (ins("skipped", bal == null ? "balance unknown" : `balance ${(bal / 1e9).toFixed(3)} < ${(CFG.MIN_BALANCE_SOL + c.stake).toFixed(3)}`)) liveStat.skipped++; return; }
  if (!ins("quoting")) return;
  const t0 = Date.now();
  try {
    const q = await jqRaw(SOL, row.mint, lamports, SLIP_BUY, true); qStat.buys++;
    const s = await swapTxFor(q, ME.toBase58(), PRIO_MAX); const tQ = Date.now() - t0;
    const tx = VersionedTransaction.deserialize(Buffer.from(s.tx64, "base64")); tx.sign([wallet]);
    const t1 = Date.now(); const sim = await simulate(tx); const tS = Date.now() - t1; if (!sim.ok) throw new Error("sim: " + String(sim.err).slice(0, 110) + " " + (sim.logs || []).filter((l) => /Error|insufficient|slippage/i.test(l)).slice(-1).join("").slice(0, 60));
    upd("sent", { buy_impact: Number(q.priceImpactPct) || 0 });
    const L = await land(tx, s.lastValidBlockHeight); liveStat.lastSig = L.sig;
    if (L.status !== "confirmed") throw new Error(`${L.status}${L.err ? " " + L.err.slice(0, 60) : ""} sig ${L.sig.slice(0, 12)}`);
    const f = await fill(L.sig, row.mint);
    if (!f || !(f.tokenDelta > 0)) throw new Error(`no fill read (sig ${L.sig.slice(0, 12)})`);
    const solIn = -f.solDelta / 1e9, rent = 0.00203928, px = (solIn - rent - f.fee / 1e9) / (f.tokenDelta / 1e6) * (row.su || 0), gap = row.entry_price > 0 && px > 0 ? px / row.entry_price - 1 : null;
    upd("confirmed", { buy_sig: L.sig, buy_ms: Date.now() - t0, buy_quote_ms: tQ, buy_sim_ms: tS, buy_land_ms: L.ms, buy_slot: L.slot || null, buy_lag_s: (f.blockTime || t) - row.t, sol_in: +solIn.toFixed(6), tokens: f.tokenDelta, buy_px: px || null, in_gap: gap != null ? +gap.toFixed(4) : null, hi: 1, buy_ts: f.blockTime || t }); // buy_lag_s = paper entry → fill, in seconds
    liveStat.buys++;
  } catch (e) { liveStat.buyFails++; liveStat.lastErr = `buy ${row.mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 140); upd("failed", { buy_err: e.message.slice(0, 120), buy_ms: Date.now() - t0, done: 1 }); }
}

/** Every 4 s: the open live position quoted longest ago gets a sell quote for its actual tokens; the venue trail off the quoted high (from 1 = what went in) or the 2-hour horizon sells it for real. */
let polling = false;
export async function livePoll() {
  if (!db || polling) return; polling = true;
  try {
    const t = now(); liveStat.open = openCount();
    const open = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM live_trades WHERE done=0 AND buy_status='confirmed' AND tokens>0 ORDER BY buy_ts").all();
    if (!open.length) return;
    // 0.9.4: the trail runs on the chain-side price stream the simulated fill keeps for the same coin (chain_fill: every transaction that moves the pool),
    // not on a Jupiter poll — Jupiter is for execution only (one quote + one swap per leg). Same decision as the simulation, so simulated vs real is like for like.
    let polled = false;
    for (const r of open) {
      const c = db.prepare("SELECT s_last_x, s_hi, s_last_ts, s_trig_ts, s_exit_reason, done FROM chain_fill WHERE mint=? AND s_tokens IS NOT NULL ORDER BY s_last_ts DESC LIMIT 1").get(r.mint);
      if (c && c.s_last_x != null && t - (c.s_last_ts || 0) < 120) {
        const x = c.s_last_x, hi = Math.max(r.hi || 1, c.s_hi || 1, x), keep = 1 - trailPct(r.curve);
        const trig = c.s_trig_ts != null || (c.done === 1 && /trail|2h/.test(c.s_exit_reason || "")) || x <= (r.hi || 1) * keep;
        const reason = trig ? "trail" : t - r.buy_ts >= HORIZON ? "2h" : null;
        db.prepare("UPDATE live_trades SET last_ts=?, last_x=?, hi=?, n_quotes=n_quotes+1 WHERE id=?").run(t, +x.toFixed(4), +hi.toFixed(4), r.id);
        if (reason && !(r.sell_status === "sending")) { let q; try { q = await jqRaw(r.mint, SOL, Math.floor(r.tokens), SLIP_SELL, true); } catch (e) { qStat.errors++; liveStat.lastErr = `sell quote ${r.mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 120); continue; } await liveSell(r, q, reason); }
        continue;
      }
      // no chain-side price for this coin (unpriced entry, or the stream is stale): the old Jupiter poll, one coin per pass, no more than every 8 s
      if (polled || Date.now() < qStat.pauseUntil || (r.last_ts && t - r.last_ts < 8)) continue; polled = true; liveStat.polls++;
      let q; try { q = await jqRaw(r.mint, SOL, Math.floor(r.tokens), SLIP_SELL, true); }
      catch (e) { qStat.errors++; liveStat.lastErr = `poll ${r.mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 120);
        if (!/paused|429/.test(e.message)) { db.prepare("UPDATE live_trades SET last_ts=?, n_quotes=n_quotes+1, sell_err=? WHERE id=?").run(t, e.message.slice(0, 80), r.id);
          if (t - r.buy_ts > GIVE_UP) { db.prepare("UPDATE live_trades SET done=1, sell_status='written_off', sell_reason='no route 3 h', sol_out=0, x=0, r=-1, sell_ts=? WHERE id=?").run(t, r.id); liveStat.writtenOff++; } }
        continue; }
      const x = Number(q.outAmount) / 1e9 / r.stake_sol, hi = Math.max(r.hi || 1, x), keep = 1 - trailPct(r.curve);
      const reason = x <= (r.hi || 1) * keep ? "trail" : t - r.buy_ts >= HORIZON ? "2h" : null;
      db.prepare("UPDATE live_trades SET last_ts=?, last_x=?, hi=?, n_quotes=n_quotes+1 WHERE id=?").run(t, +x.toFixed(4), +hi.toFixed(4), r.id);
      if (reason) await liveSell(r, q, reason);
    }
  } catch (e) { liveStat.lastErr = "poll: " + e.message.slice(0, 120); }
  finally { polling = false; }
}

async function liveSell(r, q, reason) {
  const t = now(), t0 = Date.now();
  db.prepare("UPDATE live_trades SET sell_status='sending', sell_reason=?, sell_tries=sell_tries+1, sell_ts=? WHERE id=?").run(reason, t, r.id);
  try {
    const s = await swapTxFor(q, ME.toBase58(), PRIO_MAX);
    const tx = VersionedTransaction.deserialize(Buffer.from(s.tx64, "base64")); tx.sign([wallet]);
    const L = await land(tx, s.lastValidBlockHeight); liveStat.lastSig = L.sig; const tSell = Date.now() - t0; // trail trigger (the quote) → landed or failed
    if (L.status !== "confirmed") throw new Error(`${L.status}${L.err ? " " + L.err.slice(0, 60) : ""}`);
    const f = await fill(L.sig, r.mint); if (!f) throw new Error("no fill read");
    const left = r.tokens + f.tokenDelta; let solOut = f.solDelta / 1e9;
    if (left > r.tokens * 0.001) { // exact-in sells everything; anything left is a partial fill — keep it open with what came back so far recorded
      db.prepare("UPDATE live_trades SET sell_status='partial', sell_sig=?, sell_err=?, sol_out=COALESCE(sol_out,0)+?, tokens=? WHERE id=?").run(L.sig, `partial: ${left} left`, +solOut.toFixed(6), left, r.id); liveStat.lastErr = `sell ${r.mint.slice(0, 6)}: partial`; return; }
    let closed = 0; try { solOut += await closeTokenAccounts(r.mint); closed = 1; } catch (e) { liveStat.lastErr = `close ${r.mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 120); }
    const total = (r.sol_out || 0) + solOut, x = total / r.sol_in;
    db.prepare("UPDATE live_trades SET sell_status='confirmed', sell_sig=?, sell_ts=?, sell_impact=?, sol_out=?, x=?, r=?, closed=?, done=1, sell_err=NULL, sell_ms=?, sell_land_ms=?, sell_slot=? WHERE id=?").run(L.sig, f.blockTime || t, Number(q.priceImpactPct) || 0, +total.toFixed(6), +x.toFixed(4), +(x - 1).toFixed(4), closed, tSell, L.ms, L.slot || null, r.id);
    liveStat.sells++;
  } catch (e) { liveStat.sellFails++; liveStat.lastErr = `sell ${r.mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 140); db.prepare("UPDATE live_trades SET sell_status='failed', sell_err=? WHERE id=?").run(e.message.slice(0, 120), r.id); } // stays open: the next poll quotes and tries again
}

/** After a full sell: close the (empty) token account(s) of that mint to get the rent back. Returns the SOL that came back (≈ 0.002). */
async function closeTokenAccounts(mint) {
  rpcStats.calls++; const res = await conn.getTokenAccountsByOwner(ME, { mint: new PublicKey(mint) }, "confirmed");
  const ixs = []; for (const a of res.value) { const amt = a.account.data.readBigUInt64LE(64); if (amt !== 0n) continue; // bytes 64–71 of a token account = amount
    ixs.push(new TransactionInstruction({ programId: a.account.owner, data: Buffer.from([9]), keys: [{ pubkey: a.pubkey, isSigner: false, isWritable: true }, { pubkey: ME, isSigner: false, isWritable: true }, { pubkey: ME, isSigner: true, isWritable: false }] })); }
  if (!ixs.length) return 0;
  const tx = await buildTx(ME, [...ixBudget(20000, 200000), ...ixs], wallet);
  const L = await land(tx, tx.lastValidBlockHeight, 20000); if (L.status !== "confirmed") throw new Error(L.status);
  const f = await fill(L.sig, mint); return f ? f.solDelta / 1e9 : 0;
}
