// launcher 0.4.0 (owner, 31 Oct 2026 18:41 UK: "paper trade any coin with a 5-minute volume of 300k, and one with 500k … under 1 million … the age can be anything"):
// two PAPER strategies on every Solana coin GMGN lists. Every 30 s it reads GMGN's top 100 coins by 5-minute volume; the first time a coin's
// 5-min volume is ≥ $300K (V300) or ≥ $500K (V500) and under $1M, it records a paper buy at GMGN's price. Nothing is bought or sold.
// 2 h 5 min after entry it scores the trade from GMGN's 1-minute candles: price at 5/15/30/60/120 min, peak, and the exits we tested before
// (2x or −50%, 1.5x or −30%, 40% trail from the peak), after the real-cost models in LESSONS.md (bonding curve vs PumpSwap).
// Market cap, age, holders and the GMGN row are kept with each entry so other cuts (e.g. market cap under $1M) can be read later.
import { gget, N } from "./gmgn.mjs";
import { sellQuote, qStat } from "./quote.mjs"; // 0.8.15

const n = (k, d) => (process.env[k] != null && process.env[k] !== "" && Number.isFinite(Number(process.env[k])) ? Number(process.env[k]) : d);
export const TIERS = [{ k: "V300", min: n("LAUNCH_V300", 300000) }, { k: "V500", min: n("LAUNCH_V500", 500000) }];
const MAX_VOL = n("LAUNCH_VOL_MAX", 1000000), SCORE_AFTER = 125 * 60;
export const volStat = { polls: 0, rows: 0, entries: 0, scored: 0, errors: 0, lastErr: "", lastPoll: null, maxVolSeen: null };
const now = () => Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
// LESSONS.md cost models: bonding curve x×0.97−0.02−1−0.02 (stops −0.01 more); PumpSwap x/1.02×0.967−1−0.006−0.02 (stops x≤0.62 −0.05 more)
const realX = (x, curve) => (x == null ? null : curve ? x * 0.97 - 0.02 - 1 - 0.02 - (x <= 0.62 ? 0.01 : 0) : x / 1.02 * 0.967 - 1 - 0.006 - 0.02 - (x <= 0.62 ? 0.05 : 0));

export function initVol(db) {
  db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vol_paper (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, strat TEXT, mint TEXT, name TEXT, symbol TEXT, entry_ts INTEGER, entry_price REAL, entry_mc REAL, vol5 REAL,
    created_ts INTEGER, age_min REAL, holders INTEGER, exchange TEXT, launchpad TEXT, curve INTEGER, row TEXT, done INTEGER DEFAULT 0, err TEXT,
    x5 REAL, x15 REAL, x30 REAL, x60 REAL, x120 REAL, peak120 REAL, peak_min REAL, low_before_peak REAL, x_tp2sl50 REAL, x_tp15sl30 REAL, x_trail40 REAL,
    r_tp2sl50 REAL, r_tp15sl30 REAL, r_trail40 REAL, r_hold60 REAL, r_hold120 REAL, UNIQUE (strat, mint))`);
  db.exec("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vol_bf_done (mint TEXT PRIMARY KEY)"); // 0.7.4
  try { db.exec("ALTER TABLE vol_paper ADD COLUMN x_half2 REAL"); } catch {} try { db.exec("ALTER TABLE vol_paper ADD COLUMN r_half2 REAL"); } catch {} // 0.5.0
  for (const c of ["mk_ts INTEGER", "mk_x REAL", "mk_hi REAL", "mk_last INTEGER", "mk_sold_x REAL", "mk_sold_ts INTEGER", "mk_r REAL"]) try { db.exec(`ALTER TABLE vol_paper ADD COLUMN ${c}`); } catch {} // 0.8.10 live mark
  // 0.8.16 (owner, 5 Nov 04:22 UK, after the best-exit study in LAUNCHER.md): the VENUE exit — a 25% trail on graduated coins, the 40% trail on curve coins —
  // scored on every trade (x_venue / r_venue / m_venue), followed by the live mark (mk_pct = the trail it used) and by the shadow sells; plus the open-position
  // quote poll (q_live_*: the latest sell quote, its high since entry and how many quotes) whose own trail fires the quoted exit (q_exit_src = 'poll'; 'mark' = quoted when the candle mark sold).
  for (const c of ["x_venue REAL", "r_venue REAL", "m_venue REAL", "mk_pct REAL", "q_live_ts INTEGER", "q_live_x REAL", "q_live_hi REAL", "q_live_n INTEGER", "q_exit_src TEXT"]) try { db.exec(`ALTER TABLE vol_paper ADD COLUMN ${c}`); } catch {}
  try { db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET x_venue=CASE WHEN curve=1 THEN x_trail40 ELSE x_trail25 END, r_venue=CASE WHEN curve=1 THEN r_trail40 ELSE r_trail25 END, m_venue=CASE WHEN curve=1 THEN m_trail40 ELSE NULL END WHERE done=1 AND err IS NULL AND x_venue IS NULL AND x_trail25 IS NOT NULL").run(); } catch {} // scored trades: the venue exit is one of two columns they already have (no candle calls)
  try { db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET q_exit_src='mark' WHERE q_exit_x IS NOT NULL AND q_exit_src IS NULL").run(); } catch {}
}
/** the venue exit's trail: 40% off the high on the curve, 25% once graduated (LAUNCHER.md 5 Nov 04:15 UK: curve runners wobble, graduated moves fade) */
export const trailPct = (curve) => (curve === 1 ? 0.4 : 0.25);

/** 0.8.10 (owner, 4 Nov 22:59 UK: "show each open trade's result within minutes… go do that"): the LIVE MARK. Every 15 s (0.8.16; was every minute), for every open trade whose venue trail (0.8.16; was the 40% trail) has not
 *  sold yet (one GMGN candle call per coin, shared by the books that hold it): price now vs entry (mk_x), the high since entry (mk_hi) and, the minute the
 *  trail would have sold, the sale (mk_sold_x, mk_sold_ts, mk_r after costs). Same replay as volScore's trail (candles after the entry minute, stop = 60% of
 *  the high), so the live number and the 2-hour score agree; the 2-hour score stays the record. A sold trade is not fetched again. */
export const markStat = { passes: 0, marked: 0, sold: 0, calls: 0, errors: 0, lastErr: "", lastPass: null };
let marking = false;
export async function volMark(db) {
  if (marking) return; marking = true;
  try {
    const t = now();
    const rows = db.prepare("SELECT id, mint, entry_ts, entry_price, curve, mk_hi, mk_last, q_tokens, q_exit_ts, strat FROM vol_paper WHERE done=0 AND err IS NULL AND mk_sold_ts IS NULL AND entry_ts>=? AND entry_ts<=? ORDER BY entry_ts LIMIT 80").all(t - 125 * 60, t - 60);
    const byMint = new Map(); for (const r of rows) { if (!byMint.has(r.mint)) byMint.set(r.mint, []); byMint.get(r.mint).push(r); }
    markStat.passes++;
    for (const [mint, rs] of byMint) {
      try {
        const from = Math.max(Math.min(...rs.map((r) => r.mk_last || r.entry_ts)) - 60, t - 98 * 60); // ≤ 100 candles a call; the high so far is carried in mk_hi
        const ks = (await candles(mint, from, t)).sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t); markStat.calls++;
        if (!ks.length) continue;
        for (const r of rs) {
          const X = (v) => v / r.entry_price, keep = 1 - trailPct(r.curve); let hi = r.mk_hi || 1, last = r.mk_last || r.entry_ts, sold = null, soldT = null; // 0.8.16: the venue trail
          for (const c of ks) { if (c.t <= last) continue; if (c.t <= r.entry_ts) { last = c.t; continue; } // the entry minute's candle is skipped, as in volScore
            if (X(c.l) <= hi * keep) { sold = hi * keep; soldT = c.t; last = c.t; break; } hi = Math.max(hi, X(c.h)); if (c.t + 60 <= t) last = c.t; } // the current minute's candle is still forming: re-read it next pass
          const nowX = X(ks[ks.length - 1].c);
          if (sold != null) { db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET mk_ts=?, mk_x=?, mk_hi=?, mk_last=?, mk_sold_x=?, mk_sold_ts=?, mk_r=?, mk_pct=? WHERE id=?").run(t, +nowX.toFixed(4), +hi.toFixed(4), last, +sold.toFixed(4), soldT, realX(sold, r.curve === 1), 1 - keep, r.id); markStat.sold++;
            if (r.q_tokens > 0 && r.q_exit_ts == null && process.env.LAUNCH_SHADOW_POLL === "1") shadowSell(db, r, sold, soldT, "mark").catch(() => {}); // 0.9.3: off with the poll — Jupiter budget to the live book // 0.8.15: the real sell quote the moment the candle mark says the trail sold (0.8.16: the mark runs every 15 s; skipped when the quote poll already sold)
            else db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET q_exit_gap=q_exit_x/?-1 WHERE id=? AND q_exit_src='poll' AND q_exit_gap IS NULL AND q_exit_x IS NOT NULL").run(sold, r.id); } // the poll's exit against the paper sale it now has
          else db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET mk_ts=?, mk_x=?, mk_hi=?, mk_last=?, mk_pct=? WHERE id=?").run(t, +nowX.toFixed(4), +hi.toFixed(4), last, 1 - keep, r.id);
          markStat.marked++;
        }
      } catch (e) { markStat.errors++; markStat.lastErr = `${mint.slice(0, 6)} ${e.message}`.slice(0, 160); if (/rate limited/i.test(e.message)) break; }
    }
    markStat.lastPass = new Date().toISOString();
  } catch (e) { markStat.errors++; markStat.lastErr = "mark: " + e.message.slice(0, 160); }
  finally { marking = false; }
}

export async function volPoll(db) {
  try {
    const d = await gget("/v1/market/rank", { chain: "sol", interval: "5m", order_by: "volume", limit: "100" });
    const rows = Array.isArray(d?.rank) ? d.rank : Array.isArray(d) ? d : []; volStat.polls++; volStat.rows = rows.length; volStat.lastPoll = new Date().toISOString();
    const t = now(), ins = db.prepare(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO vol_paper (strat, mint, name, symbol, entry_ts, entry_price, entry_mc, vol5, created_ts, age_min, holders, exchange, launchpad, curve, row)
      VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`);
    for (const r of rows) {
      const mint = r.address, v5 = N(r.volume), mc = N(r.market_cap), px = N(r.price), created = N(r.creation_timestamp ?? r.open_timestamp);
      if (v5 != null) volStat.maxVolSeen = Math.max(volStat.maxVolSeen || 0, v5);
      if (!mint || !(px > 0) || v5 == null || v5 >= MAX_VOL) continue;
      const ex = r.exchange ?? null, curve = ex ? (/^pump(fun|_fun)?$|bonding|curve/i.test(ex) ? 1 : 0) : null;
      // 0.7.2 (owner, 3 Nov 11:23 UK): VA — older coins (12 h to 14 days), 5-min volume ≥ $100K / $200K / $300K, market cap under $2M (the $1M cut is a column);
      // one strategy key per level × age bucket (VA100_12h, VA200_24h, …). Paper only; "the market when the bundles are out and new alpha may come in".
      if (created && mc != null && mc < 2e6) { const ageH = (t - created) / 3600; const bucket = ageH >= 12 && ageH < 24 ? "12h" : ageH >= 24 && ageH < 36 ? "24h" : ageH >= 36 && ageH < 48 ? "36h" : ageH >= 48 && ageH <= 14 * 24 ? "48h" : null;
        if (bucket) for (const lvl of [100000, 200000, 300000]) if (v5 >= lvl) { const ch = ins.run(`VA${lvl / 1000}_${bucket}`, mint, r.name ?? null, r.symbol ?? null, t, px, mc, v5, created, +((t - created) / 60).toFixed(1), N(r.holder_count), ex, r.launchpad_platform ?? r.launchpad ?? null, curve, JSON.stringify(r).slice(0, 1500)).changes; if (ch) volStat.entries++; } }
      for (const tier of TIERS) if (v5 >= tier.min) {
        const ch = ins.run(tier.k, mint, r.name ?? null, r.symbol ?? null, t, px, mc, v5, created, created ? +((t - created) / 60).toFixed(1) : null, N(r.holder_count), ex, r.launchpad_platform ?? r.launchpad ?? null, curve, JSON.stringify(r).slice(0, 1500)).changes;
        if (ch) volStat.entries++;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) { volStat.errors++; volStat.lastErr = "poll: " + e.message.slice(0, 160); }
}

async function candles(mint, from, to) {
  const d = await gget("/v1/market/token_kline", { chain: "sol", address: mint, resolution: "1m", from: String(from * 1000), to: String(to * 1000) });
  return (d?.list || (Array.isArray(d) ? d : [])).map((c) => ({ t: Math.floor(Number(c.time) / 1000), h: N(c.high), l: N(c.low), c: N(c.close) })).filter((c) => c.h > 0);
}
let scoring = false;
/** 0.8.8 (owner, 4 Nov 18:53 UK: "a time for each position at what time it was actually live"): fill the 40%-trail exit minute on trades scored before this column existed, without blanking them. */
let filling = false;
export async function volFillExitTime(db) {
  if (filling) return; filling = true;
  try {
    for (const p of db.prepare("SELECT id, mint, entry_ts, entry_price FROM vol_paper WHERE done=1 AND err IS NULL AND m_trail40 IS NULL AND x_trail40 IS NOT NULL AND entry_ts>=? ORDER BY entry_ts DESC LIMIT 15").all(now() - 3 * 86400)) {
      try {
        const e = p.entry_ts, path = [...await candles(p.mint, e - 60, e + 60 * 60), ...await candles(p.mint, e + 60 * 60, e + 121 * 60)].filter((c) => c.t >= e - 60).sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t).filter((c, i, a) => i === 0 || c.t !== a[i - 1].t);
        const after = path.filter((c) => c.t >= e - 60), X = (v) => v / p.entry_price; let hi = 1, t = null;
        for (const c of after.slice(1)) { if (X(c.l) <= hi * 0.6) { t = c.t; break; } hi = Math.max(hi, X(c.h)); }
        db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET m_trail40=? WHERE id=?").run(t ? +((t - e) / 60).toFixed(1) : -1, p.id); // −1 = still held at 2 h
      } catch (er) { if (/rate limited/i.test(er.message)) break; db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET m_trail40=-2 WHERE id=?").run(p.id); }
    }
  } catch {} finally { filling = false; }
}
export async function volScore(db) {
  if (scoring) return; scoring = true;
  try {
    for (const p of db.prepare("SELECT * FROM vol_paper WHERE done=0 AND entry_ts<=? ORDER BY entry_ts LIMIT 40").all(now() - SCORE_AFTER)) {
      try {
        const e = p.entry_ts, path = [...await candles(p.mint, e - 60, e + 60 * 60), ...await candles(p.mint, e + 60 * 60, e + 121 * 60)].filter((c) => c.t >= e - 60).sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t);
        const uniq = path.filter((c, i) => i === 0 || c.t !== path[i - 1].t);
        if (!uniq.length) { db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET done=1, err=? WHERE id=?").run("no candles", p.id); continue; }
        const X = (v) => v / p.entry_price, at = (m) => { const c = uniq.filter((x) => x.t <= e + m * 60).pop(); return c ? +X(c.c).toFixed(4) : null; };
        const after = uniq.filter((c) => c.t >= e - 60);
        let peak = 0, peakT = e; for (const c of after) if (X(c.h) > peak) { peak = X(c.h); peakT = c.t; }
        const lowBefore = Math.min(...after.filter((c) => c.t <= peakT).map((c) => X(c.l)));
        const tpsl = (tp, sl) => { for (const c of after.slice(1)) { if (X(c.l) <= sl) return sl; if (X(c.h) >= tp) return tp; } return X(after[after.length - 1].c); }; // the stop is checked first in a candle: the worse case
        let hi = 1, trail = null, trailT = null; for (const c of after.slice(1)) { if (X(c.l) <= hi * 0.6) { trail = hi * 0.6; trailT = c.t; break; } hi = Math.max(hi, X(c.h)); } if (trail == null) trail = X(after[after.length - 1].c); // 0.8.8: trailT = when the 40% trail sold (null = still held at 2 h)
        // 0.5.0: half at 2x, the rest on a 40% trail from the peak; −30% stop before 2x
        let half = null; { let hit = false, hi2 = 2; for (const c of after.slice(1)) { if (!hit) { if (X(c.l) <= 0.7) { half = 0.7; break; } if (X(c.h) >= 2) { hit = true; hi2 = Math.max(2, X(c.h)); } } else { if (X(c.l) <= hi2 * 0.6) { half = 1 + 0.5 * hi2 * 0.6; break; } hi2 = Math.max(hi2, X(c.h)); } }
          if (half == null) half = hit ? 1 + 0.5 * X(after[after.length - 1].c) : X(after[after.length - 1].c); }
        const cv = p.curve === 1, t2 = tpsl(2, 0.5), t15 = tpsl(1.5, 0.7), h60 = at(60), h120 = at(120);
        // 0.8.0: hard out at minute M (winners peak at ~16 min and are worthless by 30), −35% stop before it; the stop is checked first in a candle
        const out = (M) => { for (const c of after.slice(1)) { if (c.t > e + M * 60) break; if (X(c.l) <= 0.65) return 0.65; } const c = uniq.filter((x) => x.t <= e + M * 60).pop(); return c ? +X(c.c).toFixed(4) : null; };
        const o10 = out(10), o15 = out(15), o20 = out(20);
        // 0.8.7 (owner, 4 Nov 18:21 UK: "more potential exit points… find the maximum"): trails of 25% and 60%, and tight-then-wide — a −35% stop until the coin has doubled, then a 55% trail off the high
        const trailN = (pct) => { let h = 1; for (const c of after.slice(1)) { if (X(c.l) <= h * (1 - pct)) return h * (1 - pct); h = Math.max(h, X(c.h)); } return X(after[after.length - 1].c); };
        const tw = (() => { let h = 1, run = false; for (const c of after.slice(1)) { if (!run) { if (X(c.l) <= 0.65) return 0.65; if (X(c.h) >= 2) { run = true; h = X(c.h); } } else { if (X(c.l) <= h * 0.45) return h * 0.45; h = Math.max(h, X(c.h)); } } return X(after[after.length - 1].c); })();
        const t25 = trailN(0.25), t60 = trailN(0.6);
        // 0.8.16: the venue exit — the 40% trail on the curve, the 25% trail once graduated — with its sale minute (m_venue; −1 = still held at 2 h)
        const vn = (() => { if (cv) return { x: trail, t: trailT }; let h = 1; for (const c of after.slice(1)) { if (X(c.l) <= h * 0.75) return { x: h * 0.75, t: c.t }; h = Math.max(h, X(c.h)); } return { x: X(after[after.length - 1].c), t: null }; })();
        db.prepare(`UPDATE vol_paper SET done=1, x5=?, x15=?, x30=?, x60=?, x120=?, peak120=?, peak_min=?, low_before_peak=?, x_tp2sl50=?, x_tp15sl30=?, x_trail40=?, r_tp2sl50=?, r_tp15sl30=?, r_trail40=?, r_hold60=?, r_hold120=?, x_half2=?, r_half2=?, x_out10=?, x_out15=?, x_out20=?, r_out10=?, r_out15=?, r_out20=?, x_trail25=?, x_trail60=?, x_tw=?, r_trail25=?, r_trail60=?, r_tw=?, m_trail40=?, x_venue=?, r_venue=?, m_venue=? WHERE id=?`)
          .run(at(5), at(15), at(30), h60, h120, +peak.toFixed(4), +((peakT - e) / 60).toFixed(1), +lowBefore.toFixed(4), t2, t15, +trail.toFixed(4), realX(t2, cv), realX(t15, cv), realX(trail, cv), realX(h60, cv), realX(h120, cv), +half.toFixed(4), realX(half, cv), o10, o15, o20, realX(o10, cv), realX(o15, cv), realX(o20, cv), +t25.toFixed(4), +t60.toFixed(4), +tw.toFixed(4), realX(t25, cv), realX(t60, cv), realX(tw, cv), trailT ? +((trailT - e) / 60).toFixed(1) : -1, +vn.x.toFixed(4), realX(vn.x, cv), vn.t ? +((vn.t - e) / 60).toFixed(1) : -1, p.id);
        try { db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET q_exit_gap=q_exit_x/x_venue-1 WHERE id=? AND q_exit_x IS NOT NULL AND q_exit_gap IS NULL AND x_venue>0").run(p.id); } catch {} // 0.8.16: a poll exit on a trade the mark never sold, against the scored venue exit
        volStat.scored++;
      } catch (er) { if (/rate limited/i.test(er.message)) throw er; db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET done=1, err=? WHERE id=?").run(er.message.slice(0, 120), p.id); }
    }
  } catch (e) { volStat.errors++; volStat.lastErr = "score: " + e.message.slice(0, 160); }
  finally { scoring = false; }
}

// 0.7.2: the VA backfill — a back-test, kept apart from the live book ("BF-VA…"). Every tracker coin that peaked ≥ $50K: 5-minute candles over the last
// 14 days (GMGN), the first crossing of $100K / $200K / $300K 5-min volume while 12 h–14 d old and under $2M market cap becomes a paper entry at that
// candle's close; scored by the same volScore from the same candles. ~1,000 coins × 3 pages. Runs once, 40 coins a tick, in the background.
export const bfStat = { coins: 0, done: 0, entries: 0, errors: 0, lastErr: "", finished: false };
let bfList = null;
export async function volBackfill(db, tdb) {
  if (!tdb || bfStat.finished) return;
  try {
    if (!bfList) { const since = now() - 15 * 86400; bfList = tdb.prepare("SELECT mint, name, symbol, create_ts, peak_mc_usd FROM tokens WHERE peak_mc_usd>=50000 AND create_ts>=? ORDER BY peak_mc_usd DESC LIMIT 1500").all(since); bfStat.coins = bfList.length;
      const seen = new Set(db.prepare("SELECT mint FROM vol_bf_done").all().map((r) => r.mint)); bfList = bfList.filter((c) => !seen.has(c.mint)); bfStat.done = seen.size; } // 0.7.4: coins scanned before a restart are not scanned again
    const ins = db.prepare(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO vol_paper (strat, mint, name, symbol, entry_ts, entry_price, entry_mc, vol5, created_ts, age_min, holders, exchange, launchpad, curve, row) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`);
    let k = 0;
    while (bfList.length && k < n("LAUNCH_BF_PER_TICK", 8)) { // 0.7.7: was 40 a tick (~1,700 GMGN calls every 2 min) — it was starving the study
      const c = bfList.shift(); k++;
      try {
        const from = Math.max(c.create_ts + 12 * 3600, now() - 14 * 86400), to = Math.min(now() - 125 * 60, c.create_ts + 14 * 86400); if (to <= from) { bfStat.done++; continue; }
        const all = [];
        for (let a = from; a < to; a += 100 * 300) { const d = await gget("/v1/market/token_kline", { chain: "sol", address: c.mint, resolution: "5m", from: String(a * 1000), to: String(Math.min(to, a + 100 * 300) * 1000) }); all.push(...(d?.list || (Array.isArray(d) ? d : []))); }
        const rows = all.map((x) => ({ t: Math.floor(Number(x.time) / 1000), c: N(x.close), v: N(x.volume) })).filter((x) => x.c > 0 && x.v != null).sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t);
        const hit = {};
        for (const x of rows) { const ageH = (x.t - c.create_ts) / 3600, mc = x.c * 1e9; if (ageH < 12 || ageH > 14 * 24 || !(mc < 2e6)) continue;
          const bucket = ageH < 24 ? "12h" : ageH < 36 ? "24h" : ageH < 48 ? "36h" : "48h";
          for (const lvl of [100000, 200000, 300000]) { const key = `BF-VA${lvl / 1000}_${bucket}`; if (x.v >= lvl && !hit[key]) { hit[key] = 1; if (ins.run(key, c.mint, c.name, c.symbol, x.t, x.c, mc, x.v, c.create_ts, +((x.t - c.create_ts) / 60).toFixed(1), null, "backfill", null, 0, JSON.stringify({ backfill: true, peak_mc_usd: c.peak_mc_usd })).changes) bfStat.entries++; } } }
        bfStat.done++; db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO vol_bf_done VALUES (?)").run(c.mint);
      } catch (e) { bfStat.errors++; bfStat.lastErr = `${c.mint.slice(0, 6)} ${e.message}`.slice(0, 140); if (/rate limited/i.test(e.message)) { bfList.unshift(c); break; } bfStat.done++; }
    }
    if (!bfList.length) bfStat.finished = true;
  } catch (e) { bfStat.errors++; bfStat.lastErr = e.message.slice(0, 140); }
}

/** 0.8.15: sell quote for the tokens the buy quote said we would hold; q_exit_x = SOL back ÷ SOL staked, q_exit_gap = q_exit_x ÷ paper exit multiple − 1 */
const SHADOW_SOL = Number(process.env.LAUNCH_SHADOW_SOL || 0.2);
async function shadowSell(db, r, paperX, soldT, src = "mark") {
  try { const q = await sellQuote(r.mint, r.q_tokens); const x = q.lamports / 1e9 / SHADOW_SOL; qStat.sells++;
    db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET q_exit_ts=?, q_exit_sol=?, q_exit_x=?, q_exit_gap=?, q_exit_delay=?, q_exit_impact=?, q_exit_src=? WHERE id=? AND q_exit_x IS NULL").run(now(), +(q.lamports / 1e9).toFixed(4), +x.toFixed(4), +(x / paperX - 1).toFixed(4), now() - soldT, q.impact, src, r.id);
  } catch (e) { qStat.errors++; qStat.lastErr = `sell ${r.mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 120); if (!/paused/.test(e.message)) db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET q_exit_ts=?, q_exit_err=? WHERE id=? AND q_exit_x IS NULL").run(now(), e.message.slice(0, 80), r.id); }
}

/** 0.8.16 (owner, 5 Nov 04:22 UK): the OPEN-POSITION QUOTE POLL — what a live bot running the venue trail off its own sell quotes would have got.
 *  Every 3 s one Jupiter sell quote for the open shadow position that was quoted longest ago (all the books' rows of that coin share it): the quoted
 *  x (SOL back ÷ SOL staked), its high since entry (from 1 = the entry, like the paper trail) and, the first quote at or under high × (1 − trail), the
 *  quoted exit (q_exit_* with q_exit_src = 'poll', delay 0). The candle mark's quote ('mark') is only taken when the poll has not sold first.
 *  With N open coins each is quoted every 3N seconds; 30 keyless requests a minute leave room for the entry quotes. */
let polling = false;
export async function volQuotePoll(db) {
  if (polling || Date.now() < qStat.pauseUntil) return; polling = true;
  try {
    const t = now();
    const first = db.prepare("SELECT mint FROM vol_paper WHERE done=0 AND err IS NULL AND q_tokens>0 AND q_exit_ts IS NULL AND entry_ts>=? AND entry_ts<=? ORDER BY (q_live_ts IS NOT NULL), q_live_ts, entry_ts LIMIT 1").get(t - 125 * 60, t - 20);
    if (!first) return;
    const rs = db.prepare("SELECT id, mint, curve, q_tokens, q_live_hi FROM vol_paper WHERE mint=? AND done=0 AND err IS NULL AND q_tokens>0 AND q_exit_ts IS NULL").all(first.mint);
    if (!rs.length) return;
    const base = rs[0]; qStat.polls++;
    let q; try { q = await sellQuote(base.mint, base.q_tokens); }
    catch (e) { qStat.errors++; qStat.lastErr = `poll ${base.mint.slice(0, 6)}: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 120);
      if (!/paused|429/.test(e.message)) for (const r of rs) db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET q_live_ts=?, q_live_n=COALESCE(q_live_n,0)+1 WHERE id=?").run(t, r.id); // a coin with no route (rugged curve) is not retried before the others
      return; }
    for (const r of rs) {
      const x = q.lamports / 1e9 / SHADOW_SOL * (r.q_tokens / base.q_tokens), hi = Math.max(r.q_live_hi || 1, x), keep = 1 - trailPct(r.curve);
      if (x <= (r.q_live_hi || 1) * keep) { // the trail off the quoted high fires on this quote: this is the quoted exit (compared with the paper exit by the page, once the mark or the score has it)
        db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET q_live_ts=?, q_live_x=?, q_live_hi=?, q_live_n=COALESCE(q_live_n,0)+1, q_exit_ts=?, q_exit_sol=?, q_exit_x=?, q_exit_delay=0, q_exit_impact=?, q_exit_src='poll' WHERE id=? AND q_exit_x IS NULL")
          .run(t, +x.toFixed(4), +hi.toFixed(4), t, +(q.lamports / 1e9 * (r.q_tokens / base.q_tokens)).toFixed(4), +x.toFixed(4), q.impact, r.id); qStat.pollExits++; qStat.sells++;
      } else db.prepare("UPDATE vol_paper SET q_live_ts=?, q_live_x=?, q_live_hi=?, q_live_n=COALESCE(q_live_n,0)+1 WHERE id=?").run(t, +x.toFixed(4), +hi.toFixed(4), r.id);
    }
  } catch (e) { qStat.errors++; qStat.lastErr = "poll: " + e.message.slice(0, 120); }
  finally { polling = false; }
}

=== FILE: launcher/src/quote.mjs (5,319 chars)

// launcher 0.8.15 (owner, 5 Nov 2026 01:04 UK, after Codex's second opinion: "measure executable quotes now, not after a book passes 40 trades"):
// QUOTE SHADOWING. Nothing is sent. At the moment a rule books a paper entry we ask Jupiter for a real swap quote (0.2 SOL → the coin) and keep
// the price it would have filled at next to the chain price the paper book used; when the live mark says the 40% trail would have sold we ask for the
// sell quote (those tokens → SOL) and keep it next to the paper exit. A sample of launches that passed a check but entered no book is quoted too,
// so the fill cost of the coins we avoid is known as well. The gap between paper and quote is the thing every paper number depends on.
// Jupiter's public quote API (no key) routes pump.fun curve coins and PumpSwap pools; the quote includes price impact for the size asked.
// 0.8.16: the keyless tier is 30 requests a minute (60-s sliding window); a 429 pauses every quote until x-ratelimit-reset (or 15 s), and the
// open-position poller (volpaper volQuotePoll) spends at most one request every 3 s so entries and exits keep their share.
// 0.8.17: the same quote endpoint feeds the LIVE trades (live.mjs) — jqRaw keeps Jupiter's whole quote, which its /swap endpoint takes back to build the transaction.
export const qStat = { buys: 0, sells: 0, samples: 0, polls: 0, pollExits: 0, paused: 0, errors: 0, lastErr: "", lastMs: 0, pauseUntil: 0, swaps: 0 };
export const SOL = "So11111111111111111111111111111111111111112", HOST = process.env.LAUNCH_JUP_HOST || "https://lite-api.jup.ag";
function limited(r) { if (r.status === 429) { const rs = Number(r.headers.get("x-ratelimit-reset")) || 0; qStat.pauseUntil = rs > 1e9 ? rs * 1000 : Date.now() + 15000; throw new Error("jup 429 rate limit"); } }
/** Jupiter's quote as it comes (outAmount, priceImpactPct, routePlan…). slippageBps = what the swap built from it will tolerate. */
export async function jqRaw(inputMint, outputMint, amount, slippageBps = 1000, direct = false) {
  if (Date.now() < qStat.pauseUntil) { qStat.paused++; throw new Error("jup paused (429)"); }
  const t0 = Date.now();
  // 0.8.20: direct = one hop only (SOL ↔ coin on pump.fun or PumpSwap) with the account count capped — the first live buy (StockPad, 08:15 UK) got a
  // two-hop transaction of 1668 bytes, over Solana's 1232, and failed in simulation. The live trades never want a multi-hop route anyway.
  const u = `${HOST}/swap/v1/quote?inputMint=${inputMint}&outputMint=${outputMint}&amount=${amount}&slippageBps=${slippageBps}&onlyDirectRoutes=${direct ? "true" : "false"}${direct ? "&maxAccounts=28&restrictIntermediateTokens=true" : ""}`;
  const r = await fetch(u, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(6000), headers: { accept: "application/json" } });
  limited(r);
  const text = await r.text(); let j; try { j = JSON.parse(text); } catch { throw new Error(`jup HTTP ${r.status}`); }
  if (!r.ok || j.error) throw new Error(`jup ${r.status} ${(j.error || j.errorCode || "").toString().slice(0, 60)}`);
  qStat.lastMs = Date.now() - t0; j._ms = Date.now() - t0;
  return j;
}
async function jq(inputMint, outputMint, amount) {
  const j = await jqRaw(inputMint, outputMint, amount, 1000);
  return { out: Number(j.outAmount), impact: Number(j.priceImpactPct) || 0, route: (j.routePlan || []).map((p) => p?.swapInfo?.label).filter(Boolean).join(">").slice(0, 40), ms: j._ms };
}
/** 0.8.17: the swap transaction Jupiter builds for a quote (base64, unsigned; our wallet is the fee payer and signer). Counts against the same keyless limit. */
export async function swapTxFor(quote, userPublicKey, maxPrioLamports = 1_000_000) {
  if (Date.now() < qStat.pauseUntil) { qStat.paused++; throw new Error("jup paused (429)"); }
  const r = await fetch(`${HOST}/swap/v1/swap`, { method: "POST", signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000), headers: { accept: "application/json", "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ quoteResponse: quote, userPublicKey, wrapAndUnwrapSol: true, dynamicComputeUnitLimit: true, dynamicSlippage: false, prioritizationFeeLamports: { priorityLevelWithMaxLamports: { maxLamports: maxPrioLamports, global: false, priorityLevel: "high" } } }) });
  limited(r);
  const text = await r.text(); let j; try { j = JSON.parse(text); } catch { throw new Error(`jup swap HTTP ${r.status}`); }
  if (!r.ok || !j.swapTransaction) throw new Error(`jup swap ${r.status} ${(j.error || j.errorCode || "").toString().slice(0, 80)}`);
  qStat.swaps++;
  return { tx64: j.swapTransaction, lastValidBlockHeight: Number(j.lastValidBlockHeight) || null, prio: Number(j.prioritizationFeeLamports) || null };
}
/** buy quote: lamports of SOL in → raw tokens out (6 decimals on pump.fun coins). Returns the SOL price per token implied by the fill. */
export async function buyQuote(mint, lamports) {
  const q = await jq(SOL, mint, lamports);
  if (!(q.out > 0)) throw new Error("jup empty");
  return { tokens: q.out, pxSol: (lamports / 1e9) / (q.out / 1e6), impact: q.impact, route: q.route, ms: q.ms };
}
/** sell quote: raw tokens in → lamports out */
export async function sellQuote(mint, tokens) {
  const q = await jq(mint, SOL, Math.floor(tokens));
  if (!(q.out > 0)) throw new Error("jup empty");
  return { lamports: q.out, impact: q.impact, route: q.route, ms: q.ms };
}

// RPC plumbing (copied from executor/src/rpc.mjs, launcher 0.1.0): Helius connection, blockhash cache, build / simulate / send (Helius Sender first, plain RPC as fallback), confirmation, fill readback.
import { Connection, VersionedTransaction, TransactionMessage, PublicKey } from "@solana/web3.js";
import { CFG } from "./config.mjs";

export const conn = new Connection(CFG.RPC_URL, { commitment: "confirmed", disableRetryOnRateLimit: true });
export const rpcStats = { calls: 0, errors: 0, sends: 0, senderErrors: 0, lastErr: "" };

let bh = { blockhash: null, lastValidBlockHeight: 0, t: 0 }, bhBusy = false;
export async function blockhash(maxAgeMs = 1500) {
  if (bh.blockhash && Date.now() - bh.t < maxAgeMs) return bh;
  if (bhBusy) { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50)); return bh.blockhash ? bh : blockhash(maxAgeMs); }
  bhBusy = true; try { rpcStats.calls++; const r = await conn.getLatestBlockhash("confirmed"); bh = { ...r, t: Date.now() }; } catch (e) { rpcStats.errors++; rpcStats.lastErr = e.message; } finally { bhBusy = false; }
  if (!bh.blockhash) throw new Error("no blockhash: " + rpcStats.lastErr); return bh;
}
export async function getAccounts(pubkeys, commitment = "processed") {
  rpcStats.calls++; try { return await conn.getMultipleAccountsInfo(pubkeys.map((p) => (p instanceof PublicKey ? p : new PublicKey(p))), { commitment }); } catch (e) { rpcStats.errors++; rpcStats.lastErr = e.message; throw e; }
}
/** v0.4.12 local stops: push notifications for an account (the bonding curve, or a pool's reserve accounts) at 'processed' commitment - the executor sees the price move within a slot. */
export function onAccount(pubkey, cb) { rpcStats.subs = (rpcStats.subs || 0) + 1; return conn.onAccountChange(pubkey instanceof PublicKey ? pubkey : new PublicKey(pubkey), (info, ctx) => { try { cb(info.data, ctx?.slot); } catch (e) { rpcStats.lastErr = "sub: " + e.message; } }, "processed"); }
export async function offAccount(id) { try { await conn.removeAccountChangeListener(id); rpcStats.subs = Math.max(0, (rpcStats.subs || 1) - 1); } catch {} }
export async function getBalance(pubkey) { rpcStats.calls++; return conn.getBalance(pubkey, "confirmed"); }
export async function buildTx(payer, ixs, signer = null, tables = []) {
  const { blockhash: b, lastValidBlockHeight } = await blockhash();
  const msg = new TransactionMessage({ payerKey: payer, recentBlockhash: b, instructions: ixs }).compileToV0Message(tables); // launcher 0.1.15: lookup tables shrink the launch transaction
  const tx = new VersionedTransaction(msg); tx.lastValidBlockHeight = lastValidBlockHeight; if (signer) tx.sign(Array.isArray(signer) ? signer : [signer]); return tx;
}
/** Simulate (no signature needed). Returns { ok, err, logs, units, ms }. */
/** Simulate; `watch` = accounts whose post-simulation state we want back (e.g. our token account, to read the real fill the chain would give). */
export async function simulate(tx, watch = []) {
  const t0 = Date.now(); rpcStats.calls++;
  try {
    const cfg = { sigVerify: false, replaceRecentBlockhash: true, commitment: "processed" }; if (watch.length) cfg.accounts = { encoding: "base64", addresses: watch.map((k) => k.toBase58()) };
    const r = await conn.simulateTransaction(tx, cfg);
    const post = (r.value.accounts || []).map((a) => (a && a.data ? Buffer.from(a.data[0], "base64") : null));
    return { ok: !r.value.err, err: r.value.err ? JSON.stringify(r.value.err) : null, logs: r.value.logs || [], units: r.value.unitsConsumed ?? null, ms: Date.now() - t0, post };
  } catch (e) { rpcStats.errors++; rpcStats.lastErr = e.message; return { ok: false, err: "rpc: " + e.message, logs: [], units: null, ms: Date.now() - t0, post: [] }; }
}
/** Send a signed transaction through Helius Sender (every landing route at once); falls back to the normal RPC. Returns { sig, via, ms }. */
export async function send(tx) {
  const raw = Buffer.from(tx.serialize()).toString("base64"), t0 = Date.now(); rpcStats.sends++;
  try {
    const r = await fetch(CFG.SENDER_URL, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4000),
      body: JSON.stringify({ id: String(Date.now()), jsonrpc: "2.0", method: "sendTransaction", params: [raw, { encoding: "base64", skipPreflight: true, maxRetries: 0 }] }) });
    const j = await r.json(); if (j.result) return { sig: j.result, via: "sender", ms: Date.now() - t0 };
    throw new Error(j.error?.message || `sender http ${r.status}`);
  } catch (e) {
    rpcStats.senderErrors++; rpcStats.lastErr = "sender: " + e.message;
    const sig = await conn.sendRawTransaction(tx.serialize(), { skipPreflight: true, maxRetries: 0 }); return { sig, via: "rpc", ms: Date.now() - t0, senderErr: e.message };
  }
}
/** Send, then keep re-sending the SAME signed transaction every 2.5 s until it is confirmed / failed or `ms` passes. Re-sending the identical
 *  transaction can never execute twice, so this is the only retry a BUY gets (a second, different buy could land as well → bought twice). */
export async function sendUntilConfirmed(tx, ms = 18000) {
  const first = await send(tx); const sig = first.sig, t0 = Date.now(); let resends = 0;
  while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) {
    const c = await waitConfirm(sig, 2500); if (c.status !== "timeout") return { ...c, sig, via: first.via, resends, ms: Date.now() - t0 };
    try { await send(tx); resends++; } catch {}
  }
  return { status: "timeout", slot: null, err: null, sig, via: first.via, resends, ms: Date.now() - t0 };
}
/** Poll until the signature is confirmed or failed, or `ms` passes. Returns { status: 'confirmed'|'failed'|'timeout', slot, err }. */
export async function waitConfirm(sig, ms = 20000) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) {
    rpcStats.calls++;
    try { const r = await conn.getSignatureStatuses([sig]); const s = r.value[0];
      if (s) { if (s.err) return { status: "failed", slot: s.slot, err: JSON.stringify(s.err) }; if (s.confirmationStatus === "confirmed" || s.confirmationStatus === "finalized") return { status: "confirmed", slot: s.slot, err: null }; } }
    catch (e) { rpcStats.errors++; rpcStats.lastErr = e.message; }
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200)); // v0.4.11: notice the landing sooner (was 400 ms)
  }
  return { status: "timeout", slot: null, err: null };
}
export async function blockHeight() { rpcStats.calls++; return conn.getBlockHeight("confirmed"); }
export async function signatureStatus(sig) { rpcStats.calls++; const r = await conn.getSignatureStatuses([sig]); const s = r.value[0]; return s ? { status: s.err ? "failed" : (s.confirmationStatus === "confirmed" || s.confirmationStatus === "finalized") ? "confirmed" : "processed", slot: s.slot, err: s.err ? JSON.stringify(s.err) : null } : { status: "unknown" }; }
/** What actually happened: SOL change for the payer (lamports, fee included) and token change for the mint. Retries a few times — the node can lag the confirmation by a moment. */
export async function readFill(sig, payer, mint, tries = 4) {
  let tx = null; for (let i = 0; i < tries && !tx?.meta; i++) { if (i) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600)); rpcStats.calls++; tx = await conn.getTransaction(sig, { maxSupportedTransactionVersion: 1, commitment: "confirmed" }).catch(() => null); }
  if (!tx?.meta) return null;
  const keys = tx.transaction.message.staticAccountKeys.map((k) => k.toBase58()), i = keys.indexOf(payer.toBase58());
  const solDelta = i >= 0 ? tx.meta.postBalances[i] - tx.meta.preBalances[i] : null;
  const tok = (arr) => (arr || []).filter((b) => b.mint === mint.toBase58() && b.owner === payer.toBase58()).reduce((a, b) => a + Number(b.uiTokenAmount.amount), 0);
  return { solDelta, tokenDelta: tok(tx.meta.postTokenBalances) - tok(tx.meta.preTokenBalances), fee: tx.meta.fee, slot: tx.slot, blockTime: tx.blockTime ?? null, err: tx.meta.err ? JSON.stringify(tx.meta.err) : null };
}

// launcher 0.1.0 (owner, 24 Oct 2026 20:08–20:15 UK): "get the tweet, make the coin, put 0.05 SOL in … if no one buys within 10 minutes, sell it".
// Every post of the accounts in the owner's TwitterAPI.io Stream (originals, replies, quotes, reposts) → a name and ticker (rules, or AI when a key is set)

// ---------- newness and copycats (tracker database, read-only) ----------
function sameBefore(name, ticker, beforeS) {
  if (!tdb) return { n: null, t: null };
  try { return { n: tdb.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM tokens WHERE create_ts BETWEEN ? AND ? AND lower(name)=lower(?)").get(beforeS - 7 * 86400, beforeS, name).n,
    t: tdb.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM tokens WHERE create_ts BETWEEN ? AND ? AND upper(symbol)=upper(?)").get(beforeS - 7 * 86400, beforeS, ticker).n }; } catch { return { n: null, t: null }; }
}
function copycats(L) {
  if (!tdb) return null; const t0 = Math.floor(L.t_post_ms / 1000);
  const rows = tdb.prepare("SELECT mint, create_ts, peak_mc_usd, graduated FROM tokens WHERE create_ts BETWEEN ? AND ? AND (lower(name)=lower(?) OR upper(symbol)=upper(?)) AND mint<>?").all(t0 - 60, t0 + 1800, L.name, L.ticker, L.mint || "");
  return { n: rows.length, first: rows.length ? Math.min(...rows.map((r) => r.create_ts)) - t0 : null, best: rows.length ? Math.max(...rows.map((r) => r.peak_mc_usd || 0)) : null, grads: rows.reduce((a, r) => a + (r.graduated ? 1 : 0), 0) };
}

// ---------- one post → one launch ----------
const seen = new Set(); let chain = Promise.resolve();
function onPost(t, ev) {
  if (seen.has(t.id)) return; seen.add(t.id); if (seen.size > 5000) seen.clear();
  const recv = Date.now(); st.posts++;
  const rawKeep = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM posts").get().n < 200 ? JSON.stringify(ev).slice(0, 6000) : null; // the first 200 raw events are kept, to check the stream's fields
  db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO posts (id, user, text, created_ms, recv_ms, raw) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)").run(t.id, t.user, t.text, t.createdMs, recv, rawKeep);
  if (CFG.ACCOUNTS.length && !CFG.ACCOUNTS.includes(t.user.toLowerCase())) { st.ignored++; return; }
  chain = chain.then(() => handle(t, recv)).catch((e) => { st.errors++; st.lastErr = "post: " + e.message; });
}
async function handle(t, recv) {
  const c = await context(t); const ctxMs = Date.now();
  const p = c.parent, kind = c.kind;
  db.prepare("UPDATE posts SET kind=?, parent_id=?, parent_user=?, parent_text=?, ctx_ms=? WHERE id=?").run(kind, p?.id || null, p?.user || null, p?.text || null, ctxMs - recv, t.id);
  if (kind === "repost" && CFG.SKIP_RETWEETS) return;
  const ownText = kind === "repost" ? "" : c.tweet.text, subjectText = p ? p.text : "";
  // 0.1.8 (owner, 31 Oct 01:00 UK: "the picture has to be identical … you must look at the tweet and see the word"): the post's own pictures
  // (or, for a reply / quote / repost, the original's) are the coin's picture and are shown to the AI, which picks the name from the words AND the pictures.
  const images = [...(kind === "repost" ? [] : c.tweet.media || []), ...(p?.media || [])].filter((u) => /^https:\/\//.test(u));
  const [rules, ai] = await Promise.all([ruleName({ ownText, subjectText }), aiName({ user: t.user, kind, ownText, subjectText, subjectUser: p?.user, images })]);
  // a picture post is never launched on a guessed name: without the AI reading the picture it is recorded only
  const pick = ai || (images.length ? null : rules);
  const base = { post_id: t.id, user: t.user, kind, t_post_ms: t.createdMs || snowMs(t.id), t_recv_ms: recv, t_named_ms: Date.now(), rule_name: rules?.name || null, rule_ticker: rules?.ticker || null,
    ai_name: ai?.name || null, ai_ticker: ai?.ticker || null, created_ts: now() };
  if (!pick) return insert({ ...base, mode: CFG.MODE, status: "skipped", image_url: images[0] || null, name: rules?.name || null, ticker: rules?.ticker || null, name_by: rules ? "rules (not used)" : null,
    reason: images.length ? (CFG.AI_KEY ? "the AI could not name it from the post and its picture" : "post has a picture — needs the AI to read it (no ANTHROPIC_API_KEY), not launched on a guessed name") : "no words to name a coin from" });
  // 0.1.7 (owner, 31 Oct 00:52 UK: "we don't want to launch it twice"): the first post of a narrative wins — a name or ticker we already launched
  // (or test-launched) in the last REPEAT_H hours is recorded as skipped. The Relaunch button still relaunches on purpose.
  const rep = db.prepare(`SELECT id, created_ts FROM launches WHERE (lower(name)=lower(?) OR upper(ticker)=upper(?)) AND created_ts>=? AND status IN ('building','sent','open','selling','stuck','sold','graduated','sim_ok','paper') ORDER BY id LIMIT 1`).get(pick.name, pick.ticker, now() - CFG.REPEAT_H * 3600);
  if (rep) return insert({ ...base, mode: CFG.MODE, status: "skipped", name: pick.name, ticker: pick.ticker, name_by: pick.by, reason: `already launched as #${rep.id} at ${new Date(rep.created_ts * 1000).toLocaleTimeString("en-GB", { timeZone: "Europe/London" })} UK (no repeats within ${CFG.REPEAT_H} h)` });
  const same = sameBefore(pick.name, pick.ticker, now());
  await launch({ ...base, name: pick.name, ticker: pick.ticker, name_by: pick.by, why: pick.why, score: pick.score, same_name_7d: same.n, same_ticker_7d: same.t, image_url: images[0] || null }, { tweetUrl: `https://x.com/${t.user}/status/${t.id}` });
}
/** The coin's picture: the post's own first picture, byte for byte; only a post with no picture at all gets the drawn ticker picture. */
async function pictureFor(L) {
  if (!L.image_url) return { bytes: coinImage(L.name, L.ticker), type: "image/png", ext: "png", from: "drawn" };
  if (L.image_url.startsWith("file:")) { const f = L.image_url.slice(5), ext = (f.split(".").pop() || "jpg").toLowerCase(); return { bytes: readFileSync(f), type: ext === "png" ? "image/png" : ext === "gif" ? "image/gif" : ext === "webp" ? "image/webp" : "image/jpeg", ext, from: "upload" }; } // 0.2.1: a picture uploaded on the Launch-now box
  const r = await fetch(L.image_url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(6000) }); if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`picture download HTTP ${r.status}`);
  const type = (r.headers.get("content-type") || "image/jpeg").split(";")[0], bytes = Buffer.from(await r.arrayBuffer());
  if (!/^image\//.test(type) || bytes.length < 500 || bytes.length > 15e6) throw new Error(`picture not usable (${type}, ${bytes.length} bytes)`);
  return { bytes, type, ext: type.includes("png") ? "png" : type.includes("gif") ? "gif" : type.includes("webp") ? "webp" : "jpg", from: "post" };
}
const insert = (o) => { const ks = Object.keys(o); const r = db.prepare(`INSERT INTO launches (${ks.join(",")}) VALUES (${ks.map(() => "?").join(",")})`).run(...ks.map((k) => o[k])); st.launches++;
  const id = Number(r.lastInsertRowid); if (o.name && !o.relaunch_of) try { scheduleCopies(db, { ...o, id }); } catch {} return id; };

async function launch(L, { tweetUrl, source = "X" }) {
  // mode for this one: live only when the owner set live, the day's cap isn't reached and the wallet can pay
  let mode = CFG.MODE, reason = null;
  if (mode === "live" && kvGet("paused") === "1") { mode = "paper"; reason = "paused (pause button)"; }
  if (mode === "live") {
    if (CFG.DAILY_CAP > 0 && liveToday() >= CFG.DAILY_CAP) { mode = "paper"; reason = `daily cap ${CFG.DAILY_CAP} reached`; }
    else { const b = await bal(true).catch(() => null); if (b == null || b < lam(CFG.MIN_BALANCE_SOL)) { mode = "paper"; reason = `wallet ${b == null ? "unreadable" : sol(b).toFixed(3) + " SOL"} below ${CFG.MIN_BALANCE_SOL}`; } }
  }
  const id = insert({ ...L, mode, status: mode === "paper" ? "paper" : "building", reason, stake_sol: CFG.STAKE_SOL });
  if (mode === "paper") return id;
  try {
    const description = L.user && L.user !== "manual" ? `${L.name} — narrative from @${L.user}'s post on ${source}${tweetUrl ? ": " + tweetUrl : ""} (not affiliated with @${L.user}).` : tweetUrl ? `${L.name} — ${tweetUrl}` : L.name;
    let uri;
    try { const img = await pictureFor(L); const m = await uploadMeta({ name: L.name, symbol: L.ticker, description, img, tweetUrl }); uri = m.uri; upd(id, { uri, upload_ms: m.ms, ...(m.image && (!L.image_url || L.image_url.startsWith("file:")) ? { image_url: m.image } : {}) }); }
    catch (e) { st.uploadsFailed++; upd(id, { status: mode === "live" ? "failed" : "sim_fail", reason: "picture / metadata upload: " + e.message.slice(0, 160) }); return id; }
    const g = await global(), mintKp = Keypair.generate(), mint = mintKp.publicKey;
    if (mode === "live" && !luts().length) await ensureLut({ wallet, kvGet, kvSet, wanted: wantedFor(ME, g, TIPS.map((t) => new PublicKey(t))) });
    const funded = mode === "live" || (await bal().catch(() => 0)) >= lam(CFG.MIN_BALANCE_SOL);
    const payer = funded ? ME : simPayer() || ME;
    const curve = freshCurve(g, payer), spend = lam(CFG.STAKE_SOL), q = quoteBuy(curve, g, spend), minTokens = (q.tokens * BigInt(Math.round((1 - CFG.SLIP) * 1e4))) / 10000n;
    upd(id, { mint: mint.toBase58(), tokens: q.tokens.toString(), net_lamports: q.net.toString(), entry_price: Number(curve.vSol + q.net) / Number(curve.vTok - q.tokens) });
    // classic buy first; if the chain refuses it in simulation, the v3 buy (what pump.fun's own site uses after create_v2)
    let ixs, sim, buyIx = kvGet("buyIx") || "classic", errs = [];
    for (const v of buyIx === "v3" ? ["v3", "classic"] : ["classic", "v3"]) {
      ixs = [...ixBudget(CFG.CU_LIMIT, CFG.CU_PRICE), ixCreateV2({ mint, user: payer, creator: payer, name: L.name, symbol: L.ticker, uri }),
        ...(v === "v3" ? ixBuyV3({ user: payer, mint, g, spend, minTokens }) : ixBuy({ user: payer, mint, curve, g, spend, minTokens }))];
      if (mode === "live") ixs.push(ixTransfer(payer, tipAccount(), lam(CFG.TIP_SOL)));
      sim = await simulate(await buildTx(payer, ixs, null, luts())); if (sim.ok) { buyIx = v; kvSet("buyIx", v); break; }
      errs.push(`${v}: ${sim.err} ${(sim.logs || []).filter((l) => /Error|error|failed|insufficient/i.test(l)).slice(-3).join(" | ")}`);
    }
    upd(id, { buy_ix: sim.ok ? buyIx : null, sim_err: sim.ok ? null : errs.join(" ‖ ").slice(0, 900), sim_units: sim.units });
    if (mode === "shadow") { upd(id, { status: sim.ok ? "sim_ok" : "sim_fail", reason: funded ? null : "simulated with a stand-in payer (launch wallet not funded)" }); sim.ok ? st.shadowOk++ : st.shadowFail++; return id; }
    if (!sim.ok) { upd(id, { status: "failed", reason: "simulation refused it — not sent" }); return id; }
    const tx = await buildTx(payer, ixs, [wallet, mintKp], luts()); upd(id, { t_sent_ms: Date.now(), status: "sent", sig: Buffer.from(tx.signatures[0]).length ? bs58(tx.signatures[0]) : null });
    confirmLater(id, tx, mint, q); // the next post doesn't wait for this one to confirm
  } catch (e) { st.errors++; st.lastErr = "launch: " + e.message; upd(id, { status: mode === "live" ? "failed" : "sim_fail", reason: e.message.slice(0, 200) }); }
  return id;
}
async function confirmLater(id, tx, mint, q) {
  try {
    const r = await sendUntilConfirmed(tx, 25000); upd(id, { sig: r.sig });
    if (r.status !== "confirmed") { upd(id, { status: "failed", reason: `transaction ${r.status}${r.err ? ": " + r.err.slice(0, 160) : ""}` }); return; }
    const fill = await readFill(r.sig, ME, mint);
    st.live++; balance = null;
    upd(id, { status: "open", t_landed_ms: Date.now(), cost_sol: fill ? sol(fill.solDelta) : null, tokens: fill ? String(fill.tokenDelta) : q.tokens.toString(), last_change_ts: now(), peak_x: 1, last_x: 1 });
    exportSoon();
  } catch (e) { st.errors++; st.lastErr = "confirm: " + e.message; upd(id, { status: "failed", reason: "confirm: " + e.message.slice(0, 160) }); }
}
const B58 = "123456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz";
const bs58 = (bytes) => { let n = 0n; for (const b of bytes) n = n * 256n + BigInt(b); let o = ""; while (n > 0n) { o = B58[Number(n % 58n)] + o; n /= 58n; } for (const b of bytes) { if (b) break; o = "1" + o; } return o; };
const TIPS = ["4ACfpUFoaSD9bfPdeu6DBt89gB6ENTeHBXCAi87NhDEE", "D2L6yPZ2FmmmTKPgzaMKdhu6EWZcTpLy1Vhx8uvZe7NZ", "9bnz4RShgq1hAnLnZbP8kbgBg1kEmcJBYQq3gQbmnSta", "5VY91ws6B2hMmBFRsXkoAAdsPHBJwRfBht4DXox3xkwn", "2nyhqdwKcJZR2vcqCyrYsaPVdAnFoJjiksCXJ7hfEYgD", "2q5pghRs6arqVjRvT5gfgWfWcHWmw1ZuCzphgd5KfWGJ", "wyvPkWjVZz1M8fHQnMMCDTQDbkManefNNhweYk5WkcF", "3KCKozbAaF75qEU33jtzozcJ29yJuaLJTy2jFdzUY8bT"];
const tipAccount = () => new PublicKey(TIPS[Math.floor(Math.random() * TIPS.length)]); // Helius Sender tip accounts (as the executor)

// ---------- watching our coins: sell back when nobody buys, or when it goes quiet ----------
let watching = false;
async function watch() {
  if (watching) return; watching = true;
  try {
    const open = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM launches WHERE mode='live' AND status IN ('open','selling') ORDER BY id").all(); if (!open.length) return;
    const g = await global(); const accs = await getAccounts(open.map((L) => curveOf(new PublicKey(L.mint))), "confirmed");
    for (let i = 0; i < open.length; i++) {
      const L = open[i], c = decodeCurve(accs[i]?.data); if (!c) continue;
      const age = now() - Math.floor((L.t_landed_ms || L.created_ts * 1000) / 1000), spent = lam(L.stake_sol || CFG.STAKE_SOL);
      const x = Number(quoteSell(c, g, BigInt(L.tokens || "0"))) / spent; // what our tokens would sell for now ÷ the stake (≈ 0.975 at the start: the 1.25% fees each way)
      const ours = BigInt(L.net_lamports || "0"), others = c.rSol > ours + 100000n; // anything beyond our own buy (0.0001 SOL slack) is someone else
      const changed = Number(c.rSol) !== Number(kvGet("rsol:" + L.id) ?? -1); if (changed) kvSet("rsol:" + L.id, Number(c.rSol));
      const o = { last_x: +x.toFixed(4), peak_x: Math.max(L.peak_x || 0, +x.toFixed(4)) };
      if (others && !L.others) { o.others = 1; o.first_other_s = age; }
      if (changed) o.last_change_ts = now();
      upd(L.id, o);
      if (c.complete) { upd(L.id, { status: "graduated", reason: "graduated — sell on PumpSwap by hand for now" }); continue; }
      const manual = L.status === "selling";
      // 0.1.11 (owner, 31 Oct 01:15 UK): at DEAD_MIN, count the buys by other wallets; fewer than MIN_BUYS → sell back (checked once)
      let buys = L.buys_10m; if (!manual && buys == null && age >= CFG.DEAD_MIN * 60) { buys = await countBuys(L).catch(() => null); if (buys != null) upd(L.id, { buys_10m: buys }); }
      const why = manual ? (L.sell_reason || "sell button") : buys != null && L.buys_10m == null && buys < CFG.MIN_BUYS ? `only ${buys} buy${buys === 1 ? "" : "s"} by others in ${CFG.DEAD_MIN} min (needs ${CFG.MIN_BUYS})`
        : buys == null && !others && !L.others && age >= CFG.DEAD_MIN * 60 ? `nobody else bought in ${CFG.DEAD_MIN} min`
        : (others || L.others) && now() - (changed ? now() : L.last_change_ts || now()) >= CFG.QUIET_MIN * 60 ? `no trades for ${CFG.QUIET_MIN} min`
        : null;
      if (why) await sellOne(L, c, g, why);
      else if (CFG.TP_X > 0 && !L.tp_done && x >= CFG.TP_X) await sellOne(L, c, g, `take-profit: ${Math.round(CFG.TP_FRACTION * 100)}% sold at ${CFG.TP_X}x`, CFG.TP_FRACTION);
    }
  } catch (e) { st.errors++; st.lastErr = "watch: " + e.message; } finally { watching = false; }
}
/** Buys by other wallets on our coin's curve so far: its transactions, those that ran a pump.fun buy and were not paid for by our wallet. */
async function countBuys(L) {
  const curve = curveOf(new PublicKey(L.mint)); rpcStats.calls++;
  const sigs = (await conn.getSignaturesForAddress(curve, { limit: 200 }, "confirmed")).filter((s) => !s.err && s.signature !== L.sig).map((s) => s.signature);
  let n = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < sigs.length; i += 25) {
    rpcStats.calls++; const txs = await conn.getTransactions(sigs.slice(i, i + 25), { maxSupportedTransactionVersion: 1, commitment: "confirmed" });
    for (const t of txs) { if (!t?.meta) continue; const payer = t.transaction.message.staticAccountKeys[0].toBase58();
      if (payer !== ME.toBase58() && (t.meta.logMessages || []).some((l) => /Instruction: (Buy|BuyExactSolIn|BuyV2|BuyV3|BuyExactQuoteIn)/.test(l))) n++; }
  }
  return n;
}
async function sellOne(L, curve, g, why, frac = 1) {
  const mint = new PublicKey(L.mint), ua = ata(ME, mint, TOKEN_2022);
  try {
    const [acc] = await getAccounts([ua], "confirmed"); const held = acc?.data?.length >= 72 ? acc.data.readBigUInt64LE(64) : 0n;
    const part = frac < 1, amount = part ? (held * BigInt(Math.round(frac * 1e4))) / 10000n : held;
    if (amount <= 0n) { upd(L.id, { status: "sold", sell_reason: why + " (no tokens left)", sold_ts: now() }); return; }
    const out = quoteSell(curve, g, amount), minSol = (out * BigInt(Math.round((1 - CFG.SELL_SLIP) * 1e4))) / 10000n;
    const sellIxs = L.buy_ix === "v3" ? ixSellV3({ user: ME, mint, g, amount, minSol, close: !part }) : ixSell({ user: ME, mint, curve, g, amount, minSol, close: !part });
    const tx = await buildTx(ME, [...ixBudget(200000, CFG.CU_PRICE), ...sellIxs, ixTransfer(ME, tipAccount(), lam(CFG.TIP_SOL))], [wallet], luts());
    const tries = (L.sell_tries || 0) + 1; if (!part) upd(L.id, { status: "selling", sell_reason: why, sell_tries: tries });
    const sim = await simulate(tx); // a sale the chain would refuse is not sent (a failed transaction still costs its fee)
    const r = sim.ok ? await sendUntilConfirmed(tx, 20000) : { status: "refused", err: sim.err };
    if (part) { // take-profit on part of the position: record it once, keep holding the rest under the normal rules
      if (r.status !== "confirmed") { st.lastErr = `take-profit ${L.ticker}: ${r.status} ${r.err || ""}`.slice(0, 200); return; }
      const f = await readFill(r.sig, ME, mint); upd(L.id, { tp_done: 1, tp_sol: f ? sol(f.solDelta) : null, tokens: (held - amount).toString(), reason: why }); balance = null; exportSoon(); return;
    }
    if (r.status !== "confirmed") { st.lastErr = `sell ${L.ticker}: ${r.status} ${r.err || ""}`.slice(0, 200); if (tries >= 6) upd(L.id, { status: "stuck", reason: "sell failed 6 times (press Sell to try again): " + String(r.err || r.status).slice(0, 120) }); return; }
    const fill = await readFill(r.sig, ME, mint); const got = fill ? sol(fill.solDelta) : null;
    upd(L.id, { status: "sold", sell_sig: r.sig, sell_sol: got, sold_ts: now(), pnl_sol: got != null && L.cost_sol != null ? +(got + (L.tp_sol || 0) + L.cost_sol).toFixed(6) : null });
    st.sells++; balance = null; exportSoon();
  } catch (e) { st.errors++; st.lastErr = "sell: " + e.message; }
}

// ---------- the website's buttons (scope writes a small JSON file into REQ_DIR; nothing else can reach this app) ----------
async function requests() {
  let files = []; try { files = readdirSync(CFG.REQ_DIR).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".json")); } catch { return; }
  for (const f of files) {
    let rq = null; try { rq = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${CFG.REQ_DIR}/${f}`, "utf8")); } catch {} try { unlinkSync(`${CFG.REQ_DIR}/${f}`); } catch {}
    if (!rq || now() - (rq.at || 0) > 300) continue;
    // 0.1.14 (owner, 31 Oct 01:26 UK): one button pauses every new launch at once; one button sells every coin we hold
    if (rq.action === "pause" || rq.action === "resume") { kvSet("paused", rq.action === "pause" ? "1" : "0"); kvSet("pausedAt", new Date().toISOString()); writeStatus(); continue; }
    if (rq.action === "sellall") { const r = db.prepare("UPDATE launches SET status='selling', sell_reason='sell-all button', sell_tries=0 WHERE mode='live' AND status IN ('open','stuck')").run(); kvSet("lastSellAll", `${new Date().toISOString()} ${r.changes} coin(s)`); writeStatus(); setTimeout(() => watch(), 50); continue; }
    // 0.2.1 (owner, 31 Oct 09:58 UK): the Launch-now box — "suggest" names a pasted post (AI reads its text and picture); "manual" launches it
    if (rq.action === "suggest" && rq.reqId) { suggest(rq).then((out) => writeFileSync(`${CFG.REQ_DIR}/resp-${rq.reqId}.out`, JSON.stringify(out))).catch((e) => writeFileSync(`${CFG.REQ_DIR}/resp-${rq.reqId}.out`, JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }))); continue; }
    if (rq.action === "study" && rq.mint) { studyAddress(db, String(rq.mint).trim(), exportNow); continue; } // 0.6.0: study a coin by address
    if (rq.action === "manual" && rq.reqId) { chain = chain.then(() => manualLaunch(rq)).catch((e) => { st.errors++; st.lastErr = "manual: " + e.message; }); continue; }
    if (!Number.isInteger(rq.id)) continue;
    const L = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM launches WHERE id=?").get(rq.id); if (!L) continue;
    if (rq.action === "sell" && L.mode === "live" && ["open", "stuck"].includes(L.status)) { upd(L.id, { status: "selling", sell_reason: "sell button", sell_tries: 0 }); setTimeout(() => watch(), 50); }
    if (rq.action === "relaunch" && L.name) {
      const same = sameBefore(L.name, L.ticker, now());
      chain = chain.then(() => launch({ post_id: L.post_id, user: L.user, kind: L.kind, name: L.name, ticker: L.ticker, name_by: L.name_by, why: "relaunch", score: L.score, rule_name: L.rule_name, rule_ticker: L.rule_ticker,
        ai_name: L.ai_name, ai_ticker: L.ai_ticker, same_name_7d: same.n, same_ticker_7d: same.t, image_url: L.image_url, t_post_ms: Date.now(), t_recv_ms: Date.now(), t_named_ms: Date.now(), relaunch_of: L.id, created_ts: now() },
        { tweetUrl: L.user && L.post_id ? `https://x.com/${L.user}/status/${L.post_id}` : "" })).catch((e) => { st.errors++; st.lastErr = "relaunch: " + e.message; });
    }
  }
}

// ---------- the Launch-now box (0.2.1) ----------
const srcOf = (link) => { const x = String(link || "").match(/(?:x|twitter)\.com\/([A-Za-z0-9_]{1,30})\/status\/(\d+)/), t = String(link || "").match(/truthsocial\.com\/@([A-Za-z0-9_]{1,40})\/(?:posts\/)?(\d+)/);
  return x ? { user: x[1], id: x[2], source: "X" } : t ? { user: t[1], id: t[2], source: "Truth Social" } : { user: "manual", id: null, source: "manual" }; };
function imagesOf(rq) {
  if (rq.imageFile && existsSync(rq.imageFile) && rq.imageFile.startsWith(`${HOME}/uploads/`)) { const ext = rq.imageFile.split(".").pop().toLowerCase(); return [{ b64: readFileSync(rq.imageFile).toString("base64"), type: ext === "png" ? "image/png" : ext === "gif" ? "image/gif" : ext === "webp" ? "image/webp" : "image/jpeg" }]; }
  return /^https:\/\//.test(rq.imageUrl || "") ? [rq.imageUrl] : [];
}
async function suggest(rq) {
  const text = String(rq.text || "").slice(0, 2000), s = srcOf(rq.link), images = imagesOf(rq);
  const ai = await aiName({ user: s.user, kind: "post", ownText: text, subjectText: "", images });
  const pick = ai || (images.length ? null : ruleName({ ownText: text, subjectText: "" }));
  if (!pick) return { ok: false, error: images.length ? "the AI could not name it (or is off) — type a name and ticker yourself" : "no words to name a coin from" };
  const rep = db.prepare(`SELECT id, created_ts FROM launches WHERE (lower(name)=lower(?) OR upper(ticker)=upper(?)) AND created_ts>=? AND status IN ('building','sent','open','selling','stuck','sold','graduated','sim_ok') ORDER BY id LIMIT 1`).get(pick.name, pick.ticker, now() - CFG.REPEAT_H * 3600);
  return { ok: true, name: pick.name, ticker: pick.ticker, score: pick.score ?? null, why: pick.why || "", by: pick.by, source: s.source, user: s.user, repeatOf: rep ? rep.id : null };
}
async function manualLaunch(rq) {
  const s = srcOf(rq.link), name = String(rq.name || "").trim().slice(0, 32), ticker = String(rq.ticker || "").replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g, "").toUpperCase().slice(0, 10);
  if (!name || ticker.length < 2) return;
  const pid = s.id ? `${s.source === "X" ? "" : "truth-"}${s.id}` : `manual-${rq.reqId}`;
  db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO posts (id, user, kind, text, created_ms, recv_ms) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)").run(pid, s.user, "manual", String(rq.text || "").slice(0, 2000), Date.now(), Date.now());
  const image_url = rq.imageFile && rq.imageFile.startsWith(`${HOME}/uploads/`) ? "file:" + rq.imageFile : /^https:\/\//.test(rq.imageUrl || "") ? rq.imageUrl : null;
  const same = sameBefore(name, ticker, now());
  await launch({ post_id: pid, user: s.user, kind: "manual", name, ticker, name_by: rq.nameBy === "ai" ? "ai (manual)" : "manual", why: `launched by hand from ${s.source}`, score: rq.score ?? null,
    t_post_ms: Date.now(), t_recv_ms: Date.now(), t_named_ms: Date.now(), image_url, src_link: String(rq.link || "").slice(0, 300) || null, same_name_7d: same.n, same_ticker_7d: same.t, created_ts: now() }, { tweetUrl: String(rq.link || "").slice(0, 300), source: s.source });
}

// ---------- housekeeping: copycats, creator fees, sweep to Phantom ----------
function copyJob() {
  try {
    for (const L of db.prepare("SELECT * FROM launches WHERE name IS NOT NULL AND copies_final=0 AND created_ts<=? ORDER BY id LIMIT 200").all(now() - 1800)) {
      const c = copycats(L); if (!c) return;
      upd(L.id, { copies_30m: c.n, copy_first_s: c.first, copy_best_peak_usd: c.best, copy_grads: c.grads, copies_checked_ts: now(), copies_final: now() - L.created_ts >= 6 * 3600 ? 1 : 0 });
    }
  } catch (e) { st.errors++; st.lastErr = "copycats: " + e.message; }
}
async function feesAndSweep() {
  if (CFG.MODE !== "live" || CFG.SMOKE) return;
  try {
    const vault = creatorVaultOf(ME), [v] = await getAccounts([vault], "confirmed"), rent = 890880; // a 0-byte account's rent-exempt minimum
    if (v && v.lamports - rent >= lam(CFG.FEE_COLLECT_MIN_SOL)) { const tx = await buildTx(ME, [...ixBudget(100000, CFG.CU_PRICE), ixCollectCreatorFee(ME)], [wallet]); const r = await sendUntilConfirmed(tx, 20000); kvSet("lastFeeCollect", `${new Date().toISOString()} ${r.status} ${sol(v.lamports - rent).toFixed(4)} SOL`); }
    if (db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM launches WHERE status IN ('building','sent','selling')").get().n) return; // never sweep in the middle of a launch or a sale
    const b = await bal(true), keep = lam(CFG.SWEEP_KEEP_SOL), extra = b - keep - 10000;
    if (extra >= lam(0.01)) { const tx = await buildTx(ME, [...ixBudget(5000, 1000), ixTransfer(ME, new PublicKey(PHANTOM), extra)], [wallet]); const r = await sendUntilConfirmed(tx, 20000); kvSet("lastSweep", `${new Date().toISOString()} ${r.status} ${sol(extra).toFixed(4)} SOL → ${PHANTOM}`); balance = null; }
  } catch (e) { st.errors++; st.lastErr = "fees/sweep: " + e.message; }
}

// ---------- self-test: build a create+buy for a test coin and simulate it (nothing signed); then one metadata upload ----------
async function selfTest() {
  const out = { at: new Date().toISOString() };
  try {
    const g = await global(); out.global = { feeBps: g.feeBps, creatorFeeBps: g.creatorFeeBps, initVSol: sol(g.initVSol), initVTok: Number(g.initVTok) / 1e6 };
    const payer = (await bal().catch(() => 0)) >= lam(CFG.MIN_BALANCE_SOL) ? ME : simPayer() || ME; out.payer = payer.equals(ME) ? "launch wallet" : "stand-in (launch wallet not funded)";
    const mint = Keypair.generate().publicKey, curve = freshCurve(g, payer), spend = lam(CFG.STAKE_SOL), q = quoteBuy(curve, g, spend);
    Object.assign(out, { quotedTokens: Number(q.tokens) / 1e6, pctSupply: +(Number(q.tokens) / Number(g.supply) * 100).toFixed(3) });
    for (const v of ["classic", "v3"]) {
      const ixs = [...ixBudget(CFG.CU_LIMIT, CFG.CU_PRICE), ixCreateV2({ mint, user: payer, creator: payer, name: "Launcher Test", symbol: "LTEST", uri: "https://ipfs.io/ipfs/QmTest" }),
        ...(v === "v3" ? ixBuyV3({ user: payer, mint, g, spend, minTokens: (q.tokens * 95n) / 100n }) : ixBuy({ user: payer, mint, curve, g, spend, minTokens: (q.tokens * 95n) / 100n }))];
      const sim = await simulate(await buildTx(payer, ixs, null, luts()), [ata(payer, mint, TOKEN_2022), payer]);
      out[v + "Bytes"] = (await buildTx(payer, [...ixs, ixTransfer(payer, tipAccount(), lam(CFG.TIP_SOL))], [Keypair.generate(), Keypair.generate()].slice(0, 0), luts())).serialize().length; // the size of a real launch with tip, signatures included (cap 1,232)
      const got = sim.post?.[0]?.length >= 72 ? sim.post[0].readBigUInt64LE(64) : null;
      out[v] = { ok: sim.ok, err: sim.err, units: sim.units, ms: sim.ms, simTokens: got != null ? Number(got) / 1e6 : null, logs: sim.ok ? sim.logs.filter((l) => /Instruction:/.test(l)).slice(0, 12) : sim.logs.slice(-14) };
      if (sim.ok && !kvGet("buyIx")) kvSet("buyIx", v);
    }
  } catch (e) { out.error = e.message; }
  // 0.1.9: the AI naming check on the owner's own example (31 Oct 00:51 UK): WhiteHouse "Dems in charge? Nothing scarier. 🎃" with its Goosebumps-style pictures
  if (CFG.AI_KEY) { const t0 = Date.now(); const r = await aiName({ user: "WhiteHouse", kind: "post", ownText: "Dems in charge? Nothing scarier. 🎃", subjectText: "", images: ["https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HV6yhmrWQAASiYk.jpg", "https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HV6yjnzW4AANlvW.jpg"] }); out.ai = r ? { ...r, ms: Date.now() - t0 } : { ok: false, ms: Date.now() - t0, err: aiStat.lastErr }; }
  // 0.1.6: is the TwitterAPI.io key itself valid? one REST lookup (15 credits) — tells "bad key" apart from "no Stream plan" when the websocket says 403
  if (CFG.TWITTER_KEY) try { const r = await fetch(`${CFG.REST}/twitter/tweets?tweet_ids=20`, { headers: { "X-API-Key": CFG.TWITTER_KEY }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(6000) }); const j = await r.json().catch(() => ({})); out.twitterRest = { http: r.status, ok: r.ok && j.status !== "error", tweets: (j.tweets || []).length, msg: String(j.msg || j.message || "").slice(0, 120) }; } catch (e) { out.twitterRest = { ok: false, err: e.message }; }
  try { const m = await uploadMeta({ name: "Launcher Test", symbol: "LTEST", description: "test upload — not launched", img: { bytes: coinImage("Launcher Test", "LTEST"), type: "image/png", ext: "png" }, tweetUrl: "" }); out.upload = { ok: true, ms: m.ms, uri: m.uri }; } catch (e) { out.upload = { ok: false, err: e.message }; }
  st.selfTest = out; kvSet("selfTest", JSON.stringify(out)); exportNow();
}

const csv = (rows) => { if (!rows.length) return ""; const cols = Object.keys(rows[0]); const e = (v) => (v == null ? "" : /[",\n]/.test(String(v)) ? `"${String(v).replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : String(v)); return [cols.join(","), ...rows.map((r) => cols.map((c) => e(r[c])).join(","))].join("\n"); };
export function status() {
  return { ...st, at: now(), wallet: ME.toBase58(), balanceSol: balance != null ? +sol(balance).toFixed(4) : null, liveToday: liveToday(), cap: CFG.DAILY_CAP || "none", minBalanceSol: CFG.MIN_BALANCE_SOL, tpFraction: CFG.TP_FRACTION, stakeSol: CFG.STAKE_SOL, deadMin: CFG.DEAD_MIN, quietMin: CFG.QUIET_MIN, tpX: CFG.TP_X,
    lut: lutStat, copies: copyStatus(), peaks: peakStat, study: studyStat, vol: volStat, mark: markStat, volBackfill: bfStat, rf: rfStat, manualStudy: manualStat, operators: opStat, creators: ctStat, fd: fdStat, snipers2: snStat, quotes: qStat, live: liveStat, chain: cfStat, farm: farmStat, sweep: sweepStat, paused: kvGet("paused") === "1", pausedAt: kvGet("pausedAt"), lastSellAll: kvGet("lastSellAll"), openCoins: db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM launches WHERE mode='live' AND status IN ('open','selling','stuck')").get().n,
    accounts: CFG.ACCOUNTS, twitter: { keySet: !!CFG.TWITTER_KEY, ...twStat }, ai: !!CFG.AI_KEY, aiStat, meta: metaStat, rpc: rpcStats, trackerDb: !!tdb, lastSweep: kvGet("lastSweep"), lastFeeCollect: kvGet("lastFeeCollect"), phantom: PHANTOM };
}

  } catch (e) { st.errors++; st.lastErr = "export: " + e.message; }
}

import { CFG } from "./config.js";
import { db, q } from "./db.js";
import { eventsFromLogs, lamports, rawTok, priceSol } from "./pump-decoder.js";
import { getSolUsd, solUsdCached } from "./price.js";
import { routeOf } from "./routes.js";
import { tg } from "./telegram.js";
import { getSocials, socialsLine, socialsAtLaunch } from "./socials.js";

/** In-memory token state. mint -> {create, slot, trades:[], finalized, tier, bundle:{...}} */
export const live = new Map();
export const memStats = () => { let trades = 0; for (const t of live.values()) trades += t.trades?.length || 0; return { live: live.size, trades }; }; // v0.18.18
export const counters = { txs: 0, creates: 0, trades: 0, tier1: 0, decodeErr: 0 };
const launchRing = []; // v0.18.118 market heat: pump.fun launches in the last hour / 10 min (in memory)
export function launchHeat(now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)) { let i = 0; while (i < launchRing.length && now - launchRing[i] > 3600) i++; if (i) launchRing.splice(0, i); let m10 = 0; for (let j = launchRing.length - 1; j >= 0 && now - launchRing[j] <= 600; j--) m10++; return { launch1h: launchRing.length, launch10m: m10 }; }
const listeners = new Map(); // event -> [fn(token, extra)]   events: create|finalized|trade|graduated|expire
export const on = (ev, fn) => { if (!listeners.has(ev)) listeners.set(ev, []); listeners.get(ev).push(fn); };
export const emit = (ev, t, extra) => { for (const fn of listeners.get(ev) || []) { try { fn(t, extra); } catch (e) { console.error(`[listener:${ev}]`, e.stack || e.message); } } };

const mcUsd = (vsol, vtok) => priceSol(vsol, vtok) * CFG.TOTAL_SUPPLY * solUsdCached();

export function handleTx({ sig, slot, logs, err }) {
  counters.txs++;
  if (err) return;
  const evs = eventsFromLogs(logs);
  if (!evs.length) return;
  const ts = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  for (const e of evs) {
    if (e.type === "create") {
      counters.creates++;
      const t = { mint: e.mint, name: e.name, symbol: e.symbol, uri: e.uri, creator: e.creator, bc: e.bondingCurve,
        slot, ts: e.ts || ts, sig, trades: [], finalized: false, tier: 0, bundle: null, lastVsol: e.vsol, lastVtok: e.vtok,
        peakMc: 0, lastMc: 0, hold: false, factory: false, graduated: false };
      live.set(e.mint, t); launchRing.push(t.ts);
      q.insToken.run(t.mint, t.name, t.symbol, t.uri, t.creator, t.bc, slot, t.ts, sig);
      if (q.creatorRecent.get(t.creator, t.ts - CFG.FACTORY_WINDOW_MIN * 60, t.mint) >= 1) { t.factory = true; q.setFactory.run(t.mint); }
      setTimeout(() => finalize(t), CFG.FINALIZE_MS);
      socialsAtLaunch(t.mint, t.uri, (soc) => { t.socOk = soc.fetched ? (soc.ok ? 1 : 0) : null; try { q.setTokenSocials.run(t.socOk, JSON.stringify({ t: soc.twitter, w: soc.website, k: soc.tiktok, g: soc.telegram }), t.mint); } catch {} }); // v0.18.156: every launch
      emit("create", t);
    } else if (e.type === "trade") {
      if (e.type === "claim") { emit("claim", null, { ...e, sig, venue: "pump" }); continue; }
      counters.trades++;
      const t = live.get(e.mint); if (!t) continue; // not a token we saw created (older launches) — ignore
      const tr = { sig, slot, ts: e.ts || ts, wallet: e.user, isBuy: e.isBuy, sol: lamports(e.sol), tokens: rawTok(e.tokens), price: priceSol(e.vsol, e.vtok), route: routeOf(logs), creatorFee: lamports(e.creatorFee || 0n), vsol: e.vsol != null ? String(e.vsol) : null, vtok: e.vtok != null ? String(e.vtok) : null };
      t.lastVsol = e.vsol; t.lastVtok = e.vtok;
      const mc = mcUsd(e.vsol, e.vtok); t.lastMc = mc; if (mc > t.peakMc) t.peakMc = mc;
      // v0.19 (5 Nov 2026, owner: "preserve future data first"): launches that are not bundle tier-1 but reach VOL_TRACK_SOL of buys also get every trade stored for TRACK_MIN —
      // the funded-dev books' coins are mostly bundle 0–50% launches with 15–30 SOL of buying in the first minute, and their dumps were lost when the launch expired at 5 min.
      if (t.finalized && t.tier < 1 && !t.vol) { t.buySol = (t.buySol || 0) + (tr.isBuy ? tr.sol : 0); if (t.buySol >= CFG.VOL_TRACK_SOL) { t.vol = true; t.volUntil = (tr.ts || ts) + CFG.VOL_TRACK_MIN * 60; counters.vol = (counters.vol || 0) + 1;
        try { db.transaction(() => { for (const x of t.trades) q.insTrade.run(t.mint, x.sig, x.slot, x.ts, x.wallet, x.isBuy ? 1 : 0, x.sol, x.tokens, x.price, x.recvMs || null, x.vsol || null, x.vtok || null); })(); } catch (e) { console.error("[vol-track]", e.message); } } }
      tr.recvMs = Date.now(); // v0.19.1
      t.trades.push(tr); if (t.finalized && t.trades.length > 4000) t.trades = t.trades.slice(0, 500).concat(t.trades.slice(-1500)); // v0.19.1: the table keeps everything; in memory keep the launch block (bundle/sniper tests) and the latest trades (last price) so a 2-hour watch stays small
      if (t.finalized && (t.tier >= 1 || t.vol)) q.insTrade.run(t.mint, sig, slot, tr.ts, tr.wallet, tr.isBuy ? 1 : 0, tr.sol, tr.tokens, tr.price, tr.recvMs, tr.vsol, tr.vtok);
      if (!t.graduated && e.vtok <= BigInt(CFG.GRAD_VTOK) * 1_000_000n) { t.graduated = true; q.setGrad.run(t.mint); emit("graduated", t, tr); }
      emit("trade", t, tr);
    }
  }
}

function finalize(t) {
  if (t.finalized) return;
  t.finalized = true;
  const maxSlot = t.slot + CFG.BUNDLE_SLOTS;
  const buys = t.trades.filter((x) => x.isBuy && x.slot <= maxSlot);
  const wallets = new Map();
  let devBuy = 0;
  for (const b of buys) {
    const w = wallets.get(b.wallet) || { tokens: 0, sol: 0, first: b.slot };
    w.tokens += b.tokens; w.sol += b.sol; wallets.set(b.wallet, w);
    if (b.wallet === t.creator) devBuy += b.sol;
  }
  const bundleTokens = [...wallets.values()].reduce((a, w) => a + w.tokens, 0);
  const bundleSol = [...wallets.values()].reduce((a, w) => a + w.sol, 0);
  const pct = (bundleTokens / CFG.TOTAL_SUPPLY) * 100;
  t.bundle = { sol: bundleSol, tokens: bundleTokens, pct, wallets: wallets.size, devBuy };
  t.tier = pct >= CFG.BUNDLE_PCT_MIN && bundleSol >= CFG.BUNDLE_SOL_MIN ? 1 : 0;
  const mc = mcUsd(t.lastVsol, t.lastVtok);
  q.finToken.run(bundleSol, bundleTokens, pct, wallets.size, devBuy, t.tier, mc, t.peakMc || mc, mc, t.mint);
  if (t.tier >= 1) {
    counters.tier1++;
    const ins = db.transaction(() => {
      for (const [w, v] of wallets) q.insCluster.run(t.mint, w, w === t.creator ? "dev" : "bundle", v.tokens, v.sol, v.first);
      for (const tr of t.trades) q.insTrade.run(t.mint, tr.sig, tr.slot, tr.ts, tr.wallet, tr.isBuy ? 1 : 0, tr.sol, tr.tokens, tr.price, tr.recvMs || null, tr.vsol || null, tr.vtok || null);
    });
    ins();
    if (CFG.TG_TIER1_ALERTS && bundleSol >= CFG.TG_TIER1_SOL_MIN) getSocials(t.mint, t.uri).then((soc) => {
      q.setTokenSocials.run(soc.fetched ? (soc.ok ? 1 : 0) : null, JSON.stringify({ t: soc.twitter, w: soc.website, k: soc.tiktok, g: soc.telegram }), t.mint);
      if (CFG.ALERT_REQUIRE_SOCIALS && soc.fetched && !soc.ok) return; // no socials = rug candidate, logged only
      tg(`🧱 <b>Prepared launch</b> — ${esc(t.name)} ($${esc(t.symbol)})\n` +
        `Bundle: <b>${pct.toFixed(1)}%</b> of supply · <b>${bundleSol.toFixed(1)} SOL</b> · ${wallets.size} wallets (dev ${devBuy.toFixed(1)} SOL)\n` +
        `${socialsLine(soc)}\nMC now: $${fmt(mc)} · SOL $${solUsdCached().toFixed(0)}\n` +
        links(t.mint) + (t.factory ? "\n⚠️ factory: same creator launched another token in the last 10 min" : ""));
    }).catch(() => {});
  }
  setTimeout(() => expire(t), (t.tier >= 1 ? CFG.TRACK_MIN : CFG.TRACK_MIN_T0) * 60_000);
  emit("finalized", t);
}

export const holds = new Set(); // v0.18.119: mints other modules (Project S) want kept in memory past the usual 5 / 30 min
function expire(t) {
  if (t.hold || holds.has(t.mint)) return setTimeout(() => expire(t), 60_000); // open paper position keeps it alive
  if (t.volUntil && Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) < t.volUntil) return setTimeout(() => expire(t), 60_000); // v0.19: a volume launch stays until its trades have been kept TRACK_MIN
  emit("expire", t); live.delete(t.mint);
}

export const links = (m) => `<a href="https://fomo.family/tokens/solana/${m}">FOMO</a> · <a href="https://axiom.trade/t/${m}">Axiom</a> · <a href="https://dexscreener.com/solana/${m}">DexScreener</a> · <a href="https://solscan.io/token/${m}">Solscan</a>\n<code>${m}</code>`;
export const esc = (s) => String(s ?? "").replace(/[<>&]/g, (c) => ({ "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", "&": "&amp;" }[c]));
export const fmt = (n) => (n >= 1e6 ? (n / 1e6).toFixed(2) + "M" : n >= 1e3 ? (n / 1e3).toFixed(1) + "K" : n.toFixed(0));
export function startPriceLoop() { getSolUsd(); setInterval(getSolUsd, 60_000); }

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tokens (
